  • 尽管伊朗海员中 DENV 血清流行率 仍然较低,但最近和过去感染的证据表明,职业海上旅行可能会导致 DENV 暴露。 [10.1186/s40794-026-00323-1]。

  • In conclusion, tomatidine derivatives, especially 5d, 5f, and 5c, showed significant potential as anti-DENV therapeutics, necessitating additional mechanistic and in vivo studies to determine their clinical efficacy.
  • Across a range of FoI scenarios representing low-to-high endemic transmission, we evaluated sample size requirements, follow-up duration, and expected power under alternative vaccine efficacy assumptions.
  • The findings reveal several barriers to coordination across government levels and policy sectors, including cultural factors such as context-blind universalism and organizational design features like fragmented data systems, lack of incentives for public health careers, and rigid performance-based budgeting.
  • Although DENV seroprevalence remained low among Iranian seafarers, evidence of both recent and past infections indicates that occupational maritime travel may contribute to DENV exposure. [10.1186/s40794-026-00323-1].

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期登革病毒新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 世卫组织通过网络研讨会推出了登革热、基孔肯雅热、寨卡病毒和黄热病等虫媒病毒疾病的临床管理指南。这是与突发卫生事件和疾病 暴发 相关的一系列更广泛活动的一部分。 [news-aaa944e3c48991ee]。
  • 登革热病例仍然很低,美属萨摩亚、马绍尔群岛和萨摩亚报告了新病例数。媒介 控制和 监测 工作正在进行中。麻疹和流感警报在多个地点均已启动。 [news-5a482873234602b3]。
  • 制造商发布的一项关键试验的长期分析报告显示,疫苗接种 后 4.5 年,对确诊登革热的疗效为 61.2%,对住院治疗的疗效为 84.1%。 [news-dc0ac064355c46a2]。
  • 中央药物标准控制组织 (CDSCO) 于 2026 年 7 月批准 Qdenga,一种登革热 疫苗,用于 4 至 60 岁的个体。疫苗 旨在针对 登革病毒 的所有四种血清型(DENV-1 至 DENV-4)。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Dengue virus news developments.
  • WHO launched guidelines for the clinical management of arboviral diseases, including dengue, chikungunya, Zika, and yellow fever, through a webinar.
  • Dengue cases remain low, with new case numbers reported in American Samoa, Marshall Islands, and Samoa. Vector control and surveillance efforts are ongoing. Measles and influenza alerts are active in various locations.
  • A longer-term analysis of the pivotal trial, published by the manufacturer, reported efficacy of 61.2% against confirmed dengue and 84.1% against hospitalisation 4.5 years after vaccination. [news-dc0ac064355c46a2].
  • The Central Drugs Standard Control Organisation (CDSCO) approved Qdenga, a dengue vaccine, for individuals aged four to 60 in July 2026.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Microbiology spectrum  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-03  |  DOI: 10.1128/spectrum.02363-26  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, e0236326

番茄碱衍生物通过进入和进入后抑制来抑制 登革病毒 感染

Tomatidine derivatives suppress Dengue virus infection through entry and post-entry inhibition
作者： Krishnendu Das, Kalichamy Alagarasu, Diya Roy, Dwidhesh Kharikar, Poonam Patil, Sarah Cherian, Banoth Venkateswara Rao, Kiran Kumar Tatapudi, Bandi Siva, Kanchankumar Patil
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
重要性 登革热是全球主要的健康威胁,每年感染数亿人,但目前还没有针对这种疾病的 抗病毒药物 药物。
设计与对象
在当前的研究中,结合体外和计算机方法评估了番茄碱和 11 种此类衍生物的抗 DENV 潜力。
核心方法
结果表明,衍生物5d、5f和5c具有最有效的抗病毒药物作用;5d 的病毒 RNA 减少了 2.81 个对数,而 5f 和 5c 在 治疗 之前和之后的条件下均抑制病毒活性(5f 的选择性指数为 43.36),包括进入阶段,如进入测定所示。
主要结果
总之,番茄碱衍生物,特别是 5d、5f 和 5c,显示出作为抗 DENV 治疗药物的巨大潜力,需要额外的机制和体内研究来确定其临床疗效。
解释与创新
计算分析进一步表明,这些分子靶向关键病毒蛋白,同时也影响支持病毒感染的 宿主 途径,从而深入了解它们如何发挥 抗病毒药物 效应。
科研与公卫意义
因此,确定安全有效的治疗方法是全球公共卫生的首要任务。
局限与证据强度
这些化合物在病毒生命周期的多个阶段发挥作用,表明它们既可以限制感染的建立,又可以在感染开始后抑制病毒的传播。

Tomatidine derivatives suppress Dengue virus infection through entry and post-entry inhibition

Authors: Krishnendu Das, Kalichamy Alagarasu, Diya Roy, Dwidhesh Kharikar, Poonam Patil, Sarah Cherian, Banoth Venkateswara Rao, Kiran Kumar Tatapudi, Bandi Siva, Kanchankumar Patil
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Dengue is a rapidly spreading disease transmitted by mosquitoes and is caused by the Dengue virus (DENV), affecting approximately 390 million individuals globally. Approved vaccines for use have limited efficacy, and there are no approved antivirals. Tomatidine, a steroidal alkaloid obtained from Solanum species, has demonstrated broad-spectrum antiviral properties, leading to the investigation of its derivatives for their anti-DENV activity. In the current study, the anti-DENV potential of tomatidine and 11 such derivatives was assessed using a combination of in vitro and in silico approaches. Vero cells were used to evaluate the cytotoxicity and antiviral effects of the derivatives in pre-, co-, and post-treatment settings. The results indicated that the derivatives 5d, 5f, and 5c possessed the most potent antiviral effects; 5d achieved a 2.81 log decrease in viral RNA, while both 5f and 5c suppressed viral activity under both pre- and post-treatment conditions (5f has a selectivity index of 43.36), including the entry phase, as indicated by the entry assay. Furthermore, effective inhibition was noted between 0 and 9 h post-infection (hpi) and 15–21 hpi via a time-of-addition assay, indicating dual-phase activity. Additionally, docking and molecular dynamics studies revealed stable interactions of compounds 5c and 5d with the non-structural proteins NS1 and NS5–RdRp of DENV, reinforcing the experimental observations. In conclusion, tomatidine derivatives, especially 5d, 5f, and 5c, showed significant potential as anti-DENV therapeutics, necessitating additional mechanistic and in vivo studies to determine their clinical efficacy. IMPORTANCE Dengue is a major global health threat, infecting hundreds of millions of people each year, yet there are no antiviral drugs available for this disease. Identifying safe and effective therapeutics is therefore a global public health priority. In this study, we discovered three promising tomatidine-derived compounds (5d, 5f, and 5c) that markedly reduced Dengue virus replication in laboratory experiments. These compounds acted at multiple stages of the viral life cycle, suggesting that they could both limit the establishment of infection and suppress viral spread after the infection has begun. Computational analyses further indicated that these molecules target key viral proteins while also influencing host pathways that support viral infection, providing insights into how they may exert their antiviral effects. Together, our findings identify tomatidine derivatives as promising lead candidates for dengue antiviral drug development and provide a strong foundation for future preclinical and clinical investigations.

Main results

COVID-19 大流行干预之前、期间和之后中国宁波市登革热的流行病学特征和 传播 动态。

Epidemiological characteristics and transmission dynamics of dengue in Ningbo City, China before, during, and after the COVID-19 pandemic interventions.
作者： Yan-Ru Chu, Yun-Kang Zhao, Buasiyamu Abudunaibi, Yao Wang, Yan-Wu Zhang, Song Lei, Yang Mao, Tianfeng He, Tian-Mu Chen, Yi Chen
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
关于登革热 流行病学 以及这些时期的传播性的证据有限。
设计与对象
病例中位年龄为 38.50 岁(IQR = 31.25-54.00),57.52% 失业、退休或从事商业服务。
核心方法
我们分析了2017年至2024年的常规登革热病例和蚊子监测数据,使用描述性分析来评估流行病学特征,并建立了数学模型来估计基本繁殖数(R0)。
主要结果
干预后观察到 发病率 反弹,但 ≤ 14 岁组、北仑区、宁海县和象山县没有观察到统计学上的显着差异。
解释与创新
暴发 年份的 R0 中位数(3.46,IQR = 2.98-3.95)高于没有本地 传播 的年份(0.00,IQR = 0.00-0.00;p < 0.001)。
科研与公卫意义
持续的 监测、快速的 诊断 和针对地区的 媒介 控制对于防止重新建立本地 传播 至关重要。
局限与证据强度
尽管诊断延迟和 媒介 密度有所减少,但 PHSM 后的 发病率 有所反弹,而且地区级异质性表明 暴发 的潜力仍在持续。

Epidemiological characteristics and transmission dynamics of dengue in Ningbo City, China before, during, and after the COVID-19 pandemic interventions.

Authors: Yan-Ru Chu, Yun-Kang Zhao, Buasiyamu Abudunaibi, Yao Wang, Yan-Wu Zhang, Song Lei, Yang Mao, Tianfeng He, Tian-Mu Chen, Yi Chen
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Ningbo experienced a local dengue outbreak in 2018-2019, followed by the implementation and relaxation of COVID-19 pandemic interventions. Evidence has been limited on dengue epidemiology and the transmissibility across these periods. METHODS: We analyzed routine dengue cases and mosquito surveillance data from 2017 to 2024, using descriptive analyses to assess the epidemiological characteristics, and a mathematical model was developed to estimate the basic reproduction number (R0). RESULTS: A total of 266 dengue cases were reported, with no deaths. Before the pandemic interventions, 51.19% of cases were overseas-imported; no cases were reported during pandemic interventions, whereas 73.47% were domestically imported after pandemic interventions. The median age of cases was 38.50 years (IQR = 31.25-54.00), and 57.52% were unemployed, retired, or in commercial services. The onset-to-diagnosis interval shortened post-interventions (4.46 days vs. Viral typing increased post-interventions (34.59% vs. 63.27%), with 83.70% of cases identified as dengue virus-1 infections. Dengue cases were reported across all districts, with higher proportions in Haishu (27.07%) and Yinzhou (24.44%). The incidence was 2.82 per 100,000 persons. Incidence did not differ significantly by sex, but was significantly higher among individuals 15-59 years and those aged ≥60years than among those aged ≤14 years (3.18 and 3.01 per 100,000 persons, respectively, vs. 0.45 per 100,000 persons). Incidence was highest in Haishu and Jiangbei (6.90 and 4.93 per 100,000 persons, respectively). A post-intervention incidence rebound was observed, although no statistically significant differences were observed in the ≤ 14 age group, Beilun District, Ninghai County and Xiangshan County. Aedes albopictus was the only vector species. The mean Breteau Index was 16.07 (SD: 8.04) and decreased across phases, and rebounded in Jiangbei, Zhenhai, and Haishu districts. The mean BI was higher in summer compared to other seasons (p 0.95). The median R0 was higher in the outbreak years (3.46, IQR = 2.98-3.95) than years without local transmission (0.00, IQR = 0.00-0.00; p < 0.001). Median R0 was 0.00 (IQR = 0.00-0.71) over 2017-2024 due to the absence of local cases in most years. CONCLUSIONS: Dengue remains non-endemic in Ningbo, and is driven primarily by imported infections rather than sustained local transmission. Despite a reduction in diagnostic delay and vector density, post-PHSM incidences rebounded, and district-level heterogeneity indicates ongoing outbreak potential. Sustained surveillance, rapid diagnosis and district-tailored vector control are critical to prevent re-establishment of local transmission.

桉树精油生物活性成分对DENV-2包膜蛋白的代谢筛选与计算机模拟研究

Metabolic Screening and In Silico Investigation of Eucalyptus globulus Essential Oil Bioactives Against DENV-2 Envelope Protein.
作者： Arslan Afzal, Ilyas Ahmad, Mahadullah, Saliha Qamar, Tayyaba Yousaf, Faiz Ullah, Dilawar Khan, Mohammad Hamid Hamdard, Zia Ur Rehman Mashwani
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The escalating prevalence of viral infectious diseases poses a significant threat to global public health. Among these, dengue virus (DENV) represents one of the most widespread arboviral infections, impacting nearly 400 million individuals annually, with severe clinical manifestations occurring in approximately 1% of cases, as reported by the World Health Organization (WHO).
设计与对象
该研究的重点是研究蓝桉精油对 DENV-2 包膜蛋白的生物活性。总共发现 14 种主要生物活性化合物与登革热高度相关,从而得出登革热和生物活性化合物之间的 70 个共同目标。
核心方法
学术界和工业界的广泛研究集中在 DENV 的各个方面,包括其分子病毒学、流行病学模式、传播 途径和治疗靶点。KEGG 和 Metascape 分析的蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用和网络通路分析显示 DENV-2 靶标具有更高程度的相关性。
主要结果
分子对接分析表明,Globulol和Ecalyptol与DENV-2的1oke和1tg8 f两种受体具有较高的结合亲和力,结合能分别为-8.3和-7.6。毒性和药物相似性分析证实了代谢物用于药物开发的安全性。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,桉树精油可能在登革热的治疗中具有潜力。四价CYD-TDV(Dengvaxia)疫苗的开发标志着登革热预防的重要进展。
科研与公卫意义
该研究提供了对潜在的登革热治疗 抗病毒药物 药物的见解,强调了探索天然化合物作为治疗选择的重要性。非随机研究强调了与当前 疫苗接种 策略相关的一些局限性,促使人们越来越重视 抗病毒药物 药物的发现和开发,作为有效登革热管理的替代或补充治疗选择。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告验证蓝桉精油治疗功效的体内研究或临床试验。该证据基于计算机模拟和代谢筛查分析,可能无法完全代表现实世界的结果。

Metabolic Screening and In Silico Investigation of Eucalyptus globulus Essential Oil Bioactives Against DENV-2 Envelope Protein.

Authors: Arslan Afzal, Ilyas Ahmad, Mahadullah, Saliha Qamar, Tayyaba Yousaf, Faiz Ullah, Dilawar Khan, Mohammad Hamid Hamdard, Zia Ur Rehman Mashwani
Original abstract (English)
The escalating prevalence of viral infectious diseases poses a significant threat to global public health. Among these, dengue virus (DENV) represents one of the most widespread arboviral infections, impacting nearly 400 million individuals annually, with severe clinical manifestations occurring in approximately 1% of cases, as reported by the World Health Organization (WHO). Extensive research across both academic and industrial sectors has focused on various aspects of DENV, including its molecular virology, epidemiological patterns, transmission pathways, and therapeutic targets. The development of the tetravalent CYD-TDV (Dengvaxia) vaccine marked a critical advancement in dengue prevention. Nonetheless, emerging evidence has highlighted several limitations associated with current vaccination strategies, prompting a growing emphasis on the discovery and development of antiviral agents as alternative or complementary therapeutic options for effective dengue management. A total of 14 major bioactive compounds were found to be highly correlated with Dengue, yielding 70 common targets between dengue and bioactive compounds. Protein-protein interaction and network pathway analysis by KEGG and Metascape analysis show higher degree of correlation for DENV-2 targets. Molecular docking analysis shows that Globulol and Ecalyptol have higher binding affinity with two receptors, 1oke and 1tg8 f DENV-2, with binding energies of -8.3 and -7.6. Toxicity and drug-likeness analyses confirmed the safety of the metabolites for drug development. These findings suggest that Eucalyptus globulus essential oil may have potential in the treatment of dengue fever. However, further in vivo studies using animal models are required to validate its therapeutic efficacy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The escalating prevalence of viral infectious diseases poses a significant threat to global public health. Among these, dengue virus (DENV) represents one of the most widespread arboviral infections, impacting nearly 400 million individuals annually, with severe clinical manifestations occurring in approximately 1% of cases, as reported by the World Health Organization (WHO).
Design and population
The study focused on the investigation of Eucalyptus globulus essential oil bioactives against DENV-2 Envelope Protein. A total of 14 major bioactive compounds were found to be highly correlated with Dengue, yielding 70 common targets between dengue and bioactive compounds.
Core methods
Extensive research across both academic and industrial sectors has focused on various aspects of DENV, including its molecular virology, epidemiological patterns, transmission pathways, and therapeutic targets. Protein-protein interaction and network pathway analysis by KEGG and Metascape analysis show higher degree of correlation for DENV-2 targets.
Main results
Molecular docking analysis shows that Globulol and Ecalyptol have higher binding affinity with two receptors, 1oke and 1tg8 f DENV-2, with binding energies of -8.3 and -7.6. Toxicity and drug-likeness analyses confirmed the safety of the metabolites for drug development.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings suggest that Eucalyptus globulus essential oil may have potential in the treatment of dengue fever. The development of the tetravalent CYD-TDV (Dengvaxia) vaccine marked a critical advancement in dengue prevention.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides insights into potential antiviral agents for dengue management, highlighting the importance of exploring natural compounds as therapeutic options. Non-randomized studies have highlighted several limitations associated with current vaccination strategies, prompting a growing emphasis on the discovery and development of antiviral agents as alternative or complementary therapeutic options for effective dengue management.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report in vivo studies or clinical trials to validate the therapeutic efficacy of Eucalyptus globulus essential oil. The evidence is based on in silico and metabolic screening analyses, which may not fully represent real-world outcomes.

用于优化登革热 疫苗 试验设计的血清型结构感染力

Serotype-structured force of infection for optimizing dengue vaccine trial design
作者： Lameck Ondieki Agasa, Faith Mugure Thuita, Thomas Achia, Wycliffe Kipkoech Cheruiyot, Antony Karanja, Dennis Abuga Makambi, Agasa, L. O., Thuita, F. M., Cheruiyot, W. K., Makambi, D. A.
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Dengue fever, transmitted primarily by Aedes aegypti, remains a major global public health threat, causing an estimated 390 million infections annually.
设计与对象
在流行地区设计有效的登革热 疫苗 疗效试验具有挑战性,因为 传播 强度随空间和时间的不同而变化,基线群体免疫具有异质性,并且终点累积很大程度上取决于当地感染力 (FoI)。
核心方法
我们开发了一个用于流行环境中试验计划的模拟框架,通过催化模型将 FoI 与特定年龄的 血清流行率 联系起来,然后将 FoI 转化为症状终点 发病率 以进行随机对照试验模拟。
主要结果
在代表低流行 疫苗 的一系列 FoI 场景中,我们评估了样本量要求、随访持续时间以及替代 疫苗 功效假设下的预期功效。
解释与创新
该框架表明,在合理的症状检测假设下,每组招募 5,000-10,000 名参与者的试验在随访 12-24 个月内可以达到至少 80% 的效果。
科研与公卫意义
敏感性分析表明,可行性阈值对 FoI 和症状分数最敏感,突出了地点选择和 监测 强度的重要性。
局限与证据强度
总体而言,所提出的基于 FoI 的方法为登革热试验设计提供了实用指导,包括样本量确定、后续计划以及流行病学条件的确定,在这些条件下,疫苗 评估在流行环境中最有效。

Serotype-structured force of infection for optimizing dengue vaccine trial design

Authors: Lameck Ondieki Agasa, Faith Mugure Thuita, Thomas Achia, Wycliffe Kipkoech Cheruiyot, Antony Karanja, Dennis Abuga Makambi, Agasa, L. O., Thuita, F. M., Cheruiyot, W. K., Makambi, D. A.
Original abstract (English)
Dengue fever, transmitted primarily by Aedes aegypti, remains a major global public health threat, causing an estimated 390 million infections annually. Designing efficient dengue vaccine efficacy trials in endemic regions is challenging because transmission intensity varies across space and time, baseline population immunity is heterogeneous, and endpoint accrual depends strongly on the local force of infection (FoI). We developed a simulation framework for trial planning in endemic settings that links FoI to age-specific seroprevalence through a catalytic model and then translates FoI into symptomatic endpoint incidence for randomized controlled trial simulations. Across a range of FoI scenarios representing low-to-high endemic transmission, we evaluated sample size requirements, follow-up duration, and expected power under alternative vaccine efficacy assumptions. In high-transmission settings, the framework indicates that trials enrolling 5,000-10,000 participants per arm can achieve at least 80% power within 12-24 months of follow-up under plausible symptomatic detection assumptions. Sensitivity analyses demonstrate that feasibility thresholds are most sensitive to FoI and the symptomatic fraction, highlighting the importance of site selection and surveillance intensity. Overall, the proposed FoI-based approach provides practical guidance for dengue trial design, including sample size determination, follow-up planning, and the identification of epidemiological conditions under which vaccine evaluation is most efficient in endemic settings.

组合探针编码多重 RT-qPCR 检测可同时检测和区分人类和蚊子样本中的登革热、寨卡病毒和基孔肯雅病毒

A combinatorial probe-coding multiplex RT-qPCR assay for simultaneous detection and differentiation of dengue, Zika, and chikungunya viruses in human and mosquito samples
作者： Marcel Kruchelski Tschá, Thiago Jacomasso, Fernanda Gatto de Almeida, Aline Burda Farias, Victoria Stadler Tasca Ribeiro, Alessandro Afornali, Lilian Motta Cantanhêde, de Mello ALeS, Felicidade Mota Pereira, Costa APdS
摘要中文翻译摘要 寨卡病毒(ZIKV)、登革病毒(DENV)和基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)等虫媒病毒仍然对热带和亚热带地区的公共卫生构成重大威胁。这些病毒主要由伊蚊传播,并且经常在同一地理区域共同传播,导致 暴发 具有重叠的临床表现和血清学检测中的交叉反应,特别是 ZIKV 和 DENV 之间的交叉反应。这些方面带来了诊断挑战,分子诊断方法的发展至关重要。在此,我们开发了一种基于多色组合探针编码 (MCPC) 概念的诊断试剂盒,通过使用四种光谱不同的染料对目标进行编码,可同时检测六种病毒目标(ZIKV、DENV1-4 和 CHIKV)和内部对照 (IC)。使用一组 660 个特征化的人类血清或血浆样本以及 9 个人工 DENV-4 样本,我们对所有目标分别获得了高于 94% 和 99% 的诊断灵敏度和特异性。该检测可同时分析多达 91 个样品,与市售产品相比,显着提高了测试通量。该测试还应用于伊蚊属、按蚊属、趋血蚊属和库蚊属的蚊子样本,证明其不仅可以作为应急响应的强大工具,而且还可以作为公共卫生环境中虫媒病毒 监测 的强大工具。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革病毒 1-4 和登革热疾病仍然是热带和亚热带地区重大的公共卫生威胁。由于临床表现重叠和血清学检测中的交叉反应带来的挑战,分子诊断方法的发展至关重要。
设计与对象
开发了一种基于多色组合探针编码 (MCPC) 概念的诊断试剂盒,可同时检测六种病毒靶标(ZIKV、DENV1-4 和 CHIKV)和内部对照 (IC)。该研究使用了一组 660 个特征化的人类血清或血浆样本以及 9 个人造 DENV-4 样本。
核心方法
该检测旨在能够同时分析多达 91 个样品,与市售产品相比,大大提高了测试通量。使用一组 660 个特征化的人类血清或血浆样本以及 9 个人工 DENV-4 样本进行评估,以获得所有目标的诊断灵敏度和特异性分别高于 94% 和 99%。
主要结果
所有目标(包括 DENV1-4)的诊断灵敏度和特异性分别高于 94% 和 99%。该检测可同时分析多达 91 个样品。
解释与创新
该测定提供了一种稳健而有效的方法来检测 登革病毒,解决了交叉反应和重叠临床表现引起的诊断挑战。
科研与公卫意义
该检测可作为公共卫生领域应急响应和虫媒病毒监测的可靠工具,增强监测快速、准确的诊断检测能力。
局限与证据强度
该研究没有报告局限性,但证据很充分,因为它使用充分表征的样本组证明了所有 登革病毒 靶点的高敏感性和特异性。

A combinatorial probe-coding multiplex RT-qPCR assay for simultaneous detection and differentiation of dengue, Zika, and chikungunya viruses in human and mosquito samples

Authors: Marcel Kruchelski Tschá, Thiago Jacomasso, Fernanda Gatto de Almeida, Aline Burda Farias, Victoria Stadler Tasca Ribeiro, Alessandro Afornali, Lilian Motta Cantanhêde, de Mello ALeS, Felicidade Mota Pereira, Costa APdS
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Arboviruses such as Zika virus (ZIKV), dengue virus (DENV), and chikungunya virus (CHIKV) remain significant public health threats in tropical and subtropical regions. These viruses are transmitted primarily by Aedes mosquitoes and often co-circulate in the same geographic areas, leading to outbreaks with overlapping clinical presentations and cross-reactivity in serological testing, particularly between ZIKV and DENV. These aspects create diagnostic challenges, and the development of molecular diagnostic methods is critically necessary. Here, we developed a diagnostic kit based on the Multicolor Combinational Probe Coding (MCPC) concept to simultaneously detect six viral targets (ZIKV, DENV1–4, and CHIKV) and an internal control (IC), by encoding targets with four spectrally distinct dyes. Using a panel of 660 characterized human serum or plasma samples, and 9 artificial DENV-4 samples, we obtained diagnostic sensitivity and specificity above 94% and 99%, respectively, for all targets. The assay enables the simultaneous analysis of up to 91 samples, substantially increasing testing throughput compared to commercially available products. The test was also applied to mosquito specimens of Aedes spp., Anopheles spp., Haemagogus spp., and Culex spp., demonstrating its utility as a robust tool not only for emergency response but also for arbovirus surveillance in public health settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue viruses 1–4 and dengue disease remain significant public health threats in tropical and subtropical regions. The development of molecular diagnostic methods is critically necessary due to the challenges posed by overlapping clinical presentations and cross-reactivity in serological testing.
Design and population
A diagnostic kit based on the Multicolor Combinational Probe Coding (MCPC) concept was developed to simultaneously detect six viral targets (ZIKV, DENV1–4, and CHIKV) and an internal control (IC). The study used a panel of 660 characterized human serum or plasma samples and 9 artificial DENV-4 samples.
Core methods
The assay was designed to enable the simultaneous analysis of up to 91 samples, substantially increasing testing throughput compared to commercially available products. It was evaluated using a panel of 660 characterized human serum or plasma samples and 9 artificial DENV-4 samples to obtain diagnostic sensitivity and specificity above 94% and 99%, respectively, for all targets.
Main results
Diagnostic sensitivity and specificity above 94% and 99%, respectively, were achieved for all targets, including DENV1–4. The assay enables the simultaneous analysis of up to 91 samples.
Interpretation and novelty
The assay provides a robust and efficient method for detecting dengue viruses, addressing the diagnostic challenges caused by cross-reactivity and overlapping clinical presentations.
Scientific and public-health significance
The test can be used as a reliable tool for emergency response and arbovirus surveillance in public health settings, enhancing the capacity for rapid and accurate diagnosis of dengue viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study does not report limitations, but the evidence is strong as it demonstrates high sensitivity and specificity for all dengue virus targets using a well-characterized sample panel.

沙特阿拉伯 DENV-3 分离株的分子表征以及针对 登革病毒 蛋白的潜在抑制剂的鉴定。

Molecular Characterization of a Saudi Arabian DENV-3 Isolate and Identification of Potential Inhibitors Targeting Dengue Virus Proteins.
作者： Shymaa Damfo, Eitezaz Abdulshakoor Zaki, Nadia Alsuhaimi, Basmah Abdul Aziz Alharbi, Hind Hassan Alrashdi
摘要中文翻译简介:登革病毒 (DENV) 是一种全球重要的致病病毒。了解 DENV 的遗传特征对于阐明病毒 进化、多样性和 进化 至关重要。此外,使用先进的分子方法研究 DENV 进化 可以促进新基因型的鉴定并扩展我们对 DENV 蛋白质组的了解,从而支持新疗法的开发。我们的研究在分子水平上检查了 DENV-3 分离序列,并寻求识别针对关键 DENV 蛋白的潜在抑制剂。方法:分析从沙特阿拉伯(2024 年)确诊的登革热病例中收集的血清样本,以确定潜在 抗病毒药物 开发的关键目标。我们进行了多重序列比对、系统发育分析、虚拟筛选、分子对接和配体-蛋白质相互作用分析。结果:系统发育研究证明,DENV-3 分离株与 2016 年沙特阿拉伯分离株 (MZ857217.1) 的 DENV 病毒相似。研究结果揭示了目标的稳定二级结构,并确定了至少四种主要化合物作为抗登革热治疗候选物,与对照相比,它们对目标具有更高的预测亲和力。结论:这些发现增强了我们对沙特阿拉伯近期登革热分离株的分子水平了解,并有助于 抗病毒药物 药物的开发。它们可以为未来的治疗策略和 暴发 管理工作提供信息。

问题与背景
该研究旨在表征 DENV-3 分离株的分子特征,并鉴定针对关键 DENV 蛋白的潜在抑制剂。登革病毒 (DENV) 是一种全球重要的致病病毒。
设计与对象
该研究涉及从 2024 年沙特阿拉伯确诊的登革热病例中收集的血清样本。它重点关注 DENV-3 分离株,并试图分析其分子特征,以促进 抗病毒药物 的开发。
核心方法
研究人员进行了多重序列比对、系统发育分析、虚拟筛选、分子对接和配体-蛋白质相互作用分析,以鉴定针对 DENV 蛋白质的潜在抑制剂。
主要结果
发现 DENV-3 分离株与 2016 年沙特阿拉伯的 DENV 分离株 (MZ857217.1) 相似。研究结果确定了至少四种化合物作为抗登革热治疗候选药物,与对照相比,它们对目标的预测亲和力更高。

这些发现可以通过深入了解沙特阿拉伯最近的 DENV-3 分离株的分子特征,为未来的治疗策略和 暴发 管理工作提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对计算方法的依赖和缺乏实验验证而受到限制。由于使用了虚拟筛选和对接技术,证据的强度中等。

Molecular Characterization of a Saudi Arabian DENV-3 Isolate and Identification of Potential Inhibitors Targeting Dengue Virus Proteins.

Authors: Shymaa Damfo, Eitezaz Abdulshakoor Zaki, Nadia Alsuhaimi, Basmah Abdul Aziz Alharbi, Hind Hassan Alrashdi
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: The dengue virus (DENV) is a globally significant pathogenic virus. Understanding the genetic characteristics of the DENV is critical to elucidating viral evolution, diversity, and transmission. Furthermore, investigating DENV evolution using advanced molecular approaches can facilitate the identification of new genotypes and expand our knowledge of the DENV proteome, thereby supporting the development of novel therapeutics. Our study examines a DENV-3 isolate sequence at the molecular level and seeks to identify a potential inhibitor targeting crucial DENV proteins. METHOD: Serum samples collected from confirmed dengue cases in Saudi Arabia (2024) were analysed to characterize crucial targets for potential antiviral development. We performed multiple sequence alignment, phylogenetic analysis, virtual screening, molecular docking, and ligand-protein interaction analysis. RESULTS: DENV-3 isolate is similar to the DENV virus from a Saudi Arabian isolate from 2016 (MZ857217.1), as demonstrated by phylogenetic research. The findings reveal a stable secondary structure of targets and identify at least four leading chemical compounds as anti-dengue therapeutic candidates that have higher predicted affinities toward the targets compared to the control. CONCLUSION: These findings enhance our molecular-level understanding of recent dengue isolates in Saudi Arabia and contribute to the development of antiviral medicines. They may inform future therapeutic strategies and outbreak management efforts.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to characterize the molecular features of a DENV-3 isolate and identify potential inhibitors targeting crucial DENV proteins. The dengue virus (DENV) is a globally significant pathogenic virus.
Design and population
The study involved serum samples collected from confirmed dengue cases in Saudi Arabia in 2024. It focused on a DENV-3 isolate and sought to analyze its molecular characteristics for antiviral development.
Core methods
The researchers conducted multiple sequence alignment, phylogenetic analysis, virtual screening, molecular docking, and ligand-protein interaction analysis to identify potential inhibitors targeting DENV proteins.
Main results
The DENV-3 isolate was found to be similar to a Saudi Arabian DENV isolate from 2016 (MZ857217.1). The findings identified at least four chemical compounds as anti-dengue therapeutic candidates with higher predicted affinities toward the targets compared to the control.
Interpretation and novelty
The study's interpretation highlights the importance of molecular approaches in understanding DENV evolution and expanding knowledge of its proteome. This contributes to the development of novel therapeutics.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform future therapeutic strategies and outbreak management efforts by providing insights into the molecular characteristics of recent DENV-3 isolates in Saudi Arabia.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on computational methods and the absence of experimental validation. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the use of virtual screening and docking techniques.

Transmission Dynamics of Dengue Virus, Ceará State, Brazil, 2010–2025

Authors: Shirlene T S de Lima, Ingra M Claro, Xin-Yi Hua, Lívia Sacchetto, Ronaldo de Jesus, Leda M Simões Mello, Júlia Forato, Erika R Manuli, Cailin McDonald, Maria Eduarda T de Lima
Original abstract (English)
We evaluated the transmission dynamics of dengue virus (DENV) during 2010–2025 in Ceará State, Brazil. Ceará has been a dengue hyperendemic region, where all 4 DENV serotypes co-circulate, predominantly DENV-1 and DENV-2, and where DENV-3 was recently reintroduced. Our findings provide an epidemiologic baseline for postlicensure dengue vaccine evaluation.

循环中的寨卡病毒和登革热 IgA-NS1 标志着产后早期暴露,与先天性寨卡综合征相关的小头畸形没有显着关联。

Circulating Zika and dengue IgA-NS1 marks early postnatal exposure without significant association with congenital Zika syndrome-related microcephaly.
作者： Juanita Castro-Meneses, Sebastián Castro-Trujillo, William R. Mejía, Martha Rocío Vega, Doris Marta Salgado, Carlos Eduardo Fonseca, A. Ortiz, Carlos Fernando Narváez, Ortiz ÁM, Ángela M Ortiz
摘要中文翻译背景:预先存在的母体 IgG-NS1 对 DENV(而非 ZIKV)的升高与先天性寨卡综合征 (CZS) 中小头畸形的风险降低相关,表明具有保护作用。虽然 IgG 和 IgM 的血清学分析在婴儿中存在生理局限性,但 IgA-NS1 代表了 宿主 暴露于正黄病毒的标志物,因为它不会经胎盘转移并反映早期活跃的 免疫应答。这项研究评估了哥伦比亚南部(登革热高流行区)产前暴露于 ZIKV 的母亲和婴儿对 ZIKV 和 DENV 的循环 IgA-NS1 反应。方法:在这项多中心研究中,我们分析了 2015-2016 年哥伦比亚流行期间确认妊娠期寨卡病毒暴露的 164 对母婴。我们使用优化的 ELISA 和充分表征的临床样本,同时评估了针对 ZIKV 和 DENV1-4 的循环 IgA-NS1 反应。通过儿科随访评估神经学结果,以确定 IgA-NS1 与 CZS 相关小头畸形之间的关联。结果:针对 ZIKV 和 DENV1-4 的 IgA-NS1 ELISA 的灵敏度/特异性分别为 94%/97% 和 86%/85%。DENV 暴露样本与 ZIKV NS1 表现出 26% 的交叉反应性。没有胎盘转移的证据,并且患有或不患有 CZS 相关小头畸形的母亲和婴儿之间的 ZIKV 和 DENV IgA-NS1 水平相当。然而,循环中的 ZIKV 和 DENV IgA-NS1 在出生后增加,并且在年龄较大的儿童中更高。结论:虽然循环 IgA-NS1 由于其稳定性和缺乏胎盘转移而成为婴儿正黄病毒暴露的有前途的产后标志物,但它似乎无助于预防严重的 CZS 结局。这些发现表明,NS1 介导的针对小头畸形的保护性免疫可能具有同种型依赖性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估产前暴露于 ZIKV 的母亲和婴儿对 ZIKV 和 DENV 的循环 IgA-NS1 反应,解决了解产后早期暴露标志物及其与先天性寨卡综合征相关小头畸形的关系的需要。
设计与对象
一项多中心研究分析了 2015-2016 年哥伦比亚流行期间确认妊娠期感染 ZIKV 的 164 对母婴,重点关注登革热高流行地区产前暴露的婴儿。
核心方法
使用经过充分表征的临床样本进行优化的 ELISA 来评估针对 ZIKV 和 DENV1-4 的循环 IgA-NS1 反应,并通过儿科随访评估神经学结果,以确定与 CZS 相关小头畸形的关联。
主要结果
ZIKV 和 DENV1-4 的 IgA-NS1 ELISA 的灵敏度/特异性分别为 94%/97% 和 86%/85%。DENV 暴露样本与 ZIKV NS1 表现出 26% 的交叉反应性。没有胎盘转移的证据,并且患有或不患有 CZS 相关小头畸形的母亲和婴儿之间的 ZIKV 和 DENV IgA-NS1 水平相当。
解释与创新
作者解释说,由于其稳定性和缺乏胎盘转移,循环 IgA-NS1 可以作为婴儿正黄病毒暴露的有希望的产后标志物,但它似乎无助于预防严重的 CZS 结局。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现告知使用 IgA-NS1 作为婴儿正黄病毒暴露的产后标志物,这可能有助于 监测 以及 ZIKV 和 DENV 感染的早期检测,尽管它们并没有直接告知针对严重先天性寨卡综合征结果的保护措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据因其关注特定地理区域和单一时间段而受到限制,结果表明 IgA-NS1 可能不是针对严重 CZS 结局的保护性标志物,表明对研究结果的可信度中等。

Circulating Zika and dengue IgA-NS1 marks early postnatal exposure without significant association with congenital Zika syndrome-related microcephaly.

Authors: Juanita Castro-Meneses, Sebastián Castro-Trujillo, William R. Mejía, Martha Rocío Vega, Doris Marta Salgado, Carlos Eduardo Fonseca, A. Ortiz, Carlos Fernando Narváez, Ortiz ÁM, Ángela M Ortiz
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Elevated preexisting maternal IgG-NS1 to DENV, rather than to ZIKV, has been associated with a reduced risk of microcephaly in congenital Zika syndrome (CZS), suggesting a protective role. While serological analysis of IgG and IgM has physiological limitations in infants, IgA-NS1 represents a marker of host exposure to orthoflavivirus, as it is not transferred transplacentally and reflects early active immune responses. This study evaluated circulating IgA-NS1 responses to ZIKV and DENV in mothers and infants prenatally exposed to ZIKV in southern Colombia, a dengue hyperendemic region. METHODS: In this multicentric study, we analyzed 164 mother-infant pairs with confirmed gestational ZIKV exposure during the Colombia 2015-2016 epidemic. Using optimized ELISAs with well-characterized clinical samples, we simultaneously assessed circulating IgA-NS1 responses against ZIKV and DENV1-4. Neurological outcomes were evaluated through pediatric follow-up to determine associations between IgA-NS1 and CZS-related microcephaly. RESULTS: IgA-NS1 ELISAs for ZIKV and DENV1-4 achieved sensitivity/specificity of 94%/97% and 86%/85%, respectively. DENV-exposed samples showed 26% cross-reactivity against ZIKV NS1. There was no evidence of placental transfer, and IgA-NS1 levels for ZIKV and DENV were comparable between mothers and infants with or without CZS-related microcephaly. However, circulating ZIKV and DENV IgA-NS1 increased postnatally and were higher in older children. CONCLUSION: While circulating IgA-NS1 serves as a promising postnatal marker of orthoflavivirus exposure in infants due to its stability and lack of placental transfer, it does not appear to contribute to protection against severe CZS outcomes. These findings demonstrate that protective NS1-mediated immunity against microcephaly may be isotype-dependent.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate circulating IgA-NS1 responses to ZIKV and DENV in mothers and infants prenatally exposed to ZIKV, addressing the need to understand early postnatal exposure markers and their association with congenital Zika syndrome-related microcephaly.
Design and population
A multicentric study analyzed 164 mother-infant pairs with confirmed gestational ZIKV exposure during the Colombia 2015-2016 epidemic, focusing on prenatally exposed infants in a dengue hyperendemic region.
Core methods
Optimized ELISAs with well-characterized clinical samples were used to assess circulating IgA-NS1 responses against ZIKV and DENV1-4, and neurological outcomes were evaluated through pediatric follow-up to determine associations with CZS-related microcephaly.
Main results
IgA-NS1 ELISAs for ZIKV and DENV1-4 achieved sensitivity/specificity of 94%/97% and 86%/85%, respectively. DENV-exposed samples showed 26% cross-reactivity against ZIKV NS1. There was no evidence of placental transfer, and IgA-NS1 levels for ZIKV and DENV were comparable between mothers and infants with or without CZS-related microcephaly.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that circulating IgA-NS1 serves as a promising postnatal marker of orthoflavivirus exposure in infants due to its stability and lack of placental transfer, but it does not appear to contribute to protection against severe CZS outcomes.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings inform the use of IgA-NS1 as a postnatal marker for orthoflavivirus exposure in infants, which could aid in surveillance and early detection of ZIKV and DENV infections, though they do not directly inform protective measures against severe congenital Zika syndrome outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is limited by its focus on a specific geographic region and a single time period, and the results suggest that IgA-NS1 may not be a protective marker against severe CZS outcomes, indicating moderate confidence in the findings.

茄科来源的螺甾烷皂苷抑制登革病毒复制和病毒蛋白表达,并对多种虫媒病毒表现活性。

Solanaceae-Derived Spirostane Saponins Inhibit Dengue Virus Replication and Viral Protein Expression and Exhibit Activity Against Multiple Arboviruses.
作者： Erika Vanessa Jimenez-Posada, Oscar Marino Mosquera-Martínez, Sara M Robledo, Diego Durango, Wiston Quiñones, Samuel Vizcaíno-Páez, Marlen Martinez-Gutierrez
摘要中文翻译蚊媒虫媒病毒(包括登革病毒(DENV)、寨卡病毒(ZIKV)和基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV))构成了日益增长的全球健康负担,但目前针对这些感染的特异性抗病毒药物疗法尚不普及。这种未满足的医疗需求凸显了从生物相关来源(如植物来源的天然产物)中识别新型抗病毒药物化合物的重要性。本研究采用基于生物活性的筛选方法,对八种茄科植物的甲醇提取物进行分析,以鉴定具有抗病毒药物活性的化合物,针对Vero细胞中DENV-2的感染。五种提取物显著降低了病毒的感染性,其中Solanum ovalifolium和Cestrum sp.在非细胞毒性浓度下实现了完全抑制。随后的分级和纯化过程分离出四种螺甾烷型甾体皂苷,其结构通过超高效液相色谱-二极管阵列检测-质谱(UPLC-DAD-MS)和一维/二维核磁共振(1D/2D NMR)光谱进行表征。功能实验表明,这些化合物抑制DENV复制,表现为病毒RNA水平、NS1和NS3蛋白表达以及传染性病毒滴度的显著下降。阶段特异性分析显示,其抗病毒药物活性主要与病毒生命周期的细胞内阶段相关,这与抑制病毒复制和/或蛋白质加工一致。分子对接分析支持这些发现,预测这些化合物与关键病毒酶(特别是NS5 RNA依赖性RNA聚合酶和NS3解旋酶/蛋白酶)具有有利的相互作用。重要的是,分离出的化合物对ZIKV和CHIKV也表现出抗病毒药物活性,表明其具有跨虫媒病毒的疗效。综上所述,这些发现表明螺甾烷皂苷是具有前景的抗病毒药物骨架,并支持其进一步开发为广谱抗病毒药物药物。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
蚊媒虫媒病毒,包括 登革病毒 (DENV)、寨卡病毒 (ZIKV) 和基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV),代表着日益严重的全球健康负担,但目前还没有针对大多数此类感染的具体 抗病毒药物 疗法。
设计与对象
在这项研究中,将生物活性引导的方法应用于八种茄科物种的甲醇提取物,以鉴定对 Vero 细胞中的 DENV-2 具有 抗病毒药物 活性的化合物。
核心方法
随后的分馏和纯化分离出了四种螺甾烷型甾体皂苷,并通过 UPLC-DAD-MS 和 1D/2D NMR 光谱进行了结构表征。
主要结果
功能测定表明,这些化合物可抑制 DENV 复制,病毒 RNA 水平、NS1 和 NS3 蛋白表达以及传染性病毒滴度显着降低就证明了这一点。
解释与创新
阶段特异性分析表明,它们的 抗病毒药物 活性主要与病毒生命周期的细胞内阶段相关,与病毒复制和/或蛋白质加工的抑制一致。
科研与公卫意义
总之,这些发现将螺罗坦皂苷确定为有前途的 抗病毒药物 支架,并支持其作为广谱 抗病毒药物 药物的进一步开发。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性或证据强度。

Solanaceae-Derived Spirostane Saponins Inhibit Dengue Virus Replication and Viral Protein Expression and Exhibit Activity Against Multiple Arboviruses.

Authors: Erika Vanessa Jimenez-Posada, Oscar Marino Mosquera-Martínez, Sara M Robledo, Diego Durango, Wiston Quiñones, Samuel Vizcaíno-Páez, Marlen Martinez-Gutierrez
Original abstract (English)
Mosquito-borne arboviruses, including dengue virus (DENV), Zika virus (ZIKV), and chikungunya virus (CHIKV), represent a growing global health burden, yet no specific antiviral therapies are currently available for most of these infections. This unmet need highlights the importance of identifying novel antiviral compounds from biologically relevant sources such as plant-derived natural products. In this study, a bioactivity-guided approach was applied to methanolic extracts from eight Solanaceae species to identify compounds with antiviral activity against DENV-2 in Vero cells. Five extracts significantly reduced viral infectivity, with Solanum ovalifolium and Cestrum sp. achieving complete inhibition at non-cytotoxic concentrations. Subsequent fractionation and purification led to the isolation of four spirostane-type steroidal saponins, structurally characterized by UPLC-DAD-MS and 1D/2D NMR spectroscopy. Functional assays demonstrated that these compounds inhibit DENV replication, as evidenced by marked reductions in viral RNA levels, NS1 and NS3 protein expression, and infectious viral titers. Stage-specific analyses revealed that their antiviral activity is primarily associated with intracellular phases of the viral life cycle, consistent with inhibition of viral replication and/or protein processing. Molecular docking analyses supported these findings by predicting favorable interactions with key viral enzymes, particularly NS5 RNA-dependent RNA polymerase and NS3 helicase/protease. Importantly, the isolated compounds retained antiviral activity against ZIKV and CHIKV, indicating cross-arboviral efficacy. Together, these findings identify spirostane saponins as promising antiviral scaffolds and support their further development as broad-spectrum antiviral agents.

Limitations and evidence strength

登革病毒 血清型 2 感染转录重编程 宿主 中性粒细胞以在小鼠中建立免疫抑制微环境

Dengue Virus Serotype 2 Infection Transcriptionally Reprograms Host Neutrophils to Establish an Immunosuppressive Microenvironment in Mice
作者： Surender Rawat, Abhigya Chhetri, Sakshi Nimesh, Abhay Deep Pandey, Srikanth Sadhu, Gazala Siddqui, Sweety Samal, Amit Awasthi, Arup Kumar Banerjee
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革病毒 (DV) 感染引发 宿主 免疫应答 的深刻改变,但疾病进展过程中先天免疫细胞的转录组重编程仍不完全清楚。在这里,我们证明 登革病毒 血清型 2 (DV-2) 感染可转录重新编程 宿主 中性粒细胞,建立有利于 发病机制 的免疫抑制微环境。通过对感染 DV-2 的 AG129 小鼠模型的骨髓和脾细胞进行 scRNA 转录组分析,我们描绘了独特的中性粒细胞特征,该特征显示了登革热感染过程中未成熟和激活群体的动态变化。我们还描述了随着疾病进展而出现的特定 Ly6G 低亚群,特别是纤维蛋白原样蛋白 2 (FGL2) 表达亚群。感染 DV-2 的小鼠和人类登革热阳性患者的血清 FGL2 水平均显着升高。此外,具有 FGL2 或富含 FGL2 的中性粒细胞分泌组的脾细胞的 治疗 抑制 T 细胞功能活性并促进免疫抑制表型。总之,这些发现揭示了 DV-2 驱动的中性粒细胞重编程如何形成免疫抑制网络,强调了研究表达 FGL2 的中性粒细胞亚群对于了解登革热免疫发病机制的重要性。这项研究的附加值这项研究提供了小鼠模型中 登革病毒 血清型 2 感染期间中性粒细胞异质性的单细胞转录组图,揭示了从早期保护亚群到严重阶段、免疫抑制性 Ly6G 低和不成熟亚群的关键时间转变。进展会触发异常细胞亚群 (Ly6G low ),该亚群分泌可溶性纤维蛋白原样蛋白 2 (FGL2) 并抑制 T 细胞功能活性,从而促进免疫抑制表型。通过验证人类登革热患者血浆 FGL2 水平升高,这项工作确定了 Ly6G 低中性粒细胞亚群,其分泌 FGL2 作为小鼠模型中 重症登革热 免疫抑制的可能介质。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解 宿主 中性粒细胞在 登革病毒 血清型 2 (DV-2) 感染期间的转录重编程,解决 宿主 免疫应答 在 登革病毒 感染期间不完全了解的变化。
设计与对象
该研究使用 AG129 小鼠模型来研究 DV-2 感染对中性粒细胞的影响,重点关注骨髓和脾细胞的 scRNA 转录组分析。
核心方法
采用单细胞 RNA 测序 (scRNA-seq) 来分析 DV-2 感染期间中性粒细胞的转录组变化,识别不同的中性粒细胞特征和子集。
主要结果
研究发现,DV-2 感染可重新编程中性粒细胞,导致 Ly6G 低亚群的出现,表达纤维蛋白原样蛋白 2 (FGL2) 并抑制 T 细胞功能活性,从而建立免疫抑制微环境。
解释与创新
该研究揭示了 DV-2 感染重新编程中性粒细胞以抑制 免疫应答 的新机制,强调了表达 FGL2 的中性粒细胞亚群在登革热免疫发病机制中的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为针对表达 FGL2 的中性粒细胞的治疗策略的开发提供信息,以减轻登革热免疫抑制并改善患者的预后。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括使用小鼠模型,该模型可能无法完全代表人类登革热感染,并且需要在人类样本中进行进一步验证。

Dengue Virus Serotype 2 Infection Transcriptionally Reprograms Host Neutrophils to Establish an Immunosuppressive Microenvironment in Mice

Authors: Surender Rawat, Abhigya Chhetri, Sakshi Nimesh, Abhay Deep Pandey, Srikanth Sadhu, Gazala Siddqui, Sweety Samal, Amit Awasthi, Arup Kumar Banerjee
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Dengue virus (DV) infection triggers profound alterations in host immune responses, yet the transcriptomic reprogramming of innate immune cells during disease progression remains incompletely understood. Here, we demonstrated that Dengue virus serotype 2 (DV-2) infection transcriptionally reprograms host neutrophils, establishing an immunosuppressive microenvironment that facilitates pathogenesis. Through scRNA transcriptomic profiling of bone marrow and spleen cells from the DV-2-infected AG129 mouse model, we delineated a distinct neutrophil signature that shows dynamic shifts in immature and activated populations over the course of dengue infection. We also characterized a specific Ly6G low subpopulation, notably Fibrinogen-like protein 2 (FGL2)-expressing subsets, that appeared with disease progression. Serum FGL2 levels were significantly elevated in both DV-2-infected mice and human dengue-positive patients. Furthermore, treatment of splenocytes with FGL2 or FGL2-enriched neutrophil secretomes suppressed T cell functional activity and promoted an immunosuppressive phenotype. Together, these findings reveal how DV-2-driven neutrophil reprogramming shapes an immunosuppressive network, highlighting the importance of studying FGL2-expressing neutrophil subsets to understand dengue immunopathogenesis. Added value of this study This study provides the single-cell transcriptomic map of neutrophil heterogeneity during dengue virus serotype 2 infection in a mouse model, uncovering a critical temporal shift from early protective subsets to a severe-stage, immunosuppressive Ly6G low and immature subpopulation. Progression triggers an abnormal cell subpopulation (Ly6G low ) that secretes the soluble protein Fibrinogen-like protein 2 (FGL2) and suppresses T cell functional activity, promoting an immunosuppressive phenotype. By validating elevated plasma FGL2 levels in human dengue patients, this work identifies Ly6G low neutrophil subsets that secrete FGL2 as a possible mediator of immunosuppression in severe dengue in the mouse model.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the transcriptional reprogramming of host neutrophils during dengue virus serotype 2 (DV-2) infection, addressing the incompletely understood alterations in host immune responses during dengue virus infection.
Design and population
The study used an AG129 mouse model to investigate the effects of DV-2 infection on neutrophils, focusing on the scRNA transcriptomic profiling of bone marrow and spleen cells.
Core methods
Single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq) was employed to profile the transcriptomic changes in neutrophils during DV-2 infection, identifying distinct neutrophil signatures and subsets.
Main results
DV-2 infection was found to reprogram neutrophils, leading to the emergence of a Ly6G low subpopulation that expresses Fibrinogen-like protein 2 (FGL2) and suppresses T cell functional activity, establishing an immunosuppressive microenvironment.
Interpretation and novelty
The study revealed a novel mechanism by which DV-2 infection reprograms neutrophils to suppress immune responses, highlighting the importance of FGL2-expressing neutrophil subsets in dengue immunopathogenesis.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings could inform the development of therapeutic strategies targeting FGL2-expressing neutrophils to mitigate dengue immunosuppression and improve patient outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the use of a mouse model, which may not fully represent human dengue infection, and the need for further validation in human samples.

急性横贯性脊髓炎是 登革热 的罕见神经系统并发症:病例报告

Acute Transverse Myelitis as a Rare Neurological Complication of Dengue Fever: A Case Report
作者： Muhammad Usman Iqbal, Tabassum Ali, Maria Qadri, Abdul Salam, Naseeb Danaf
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景/简介 登革热是一种血源性疾病,具有一致的发热、肌痛和血小板减少症状,而登革热累及神经系统的报道很少。本 病例报告 介绍了登革热诱发的急性横贯性脊髓炎 (ATM) 的罕见病例,强调了早期 诊断 的重要性,并提示 治疗 预防永久性神经功能缺损。病例介绍 患者出现发烧、眼眶后疼痛和呕吐。登革热 血清学 的血液样本(包括 IgM 和 IgG)呈阳性。随后,他突然出现下肢截瘫,脐以下感觉丧失。神经系统检查显示双下肢力量为零,巴宾斯基征阳性,下肢反射活跃,大小便失控。脊髓磁共振成像显示弥漫性超强信号。ATM 可能的 诊断 是由于并发登革热感染且缺乏其他发现表明 诊断 不是登革热而产生的。他接受静脉注射甲泼尼龙和托珠单抗治疗,病情开始逐渐好转。结论 该病例强调 ATM 是 登革热 的一种罕见但可逆的神经系统并发症。通过神经影像学早期识别和 诊断 并排除其他原因,然后及时开始大剂量皮质类固醇和免疫调节治疗,可以带来良好的结果。在登革热流行地区,对这种关联的认识对于确保及时 治疗 和最佳恢复至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在强调 登革热 的罕见神经并发症,特别是急性横贯性脊髓炎,及其对患者预后的潜在影响。
设计与对象
本 病例报告 描述了一名患有急性横贯性脊髓炎的 登革热 患者,强调了临床表现和诊断过程。
核心方法
通过临床评估、登革热血清学检测和脊髓磁共振成像来评估患者的病情,以确认急性横贯性脊髓炎的诊断。
主要结果
登革热 血清学 检测 IgM 和 IgG 呈阳性,磁共振成像显示脊髓中弥漫性超强信号。患者突然出现截瘫,脐以下感觉丧失。
解释与创新
作者将此病例解释为 登革热 罕见但可逆的神经系统并发症的一个例子,强调了早期 诊断 和 治疗 的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
该病例凸显了在流行地区识别登革热引起的神经系统并发症的重要性,以确保及时干预并改善患者康复。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单个 病例报告,可能无法推广。然而,鉴于明确的临床和诊断结果,证据是强有力的。

Acute Transverse Myelitis as a Rare Neurological Complication of Dengue Fever: A Case Report

Authors: Muhammad Usman Iqbal, Tabassum Ali, Maria Qadri, Abdul Salam, Naseeb Danaf
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background/ Introduction Dengue is a blood borne disease having a consistent presentation of fever, myalgia and thrombocytopenia while neurological involvement in dengue is rarely reported. This case report presents a rare case of dengue induced acute transverse myelitis (ATM) emphasizing the importance of early diagnosis and prompt treatment to prevent permanent neurological deficits. Case presentation The patient presented with fever, retro orbital pain and vomiting. A blood sample for dengue serology including IgM and IgG were positive. Later on, he developed sudden onset of paraplegia of lower limbs and loss of sensations below the level of umbilicus. Neurological examination revealed a power of zero in both lower limbs, positive Babinski sign, brisk lower limb reflexes and loss of control of bowel and bladder. Magnetic resonance imaging of the spinal cord revealed diffuse hyper intense signals. A possible diagnosis of ATM was made due to concurrent dengue infection and lack of other findings suggesting a diagnosis other than dengue. He was treated with IV methylprednisolone and tocilizumab and his condition started improving gradually. Conclusion This case underscores ATM as a rare yet reversible neurological complication of dengue fever. Early recognition and diagnosis through neuroimaging and exclusion of other causes, followed by timely initiation of high-dose corticosteroids and immunomodulatory therapy, can lead to favorable outcomes. Awareness of this association is very crucial in endemic regions of dengue to ensure prompt treatment and optimal recovery.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to highlight the rare neurological complication of dengue fever, specifically acute transverse myelitis, and its potential impact on patient outcomes.
Design and population
This case report describes a single patient with dengue fever who developed acute transverse myelitis, emphasizing the clinical presentation and diagnostic process.
Core methods
The patient's condition was assessed through clinical evaluation, dengue serology testing, and magnetic resonance imaging of the spinal cord to confirm the diagnosis of acute transverse myelitis.
Main results
Dengue serology tests were positive for IgM and IgG, and magnetic resonance imaging revealed diffuse hyper intense signals in the spinal cord. The patient experienced sudden onset of paraplegia and loss of sensations below the umbilicus.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret this case as an example of a rare yet reversible neurological complication of dengue fever, underscoring the need for early diagnosis and treatment.
Scientific and public-health significance
This case highlights the importance of recognizing dengue-induced neurological complications in endemic regions to ensure timely intervention and improve patient recovery.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single case report, which may not be generalizable. However, the evidence is strong given the clear clinical and diagnostic findings.

非接触式指尖视频可区分 登革热 血浆渗漏中的登革出血热:一项前瞻性概念验证 队列研究 研究

Non-contact fingertip video to differentiate dengue haemorrhagic fever in plasma leakage from dengue fever: A prospective proof-of-concept cohort study
作者： M. Thisara S. Perera, Shanali Thathsarani Manamperi, S.A.A. Janindu M. Athukorala, K.W. Sameera M. Wijayawardhana, Akhila Rakshitha Wimalasundera, Salomi Arasaratnam, D.M. Janitha Prathapa, Yudara Kularathne, Perera MHMTS, Thisara Shadwarna Perera
摘要中文翻译大多数登革热患者无需干预即可康复,但少数患者出现血浆渗漏,威胁循环,区分他们是登革热护理的核心问题。血小板计数是常规系列使用的标志物,本身也是登革出血热 (DHF) 的四个定义标准之一,因此 登革热 (DF) 中的血小板减少症可能与 DHF 在用于区分它们的标志物上重叠。我们询问非接触式光信号是否携带血小板计数以外的辨别信息。我们在国家登革热转诊中心进行了一项前瞻性观察性 队列研究 研究。所有 DHF 记录都是在血浆泄漏发生时或之后获得的,因此分析比较的是既定的临床状态,而不是泄漏前的风险。记录连续非接触式指尖视频并同时进行临床和血液学测量。在保留的 50 个记录(20 个 DHF、30 个 DF)中,视频推导的概率以 0.963 的接收者操作特性曲线 (AUC) 下面积(95% CI 0.918-1.000;八种架构的平均值为 0.957)将各组分开。在 49 个血小板计数记录中,视频 AUC 为 0.961,血小板计数为 0.888(差异 0.074,p = 0.19)。在探索性分析中,视频添加了血小板计数信息(似然比 p < 0.001),显示与血小板计数没有显着的组内相关性,并且相对于血小板减少 DF 保留了 0.970 的 AUC,其中血小板计数降至 0.759。床边脉搏率、体温和血细胞比容没有区分各组,并且与视频评分无关。在该测试集中,一段 30 秒的指尖视频将血浆渗漏中的 DHF 与 DF 区分开来,探索性分析表明它携带的信息超出了血小板计数。这些发现是单中心内部验证的概念证明,而不是泄漏前预测的证据;需要进行前瞻性预泄漏研究和外部验证,以确定该信号是否可以支持早期风险分层。作者摘要登革热在斯里兰卡很常见。虽然大多数患者都能康复,但仍有一部分患者会出现严重的血管液体渗漏,如果不及时治疗,可能会导致休克、出血和器官功能障碍。我们监测血小板计数,因为它们可以追踪疾病进展,但漏液患者和不漏液患者的计数可能会下降,因此仅凭低计数无法区分两组。我们研究了一段简短的非接触式指尖视频是否可以区分这些群体,而不仅仅是血小板计数。我们在泄漏发生时或之后录制了登革出血热视频,并且在训练中保留的测试记录中,视频将两组分开,并且在探索性分析中,似乎添加了血小板计数之外的信息。这在两组血小板计数低的患者中都存在。这项单家医院研究并未建立可供临床使用的诊断测试。其意义在于表明简单的光学记录可以检测与已确定的疾病相关的差异。我们的下一个问题是,是否可以在泄漏开始之前检测到这种差异?如果是这样,相同的记录最终可以帮助在人群水平上确定哪些登革热患者最有可能出现这些并发症。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革出血热 (DHF) 是一种以血浆渗漏为特征的危急病症,如果不及时治疗会带来重大风险。由于临床标志物重叠,区分 DHF 和 登革热 (DF) 仍然是登革热护理中的一个主要挑战。
设计与对象
一项前瞻性观察性 队列研究 研究在国家登革热转诊中心进行,重点关注已确定 DHF 和 DF 临床状态的患者。该研究包括 50 份记录,其中 20 例 DHF 和 30 例 DF 病例,全部在血浆泄漏发生时或之后获得。
核心方法
记录连续非接触式指尖视频以及同时进行的临床和血液学测量。使用受试者工作特征曲线 (AUC) 下的面积对这些视频进行分析,以评估其与血小板计数相比的辨别能力。
主要结果
在保留的 50 个录音集中,视频导出的概率将 DHF 组和 DF 组分开,AUC 为 0.963 (95% CI 0.918-1.000)。该视频还显示与血小板计数没有显着的组内相关性,并且相对于血小板减少性 DF 保留了 0.970 的 AUC,其中血小板计数降至 0.759。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,简短的非接触式指尖视频可以提供血小板计数之外的辨别信息,而血小板计数是 DHF 的关键标志。这种方法可以为区分 DHF 和 DF 提供更多见解。
科研与公卫意义
该研究表明,简单的光学记录可能有助于识别有 重症登革热 并发症风险的患者,为风险分层和早期干预提供新工具。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单一医院环境,并未建立用于临床的诊断测试。证据是内部验证,还需要进一步的前瞻性预泄漏研究和外部验证来确认其实用性。

Non-contact fingertip video to differentiate dengue haemorrhagic fever in plasma leakage from dengue fever: A prospective proof-of-concept cohort study

Authors: M. Thisara S. Perera, Shanali Thathsarani Manamperi, S.A.A. Janindu M. Athukorala, K.W. Sameera M. Wijayawardhana, Akhila Rakshitha Wimalasundera, Salomi Arasaratnam, D.M. Janitha Prathapa, Yudara Kularathne, Perera MHMTS, Thisara Shadwarna Perera
Original abstract (English)
Most patients with dengue recover without intervention, but a minority develop plasma leakage that threatens the circulation, and differentiating them is the central problem in dengue care. Platelet count is the marker in routine serial use and is itself one of the four defining criteria of dengue haemorrhagic fever (DHF), so thrombocytopenia in dengue fever (DF) can overlap with DHF on the very marker used to distinguish them. We asked whether a non-contact optical signal carries discriminative information beyond platelet count. We conducted a prospective observational cohort study at a national dengue referral centre. All DHF recordings were obtained at or after plasma-leakage onset, so the analysis compares established clinical states rather than pre-leakage risk. Serial non-contact fingertip videos were recorded with contemporaneous clinical and haematological measurements. In a held-out set of 50 recordings (20 DHF, 30 DF), video-derived probability separated the groups with an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of 0.963 (95% CI 0.918-1.000; 0.957 averaged across eight architectures). In the 49 recordings with a platelet count, the video AUC was 0.961 against 0.888 for platelet count (difference 0.074, p = 0.19). In exploratory analyses the video added information to platelet count (likelihood-ratio p < 0.001), showed no significant within-group correlation with platelet count, and retained an AUC of 0.970 against thrombocytopenic DF, where platelet count fell to 0.759. Bedside pulse rate, body temperature and haematocrit did not separate the groups and were unrelated to the video score. A [~]30-second fingertip video separated DHF in plasma leakage from DF in this test set, and exploratory analyses suggest it carries information beyond platelet count. These findings are proof of concept from single-centre internal validation, not evidence of pre-leakage prediction; prospective pre-leakage studies and external validation are needed to establish whether the signal can support earlier risk stratification. Author summaryDengue is common in Sri Lanka. While most patients recover, a subset experiences a critical leakage of fluid from their blood vessels, which can progress to shock, bleeding and organ dysfunction if untreated. We monitor platelet counts because they track disease progression, but counts can fall in patients who leak fluid and in those who do not, so a low count alone cannot separate the two groups. We investigated whether a brief, non-contact fingertip video could tell these groups apart, beyond platelet count. We recorded dengue haemorrhagic fever videos at or after the onset of leakage, and in test recordings held out from training, the video separated the two groups and, in exploratory analyses, appeared to add information beyond the platelet count. This held among patients with low platelet counts in both groups. This single-hospital study does not establish a diagnostic test for clinical use. Its significance is in showing that a simple optical recording can detect a difference associated with established disease. Our next question is whether this difference can be detected before leakage begins; if so, the same recording could eventually help identify, at a population level, which dengue patients are most at risk of these complications.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue haemorrhagic fever (DHF) is a critical condition characterized by plasma leakage, which poses significant risks if untreated. Differentiating DHF from dengue fever (DF) remains a central challenge in dengue care due to overlapping clinical markers.
Design and population
A prospective observational cohort study was conducted at a national dengue referral centre, focusing on patients with established clinical states of DHF and DF. The study included 50 recordings, with 20 DHF and 30 DF cases, all obtained at or after plasma-leakage onset.
Core methods
Serial non-contact fingertip videos were recorded alongside contemporaneous clinical and haematological measurements. These videos were analyzed using an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) to assess their discriminative ability compared to platelet count.
Main results
In a held-out set of 50 recordings, the video-derived probability separated DHF and DF groups with an AUC of 0.963 (95% CI 0.918-1.000). The video also showed no significant within-group correlation with platelet count and retained an AUC of 0.970 against thrombocytopenic DF, where platelet count fell to 0.759.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that a brief, non-contact fingertip video can provide discriminative information beyond platelet count, which is a key marker for DHF. This approach may offer additional insights into differentiating DHF from DF.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study demonstrates that a simple optical recording could potentially aid in identifying patients at risk of severe dengue complications, offering a new tool for risk stratification and early intervention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single-hospital setting and does not establish a diagnostic test for clinical use. The evidence is internal validation, and further prospective pre-leakage studies and external validation are needed to confirm its utility.

孟加拉国的登革热 暴发:从反应性控制到可持续预防

Dengue Outbreaks in Bangladesh: From Reactive Control to Sustainable Prevention
作者： Mohammad Saiful Hasan, Abul Bashar
摘要中文翻译在孟加拉国,登革热已从偶尔的城市流行病转变为反复发生的国家公共卫生紧急事件。2023 年的 暴发 记录了 321,179 例病例和 1,705 例死亡,这是有记录以来的最高年度死亡人数,传播 到达了曾经被认为是低风险的地区。此后的几个赛季已经证实,这个问题是持续存在的,而不是偶发的。这篇社论认为,围绕季节性雾霾、紧急医院扩建和公众呼吁而采取的应对措施已不再与威胁相称。来自达卡的证据表明,主要的埃及伊蚊(媒介)对拟除虫菊酯具有强烈的耐药性,许多患者在入院一天内死亡。我们概述了向综合性、基于证据的预防的转变:以耐药性为导向的 媒介 管理、社区动员、与血清型数据相关的全年 监测、各级护理中预警信号的早期临床识别,以及对沃尔巴克氏体释放和 疫苗 的仔细评估。医学院作为教学医院和培训机构,在这一转变中发挥着核心作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在孟加拉国,登革热已从偶尔的城市流行病转变为反复发生的国家公共卫生紧急事件。该研究旨在解决对更可持续的登革热预防方法的需求。
设计与对象
2023 年的 暴发 记录了 321,179 例病例和 1,705 例死亡,这是有记录以来的最高年度死亡人数,传播 到达了曾经被认为是低风险的地区。此后的几个赛季已经证实,这个问题是持续存在的,而不是偶发的。
核心方法
该研究分析了 2023 年 暴发 及后续季节的数据,以评估登革热 传播 的持续性。它还评估了当前控制措施的有效性并提出了替代策略。
主要结果
来自达卡的证据表明,主要的埃及伊蚊(媒介)对拟除虫菊酯具有强烈的耐药性,许多患者在入院一天内死亡。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为当前反应措施不足的证据,他们建议转向综合的、基于证据的预防策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,需要更有效的 媒介 管理、社区动员和 监测 系统来应对孟加拉国持续存在的登革热威胁。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于抽象证据,这可能会限制分析的深度。然而,2023 年 暴发 及后续赛季的数据为结论提供了中等程度的可信度。

Dengue Outbreaks in Bangladesh: From Reactive Control to Sustainable Prevention

Authors: Mohammad Saiful Hasan, Abul Bashar
Original abstract (English)
Dengue has moved from an occasional urban epidemic to a recurring national public health emergency in Bangladesh. The 2023 outbreak recorded 321,179 cases and 1,705 deaths, the highest annual toll on record, and transmission reached districts once considered low-risk. The seasons since have confirmed that the problem is persistent rather than episodic. This editorial argues that a response built around seasonal fogging, emergency hospital expansion and public appeals is no longer proportionate to the threat. Evidence from Dhaka shows intense pyrethroid resistance in Aedes aegypti, the principal vector, and many patients died within a day of admission. We outline a shift towards integrated, evidence-based prevention: resistance-guided vector management, community mobilisation, year-round surveillance linked to serotype data, early clinical recognition of warning signs at every level of care, and careful evaluation of Wolbachia releases and vaccines. Medical colleges, as teaching hospitals and training institutions, have a central role in this transition.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue has moved from an occasional urban epidemic to a recurring national public health emergency in Bangladesh. The study aimed to address the need for a more sustainable approach to dengue prevention.
Design and population
The 2023 outbreak recorded 321,179 cases and 1,705 deaths, the highest annual toll on record, and transmission reached districts once considered low-risk. The seasons since have confirmed that the problem is persistent rather than episodic.
Core methods
The study analyzed data from the 2023 outbreak and subsequent seasons to assess the persistence of dengue transmission. It also evaluated the effectiveness of current control measures and proposed alternative strategies.
Main results
Evidence from Dhaka shows intense pyrethroid resistance in Aedes aegypti, the principal vector, and many patients died within a day of admission.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as evidence that the current reactive measures are insufficient, and they propose a shift towards integrated, evidence-based prevention strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for more effective vector management, community mobilization, and surveillance systems to address the persistent threat of dengue in Bangladesh.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence, which may limit the depth of analysis. However, the data from the 2023 outbreak and subsequent seasons provide a moderate level of confidence in the conclusions.

解决南半球气候敏感疾病的组织障碍:秘鲁登革热应对案例研究

Organizational barriers to addressing climate-sensitive diseases in the Global South: A case study of Peru’s dengue response
作者： Maria Isabel Espinoza
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究需要了解组织结构如何影响政府应对登革热等公共卫生威胁的能力。该论文探讨了卫生系统的组织如何影响南半球应对气候敏感疾病的能力。
设计与对象
The study is a case study of Peru’s dengue control program, examining how decentralization and performance-based management, which are legacies of New Public Management (NPM) principles and neoliberal reforms in the 1990s and early 2000s, shape the organizational culture and design of the health system.
核心方法
该研究采用基于组织社会学的定性分析,探讨权力下放和基于绩效的管理对组织文化和秘鲁卫生系统设计的影响。
主要结果
研究结果揭示了政府层面和政策部门之间协调的几个障碍,包括文化因素(如无背景的普遍主义)和组织设计特征(如分散的数据系统)、缺乏对公共卫生职业的激励以及严格的基于绩效的预算。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为强调组织设计和文化如何阻碍登革热应对的有效协调。新颖之处在于将组织结构与气候敏感疾病背景下的公共卫生结果联系起来。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为改善卫生系统部门间合作和组织设计的策略提供参考,以更好地应对登革热等公共卫生威胁,特别是在南半球国家。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其定性性质和只关注单一国家而受到限制,这可能会影响其研究结果的普遍性。由于缺乏定量数据,证据强度中等。

Organizational barriers to addressing climate-sensitive diseases in the Global South: A case study of Peru’s dengue response

Authors: Maria Isabel Espinoza
Original abstract (English)
To address infectious diseases such as dengue, the World Health organization advocates a 'whole-of-government' approach that promotes coordination across all levels of government within the health sector and across related sectors. Yet not all health systems have the organizational arrangements needed to facilitate such coordination. This paper examines Peru's dengue control program to investigate how the organization of the health system shapes the government's capacity to respond to public health threats. Drawing on the sociology of organizations and qualitative analysis, the study examines how decentralization and performance-based management-legacies of adopting New Public Management (NPM) principles and neoliberal reforms in the 1990s and early 2000s-shape the organizational culture and design of Peru's health system. The findings identify several barriers to coordination across government levels and policy sectors. These include cultural factors such as context-blind universalism and organizational environments that discourage public-sector entrepreneurship, as well as organizational design features such as fragmented data systems, a lack of incentives to pursue a public health career, and rigid performance-based budgeting. Together, these factors limit coordination within the health sector and hinder intersectoral collaboration. The study proposes that improving the government's capacity to respond to health threats requires integrating whole-of-government principles into existing NPM arrangements to strengthen both hierarchical and horizontal coordination.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study was needed to understand how organizational structures affect the government's ability to respond to public health threats like dengue. The paper asks how the organization of the health system influences the capacity to address climate-sensitive diseases in the Global South.
Design and population
The study is a case study of Peru’s dengue control program, examining how decentralization and performance-based management, which are legacies of New Public Management (NPM) principles and neoliberal reforms in the 1990s and early 2000s, shape the organizational culture and design of the health system.
Core methods
The study uses qualitative analysis grounded in the sociology of organizations to explore the impact of decentralization and performance-based management on the organizational culture and design of Peru's health system.
Main results
The findings reveal several barriers to coordination across government levels and policy sectors, including cultural factors such as context-blind universalism and organizational design features like fragmented data systems, lack of incentives for public health careers, and rigid performance-based budgeting.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings as highlighting how organizational design and culture hinder effective coordination in dengue response. The novelty lies in linking organizational structures to public health outcomes in the context of climate-sensitive diseases.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform strategies to improve intersectoral collaboration and organizational design in health systems to better address public health threats like dengue, particularly in the Global South.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its qualitative nature and focus on a single country, which may affect the generalizability of its findings. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the lack of quantitative data.

对 登革病毒 衣壳蛋白构象动力学的计算见解。

Computational Insights Into the Conformational Dynamics of the Dengue Virus Capsid Protein.
作者： Dwaipayan Chaudhuri, Kalyan Giri
摘要中文翻译登革病毒 (DENV) 衣壳蛋白在病毒生命周期中发挥重要作用,包括 基因组 的衣壳化、脂滴的招募和颗粒的组装。衣壳蛋白包含一个疏水口袋,它在这些功能以及闭合和开放构象之间的转换中发挥着核心作用。这项工作研究了衣壳蛋白的构象变化途径,特别是如何控制控制疏水袋打开和关闭的分子机制。通过结合使用不同类型的分子动力学模拟和增强的采样方法,我们分享了对在控制这些转变中发挥关键作用的结构重排的见解。我们的结果表明,脂质结合通过环境因素调节疏水袋的可及性。在开放构象中,口袋参与与脂质的相互作用、衣壳二聚体的稳定以及促进未成熟病毒的组装。相反,闭合构象可以保护疏水袋免受周围水介质的影响,同时还可以防止溶液相中过早的相互作用,从而破坏蛋白质的稳定性。这些见解使人们能够深入了解衣壳蛋白的构象动力学,并为旨在干扰其功能转变的 抗病毒药物 策略提供了一组有前景的有效靶标。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解 登革病毒 衣壳蛋白的构象动力学,特别是分子机制如何控制其疏水口袋的打开和关闭,这对于其在病毒生命周期中的功能至关重要。
设计与对象
这项计算研究的重点是 登革病毒 衣壳蛋白,通过分子动力学模拟和增强的采样方法检查其结构重排。
核心方法
该研究结合了分子动力学模拟和增强采样技术来研究衣壳蛋白的结构转变,特别是其疏水口袋的打开和关闭。
主要结果
结果表明,脂质结合通过调节环境信号影响疏水袋的可及性,从而影响其功能转变。
解释与创新
这些发现提供了对衣壳蛋白构象动力学的更深入的了解,并强调了其作为干扰其功能转变的 抗病毒药物 策略的目标的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
这些见解有助于开发针对 登革病毒 衣壳蛋白的 抗病毒药物 策略,该策略有助于预防和预防登革热感染。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于计算模型,可能无法完全捕捉体内的复杂性,但由于对结构转变的详细分析,证据是强有力的。

Computational Insights Into the Conformational Dynamics of the Dengue Virus Capsid Protein.

Authors: Dwaipayan Chaudhuri, Kalyan Giri
Original abstract (English)
The Dengue virus (DENV) capsid protein plays an important role in the viral lifecycle, including encapsidation of the genome, recruitment of lipid droplets, and assembly of particles. Capsid protein includes a hydrophobic pocket, which plays a central role in these functions and switches between closed and open conformations. This work studies the conformational change pathway of the capsid protein, specifically how the molecular mechanisms controlling the opening and closing of the hydrophobic pocket are controlled. By using a combination of different types of molecular dynamics simulations and enhanced sampling methods, we share insights on the structural rearrangements that play a crucial role in governing these transitions. Our results show that lipid binding modulates the accessibility of the hydrophobic pocket through environmental cues. In the open conformation, the pocket is involved in the interaction with lipids, stabilization of capsid dimers, and the promotion of immature virus assembly. Conversely, the closed conformation protects the hydrophobic pocket from the surrounding aqueous media as also preventing premature interactions in the solution phase that might disrupt protein stability. Such insights allow a deep understanding of capsid protein's conformational dynamics and represent a promising set of potent targets for antiviral strategies aiming to interfere with its functional transitions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the conformational dynamics of the Dengue virus capsid protein, particularly how the molecular mechanisms control the opening and closing of its hydrophobic pocket, which is essential for its functions in the viral lifecycle.
Design and population
This computational study focused on the Dengue virus capsid protein, examining its structural rearrangements through molecular dynamics simulations and enhanced sampling methods.
Core methods
The study employed a combination of molecular dynamics simulations and enhanced sampling techniques to investigate the structural transitions of the capsid protein, specifically the opening and closing of its hydrophobic pocket.
Main results
The results indicate that lipid binding influences the accessibility of the hydrophobic pocket by modulating environmental cues, which affects its functional transitions.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings provide a deeper understanding of the capsid protein's conformational dynamics and highlight its potential as a target for antiviral strategies that interfere with its functional transitions.
Scientific and public-health significance
These insights contribute to the development of antiviral strategies targeting the Dengue virus capsid protein, which could aid in the prevention and treatment of dengue infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to computational models, which may not fully capture in vivo complexities, but the evidence is strong due to the detailed analysis of structural transitions.

AG129 小鼠模型中 登革病毒 血清型 1-4 致病特征的表征

Characterization of Pathogenic Features of Dengue Virus Serotypes 1–4 in the AG129 Mouse Model
作者： Cong Cai, Kai-Yun Ding, Jia-Hong Gao, Yun Yang, Qing Huang, Cong Tang, Ya-Nan Zhou, Jun-Bin Wang, Hao Yang, De-Xuan Kuang
摘要中文翻译建立了包含四种登革热病毒分离株的 AG129 小鼠感染模型,以系统地表征这些毒株的早期致病表型。DENV-1 在 15 dpi 时导致 100% 死亡率,伴有持续病毒血症、广泛的多器官损伤和持续性白细胞减少症。DENV-4 在 4 dpi 时表现出病毒血症峰值和独特的神经毒力,表现为独特的后肢麻痹、严重肺出血和特征性脑实质病变。DENV-2和DENV-3仅诱导短暂的轻度病毒血症和局部组织改变,而没有死亡率。在受感染的动物中注意到血液学和生化的改变,以及偶尔的脾脏含铁血黄素沉积。该临床前数据集提供了对血清型特异性 DENV 发病机制 的深入了解,并可能有助于未来评估广谱 抗病毒药物 和四价 疫苗 候选药物。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在使用 AG129 小鼠感染模型系统地表征 登革病毒 血清型 1-4 的早期致病表型。
设计与对象
该研究使用感染四种登革热病毒分离株的 AG129 小鼠模型来评估感染后不同时间点的血清型特异性致病特征。
核心方法
研究人员用四种登革热病毒分离株建立了 AG129 小鼠感染模型,并监测了感染动物的血液学、生化和组织病理学变化。
主要结果
DENV-1 在 15 dpi 时导致 100% 死亡率,伴有持续病毒血症、广泛的多器官损伤和持续性白细胞减少症。DENV-4 在 4 dpi 时表现出病毒血症峰值和独特的神经毒力,表现为独特的后肢麻痹、严重肺出血和特征性脑实质病变。DENV-2和DENV-3仅诱导短暂的轻度病毒血症和局部组织改变,而没有死亡率。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了 发病机制 中血清型特异性的差异,提供了可能为未来 抗病毒药物 和 疫苗 开发提供信息的临床前数据集。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对 DENV 血清型差异致病机制的见解,可以指导广谱 抗病毒药物 和四价 疫苗 候选药物的开发。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于临床前模型,证据基于仅抽象数据集的观察,该数据集可能无法完全捕捉人类疾病的复杂性。

Characterization of Pathogenic Features of Dengue Virus Serotypes 1–4 in the AG129 Mouse Model

Authors: Cong Cai, Kai-Yun Ding, Jia-Hong Gao, Yun Yang, Qing Huang, Cong Tang, Ya-Nan Zhou, Jun-Bin Wang, Hao Yang, De-Xuan Kuang
Original abstract (English)
An AG129 mouse infection model with four DENV isolates was established to systematically characterize the early pathogenic phenotypes of these strains. DENV-1 caused 100% mortality by 15 dpi, with sustained viremia, widespread multi-organ injury, and persistent leukopenia. DENV-4 exhibited peak viremia at 4 dpi and unique neurovirulence, presenting exclusive hind-limb paralysis, severe pulmonary hemorrhage, and characteristic cerebral parenchymal lesions. DENV-2 and DENV-3 induced only transient mild viremia and focal tissue alterations without mortality. Hematological and biochemical alterations, as well as occasional splenic hemosiderin deposition, were noted among infected animals. This preclinical dataset offers insights into serotype-specific DENV pathogenesis and may aid future evaluation of broad-spectrum antivirals and tetravalent vaccine candidates.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to systematically characterize the early pathogenic phenotypes of Dengue virus serotypes 1–4 using an AG129 mouse infection model.
Design and population
The study used an AG129 mouse model infected with four DENV isolates to evaluate serotype-specific pathogenic features across different time points post-infection.
Core methods
The researchers established an AG129 mouse infection model with four DENV isolates and monitored hematological, biochemical, and histopathological changes in infected animals.
Main results
DENV-1 caused 100% mortality by 15 dpi, with sustained viremia, widespread multi-organ injury, and persistent leukopenia. DENV-4 exhibited peak viremia at 4 dpi and unique neurovirulence, presenting exclusive hind-limb paralysis, severe pulmonary hemorrhage, and characteristic cerebral parenchymal lesions. DENV-2 and DENV-3 induced only transient mild viremia and focal tissue alterations without mortality.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight serotype-specific differences in pathogenesis, providing a preclinical dataset that may inform future antiviral and vaccine development.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results offer insights into the differential pathogenic mechanisms of DENV serotypes, which could guide the development of broad-spectrum antivirals and tetravalent vaccine candidates.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a preclinical model, and the evidence is based on observations from an abstract-only dataset, which may not fully capture human disease complexity.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

干扰素刺激的长非编码RNA PKR-IT1在调节寨卡病毒和登革病毒复制和抗病毒药物反应中的作用

The role of interferon-stimulated long non-coding RNA PKR-IT1 in the regulation of Zika virus and dengue virus replication and antiviral response
作者： Yi Wang, Ai Fu, Liping Chen, Yue Shi, Qian Chen, Fei Wang, Qian Huang, Shourong Liu
摘要中文翻译摘要 干扰素刺激基因在抵抗黄病毒感染的先天 免疫应答 中发挥着关键作用。虽然最近的研究报道干扰素刺激的lncRNA参与抗病毒药物免疫,但许多lncRNA在宿主-黄病毒相互作用中的功能仍然未知。在这里,我们证明 lncRNA PKR-IT1 的表达是由 ZIKV 和 DENV 感染诱导的。PKR-IT1 在 A549 细胞中由病毒、poly(I:C) 和 IFN 诱导,但在病毒感染期间敲除 IPS-1 或 IFNAR1 后,这种诱导作用基本上被消除。此外,PKR-IT1 过表达抑制病毒产生,而 A549、Huh7 和 SH-SY5Y 细胞中 PKR-IT1 的抑制则增强了 ZIKV 复制。在 PKR-IT1 敲除细胞中,与对照细胞相比,病毒复制也显着增加。有趣的是,PKR-IT1过表达增强了黄病毒感染触发的JAK-STAT信号通路的激活并上调了ISG表达。相反,PKR-IT1 缺陷会降低 JAK1 和 STAT1 磷酸化水平,从而损害 ISG 表达。从机制上讲,PKR-IT1 过表达细胞中 STAT1 的沉默部分恢复了 ZIKV 复制。此外,PKR-IT1直接结合PABPC1并促进其蛋白酶体依赖性降解。人们发现 PABPC1 与 ZIKV RNA 结合,而抑制 PABPC1 会减弱 ZIKV 蛋白、病毒颗粒以及后来病毒 RNA 的水平。值得注意的是,重新引入 PABPC1 可恢复 PKR-IT1 过表达细胞中的病毒复制。临床上,PKR-IT1水平与发热持续时间和症状数量呈负相关,并且在患有严重白细胞减少和血小板减少的DENV感染患者中较低。总之,PKR-IT1 调节黄病毒复制和 JAK-STAT 信号传导,并可能作为登革热严重程度的临床相关因素,为 抗病毒药物 疗法的开发提供见解。重要性 ZIKV 和 DENV 等黄病毒对公共卫生构成重大威胁,但长链非编码 RNA (lncRNA) 在 宿主 抗病毒药物 免疫中的作用仍知之甚少。在这里,我们发现PKR-IT1作为一种干扰素刺激的lncRNA,不仅通过结合PABPC1促进其蛋白酶体介导的降解来抑制黄病毒复制,而且还增强黄病毒感染期间JAK-STAT信号级联的激活。重要的是,临床相关性表明,较低的 PKR-IT1 表达与 DENV 感染患者的严重白细胞减少和血小板减少以及较长的发烧持续时间和更多症状相关。这些发现证实 PKR-IT1 不仅充当黄病毒复制和 JAK-STAT 信号通路的新型调节剂,而且还可能与登革热疾病严重程度相关。通过阐明以前未表征的 lncRNA 介导的调节机制,这项工作为开发针对黄病毒感染的治疗策略和预后工具奠定了基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革病毒 等黄病毒对公共卫生构成重大威胁,但长链非编码 RNA (lncRNA) 在 宿主 抗病毒药物 免疫中的作用仍知之甚少。该研究旨在研究干扰素刺激的lncRNA在宿主-黄病毒相互作用中的功能。
设计与对象
该研究涉及使用 A549、Huh7 和 SH-SY5Y 细胞进行的体外实验,以及登革热感染患者的临床分析。检查了 PKR-IT1 表达对 登革病毒 和寨卡病毒感染的反应,特别关注其对病毒复制和信号通路的影响。
核心方法
研究人员利用 RNA 测序、基因敲除技术和过表达实验来评估 PKR-IT1 的表达和功能。他们还通过生化测定和临床数据分析评估了 PKR-IT1 对 JAK-STAT 信号传导和 ISG 表达的影响。
主要结果
PKR-IT1 过表达抑制 登革病毒 复制,而其抑制则增加病毒复制。在 PKR-IT1 敲除细胞中,病毒复制显着增强。此外,PKR-IT1 过表达会增加 JAK1 和 STAT1 磷酸化水平,而其缺乏会降低这些水平。
解释与创新
研究结果证实,PKR-IT1 作为 登革病毒 复制和 JAK-STAT 信号通路的新型调节剂,为 抗病毒药物 黄病毒免疫机制提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可能为 抗病毒药物 感染的治疗策略和预后工具的开发提供信息,特别是在了解 lncRNA 在 抗病毒药物 反应和疾病严重程度中的作用方面。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外和临床观察,证据仅基于摘要数据。这些发现得到了多种实验方法的支持,表明结论具有中等程度的可信度。

The role of interferon-stimulated long non-coding RNA PKR-IT1 in the regulation of Zika virus and dengue virus replication and antiviral response

Authors: Yi Wang, Ai Fu, Liping Chen, Yue Shi, Qian Chen, Fei Wang, Qian Huang, Shourong Liu
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Interferon-stimulated genes play a critical role in the innate immune response against flaviviral infection. While recent studies have reported that interferon-stimulated lncRNAs participate in antiviral immunity, the functions of numerous lncRNAs in host-flavivirus interactions remain uncharacterized. Here, we demonstrate that the expression of lncRNA PKR-IT1 is induced by ZIKV and DENV infection. PKR-IT1 was induced by virus, poly(I:C), and IFNs in A549 cells, but this induction was largely abolished upon knockout of IPS-1 or IFNAR1 during viral infection. Additionally, PKR-IT1 overexpression inhibited viral production, whereas its suppression in A549, Huh7, and SH-SY5Y cells enhanced ZIKV replication. In PKR-IT1 knockout cells, viral replication also markedly increased compared with that in control cells. Interestingly, PKR-IT1 overexpression enhanced the activation of the JAK-STAT signaling pathway triggered by flaviviral infection and upregulated ISG expression. Conversely, PKR-IT1 deficiency decreased JAK1 and STAT1 phosphorylation levels, thereby impairing ISG expression. Mechanistically, the silencing of STAT1 in PKR-IT1-overexpressing cells partially restored ZIKV replication. Furthermore, PKR-IT1 directly bound PABPC1 and promoted its proteasome-dependent degradation. PABPC1 was found to bind to ZIKV RNA, while the inhibition of PABPC1 attenuated the levels of ZIKV protein, viral particles, and later viral RNA. Notably, the reintroduction of PABPC1 restored viral replication in PKR-IT1-overexpressing cells. Clinically, PKR-IT1 levels negatively correlate with fever duration and symptom number, and are lower in DENV-infected patients with severe leukopenia and thrombocytopenia. In summary, PKR-IT1 modulates flavivirus replication and JAK-STAT signaling, and may serve as a clinical correlate of dengue severity, offering insights for antiviral therapy development. IMPORTANCE Flaviviruses such as ZIKV and DENV pose significant public health threats, yet the roles of long non-coding RNAs (lncRNAs) in host antiviral immunity remain poorly understood. Here, we identified that PKR-IT1, as an interferon-stimulated lncRNA, not only suppresses flavivirus replication by binding PABPC1 to promote its proteasome-mediated degradation, but also enhances the activation of the JAK-STAT signaling cascade during flavivirus infection. Importantly, clinical correlations reveal that lower PKR-IT1 expression is associated with severe leukopenia and thrombocytopenia in DENV-infected patients, as well as longer fever duration and more symptoms. These findings establish that PKR-IT1 not only acts as a novel modulator of both flavivirus replication and the JAK-STAT signaling pathway, but also may serve as a correlate of dengue disease severity. By elucidating a previously uncharacterized lncRNA-mediated regulatory mechanism, this work provides a foundation for developing therapeutic strategies and prognostic tools against flaviviral infections.
