Question and background
Flaviviruses such as dengue virus pose significant public health threats, yet the roles of long non-coding RNAs (lncRNAs) in host antiviral immunity remain poorly understood. The study aimed to investigate the function of interferon-stimulated lncRNAs in host-flavivirus interactions.
Design and population
The study involved in vitro experiments using A549, Huh7, and SH-SY5Y cells, as well as clinical analysis of dengue-infected patients. PKR-IT1 expression was examined in response to dengue virus and Zika virus infection, with specific focus on its effects on viral replication and signaling pathways.
Core methods
The researchers used RNA sequencing, gene knockout techniques, and overexpression experiments to assess PKR-IT1 expression and function. They also evaluated the impact of PKR-IT1 on JAK-STAT signaling and ISG expression through biochemical assays and clinical data analysis.
Main results
PKR-IT1 overexpression inhibited dengue virus replication, while its suppression increased viral replication. In PKR-IT1 knockout cells, viral replication was significantly enhanced. Additionally, PKR-IT1 overexpression increased JAK1 and STAT1 phosphorylation levels, whereas its deficiency decreased these levels.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings establish that PKR-IT1 acts as a novel modulator of both dengue virus replication and the JAK-STAT signaling pathway, providing new insights into the mechanisms of antiviral immunity against flaviviruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results may inform the development of therapeutic strategies and prognostic tools for dengue virus infections, particularly in understanding the role of lncRNAs in antiviral responses and disease severity.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro and clinical observations, and the evidence is based on abstract-only data. The findings are supported by multiple experimental approaches, suggesting a moderate level of confidence in the conclusions.

对 宿主 基因 sigma-1 受体的不同依赖性塑造丙型肝炎和正黄病毒感染。

Divergent dependence on host gene sigma-1 receptor shapes hepatitis C and orthoflavivirus infection.
作者： Carlos García-Crespo, Victor Venturini, Gema Calvo, Emma Díaz-Piñero, Victoria A. Castro, Pablo Gastaminza, Carlos García‐Crespo
摘要中文翻译黄病毒科包括主要的人类病原体,例如丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 和 登革病毒 (DENV)。尽管共享 基因组 组织和复制策略,但这些病毒在 宿主 相互作用、组织向性和复制动力学方面存在差异,从而导致不同的疾病结果。Sigma-1 受体 (SIGMAR1) 编码一种调节脂质转运、自噬和蛋白质稳态的内质网相关蛋白。我们之前表明,SIGMAR1 沉默会降低早期 HCV 感染效率,而不影响不相关的病毒。在这里,我们证明 SIGMAR1 对正常双膜囊泡 (DMV)(假定的 HCV 复制区室)的形成具有限速作用。相比之下,DENV 和西尼罗河病毒 (WNV) RNA 复制在 SIGMAR1 耗尽后仍然有效,表明在 HCV DMV 形成中对 SIGMAR1 的选择性需求。值得注意的是,SIGMAR1 敲低显着损害了传染性 DENV 和 WNV 颗粒的分泌,而不减少细胞内后代,这表明 SIGMAR1 在正黄病毒病毒颗粒释放中发挥作用。SIGMAR1 沉默还破坏了高尔基体结构并改变了细胞耐洗涤剂膜的组成,这与囊泡运输的缺陷一致。这些发现将 SIGMAR1 确定为 宿主 因子,在肝炎病毒和正黄病毒的复制周期中具有独特的功能,并强调其在维持黄病毒科有效感染所需的膜组织方面的更广泛作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
鉴于该家族多样化的 宿主 相互作用和疾病结果,该研究旨在调查 sigma-1 受体在 登革病毒 复制和感染过程中的作用。
设计与对象
该研究涉及检查 sigma-1 受体沉默对 登革病毒 和其他相关黄病毒的影响,重点是复制动力学和病毒粒子释放。
核心方法
研究人员使用 sigma-1 受体沉默技术来评估其对 登革病毒 复制和病毒粒子释放的影响,并将这些影响与在其他相关病毒中观察到的影响进行比较。
主要结果
Sigma-1受体沉默降低了早期登革病毒感染效率并损害了感染性登革病毒颗粒的分泌,但不影响细胞内后代。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,sigma-1 受体在 登革病毒 复制和病毒颗粒释放中发挥着关键作用,突显了其与其他相关黄病毒相比的独特功能。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对 登革病毒 感染的潜在治疗靶点的见解,并强调了 宿主 因子在病毒复制和 发病机制 中的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象级别的证据,结果基于 sigma-1 受体沉默实验,可能无法完全捕捉体内的复杂性。

Divergent dependence on host gene sigma-1 receptor shapes hepatitis C and orthoflavivirus infection.

Authors: Carlos García-Crespo, Victor Venturini, Gema Calvo, Emma Díaz-Piñero, Victoria A. Castro, Pablo Gastaminza, Carlos García‐Crespo
Original abstract (English)
The Flaviviridae family encompasses major human pathogens, such as hepatitis C virus (HCV) and dengue virus (DENV). Despite shared genome organization and replication strategies, these viruses differ in host interactions, tissue tropism and replication dynamics, leading to distinct disease outcomes. Sigma-1 receptor (SIGMAR1) encodes an endoplasmic reticulum-associated protein that regulates lipid transport, autophagy and proteostasis. We previously showed that SIGMAR1 silencing reduces early HCV infection efficiency without affecting unrelated viruses. Here, we demonstrate that SIGMAR1 is rate-limiting for the formation of normal double-membrane vesicles (DMVs), the putative HCV replication compartments. In contrast, DENV and West Nile virus (WNV) RNA replication remained efficient following SIGMAR1 depletion, indicating a selective requirement for SIGMAR1 in HCV DMV formation. Notably, SIGMAR1 knockdown markedly impaired secretion of infectious DENV and WNV particles without reducing intracellular progeny, suggesting a role for SIGMAR1 in orthoflavivirus virion release. SIGMAR1 silencing also disrupted Golgi architecture and altered cellular detergent-resistant membrane composition, consistent with defects in vesicular trafficking. These findings identify SIGMAR1 as a host factor with distinct functions in the replication cycles of hepaciviruses and orthoflaviviruses and highlight its broader role in maintaining membrane organization required for efficient infection by Flaviviridae .
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the role of the sigma-1 receptor in the replication and infection processes of dengue virus, given the family's diverse host interactions and disease outcomes.
Design and population
The study involved examining the effects of sigma-1 receptor silencing on dengue virus and other related flaviviruses, with a focus on replication dynamics and virion release.
Core methods
The researchers used sigma-1 receptor silencing techniques to assess its impact on dengue virus replication and virion release, comparing these effects with those observed in other related viruses.
Main results
Sigma-1 receptor silencing reduced early dengue virus infection efficiency and impaired the secretion of infectious dengue virus particles without affecting intracellular progeny.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the sigma-1 receptor plays a critical role in dengue virus replication and virion release, highlighting its distinct function compared to other related flaviviruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into potential therapeutic targets for dengue virus infection and underscore the importance of host factors in viral replication and pathogenesis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-level evidence, and the results are based on sigma-1 receptor silencing experiments, which may not fully capture in vivo complexities.

一种具有广泛活性的黄病毒 NS4B 靶向化合物,可阻断 NS4B-NS3 结合和复制细胞器形成

A broadly active flavivirus NS4B targeting compound that blocks NS4B-NS3 binding and replication organelle formation
作者： Jessica Louise Smith, Theresa Hoang-Anh Nguyen, Si-Xue Zhang, O. Moukha-Chafiq, C. Augelli-Szafran, Alec J. Hirsch, Omar Moukha‐Chafiq, Corinne E. Augelli‐Szafran
摘要中文翻译摘要 蚊媒正黄病毒,包括西尼罗河病毒 (WNV)、登革病毒 (DENV)、寨卡病毒 (ZIKV) 和日本脑炎病毒 (JEV),导致大量全球发病率和 死亡率,但目前还没有广泛有效的直接作用 抗病毒药物。病毒非结构蛋白 NS4B 已成为一个有前途的 抗病毒药物 靶点,尽管迄今为止描述的化合物主要是 DENV 特异性的。在这里,我们表征了 SRI-38841,这是一种黄嘌呤类似物,源自先前描述的 WNV 抑制剂的命中到先导优化,它抑制 WNV 复制,IC90 为 0.87 μM,细胞毒性低,并保留针对 DENV、ZIKV 和 JEV 的活性。WNV 在化合物选择下的连续传代产生了 NS4B 中带有 突变 的抗性病毒,并使用反向遗传学来确认单个氨基酸取代的作用。其中一些残基映射到与 DENV NS4B 抑制剂 JNJ-A07 和 NITD-688 抗性相关的区域类似的区域。SRI-38841 与 WNV NS4B 的 AlphaFold2 模型的对接预测了面向胞质的漏斗内的结合口袋,口袋残基 A45 和 K56 的 突变 同样赋予了抗性,而 Q170 和 T220 的变化则没有。总之,这些数据有利于一种拓扑模型,其中该化合物与面向细胞质的口袋接合,而不是将这些残基置于内质网腔中。从机制上讲,SRI-38841 破坏了 NS4B-NS3 相互作用,并消除了受感染细胞中复制细胞器的形成。与对 WNV 和 ZIKV 无活性的 JNJ-A07 和 NITD-688 不同,SRI-38841 可抑制所有四种测试的病毒,从而将黄嘌呤类确定为具有广泛活性的正黄病毒 抗病毒药物 的起点。重要性 西尼罗河病毒 (WNV)、登革病毒 (DENV) 和寨卡病毒 (ZIKV) 等蚊媒正黄病毒仍然是全球健康的重大负担,目前尚无广泛有效的直接作用 抗病毒药物。NS4B 已成为该领域最有前途的靶点之一,但迄今为止描述的抑制剂,包括临床阶段化合物 JNJ-A07/JNJ-1802 和 NITD-688,很大程度上是 DENV 特异性的。一直缺乏具有真正的泛正黄病毒活性的 抗病毒药物。在这项研究中,我们表征了 SRI-38841,这是一种黄嘌呤类似物,源自我们之前描述的 WNV 抑制剂的命中到先导优化。SRI-38841 具有广谱活性,对 DENV2、ZIKV、JEV 以及 WNV 均有效。它以 NS4B 蛋白为靶点,耐药情况与上述 DENV 抑制剂一致。最后,SRI-38841 与 AlphaFold2 模型的对接预测了面向胞质的漏斗内的结合口袋。这些结果有利于 NMR/计算拓扑模型,而不是将这些残基置于 ER 腔中——这一区别直接影响了如何理解和优化此类抑制剂。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革病毒 1-4 和登革热疾病仍然是全球重大健康负担,并且没有广泛有效的直接作用 抗病毒药物。病毒非结构蛋白 NS4B 已成为该领域最有前途的靶点之一,但迄今为止描述的抑制剂,包括临床阶段化合物 JNJ-A07/JNJ-1802 和 NITD-688,很大程度上是 DENV 特异性的。
设计与对象
这项研究描述了 SRI-38841 的特征,它是一种黄嘌呤类似物,源自先前描述的 WNV 抑制剂的命中至先导优化,它能抑制 WNV 复制,IC90 为 0.87 μM,细胞毒性低,并保留针对 DENV、ZIKV 和 JEV 的活性。WNV 在化合物选择下的连续传代产生了 NS4B 中带有 突变 的抗性病毒,并使用反向遗传学来确认单个氨基酸取代的作用。
核心方法
SRI-38841 通过先前描述的 WNV 抑制剂的命中到先导优化来表征。在化合物选择下连续传代西尼罗河病毒用于鉴定抗性病毒,并采用反向遗传学来确认单个氨基酸取代在抗性中的作用。SRI-38841 与 WNV NS4B 的 AlphaFold2 模型的对接预测了面向胞质的漏斗内的结合袋。
主要结果
其中一些残基映射到与 DENV NS4B 抑制剂 JNJ-A07 和 NITD-688 抗性相关的区域类似的区域。口袋残基 A45 和 K56 的 突变 同样赋予抗性,而 Q170 和 T220 的变化则没有。
解释与创新
这些结果有利于 NMR/计算拓扑模型,而不是将这些残基置于 ER 腔中——这一区别直接影响了如何理解和优化此类抑制剂。与对 WNV 和 ZIKV 无活性的 JNJ-A07 和 NITD-688 不同,SRI-38841 可抑制所有四种测试的病毒,从而将黄嘌呤类确定为具有广泛活性的正黄病毒 抗病毒药物 的起点。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,SRI-38841 是开发广泛活性的正黄病毒 抗病毒药物 的有希望的候选者,这可能对 登革病毒 感染的 治疗 和其他相关黄病毒产生重大影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于抽象证据,结果来自体外实验。由于缺乏详细的临床或体内数据,证据强度中等。

A broadly active flavivirus NS4B targeting compound that blocks NS4B-NS3 binding and replication organelle formation

Authors: Jessica Louise Smith, Theresa Hoang-Anh Nguyen, Si-Xue Zhang, O. Moukha-Chafiq, C. Augelli-Szafran, Alec J. Hirsch, Omar Moukha‐Chafiq, Corinne E. Augelli‐Szafran
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Mosquito-borne orthoflaviviruses, including West Nile virus (WNV), dengue virus (DENV), Zika virus (ZIKV), and Japanese encephalitis virus (JEV), cause substantial global morbidity and mortality, yet no broadly effective direct-acting antivirals are available. The viral nonstructural protein NS4B has emerged as a promising antiviral target, although the compounds described to date are largely DENV-specific. Here we characterize SRI-38841, a xanthine analog derived from hit-to-lead optimization of a previously described WNV inhibitor, which inhibits WNV replication with an IC90 of 0.87 μM and low cytotoxicity, and retains activity against DENV, ZIKV, and JEV. Serial passage of WNV under compound selection yielded resistant viruses bearing mutations in NS4B, and reverse genetics was used to confirm the role of individual amino acid substitutions. Several of these residues map to regions analogous to those implicated in resistance to the DENV NS4B inhibitors JNJ-A07 and NITD-688. Docking of SRI-38841 to an AlphaFold2 model of WNV NS4B predicted a binding pocket within a cytosolic-facing funnel, and mutation of the pocket residues A45 and K56 likewise conferred resistance, while changes at Q170 and T220 did not. Together, these data favor a topology model in which the compound engages a cytosolic-facing pocket over one placing these residues in the ER lumen. Mechanistically, SRI-38841 disrupts the NS4B–NS3 interaction and abolishes replication organelle formation in infected cells. Unlike JNJ-A07 and NITD-688, which were inactive against WNV and ZIKV, SRI-38841 inhibits all four viruses tested, identifying this xanthine class as a starting point for broadly active orthoflavivirus antivirals. Importance Mosquito-borne orthoflaviviruses such as West Nile virus (WNV), dengue virus (DENV), and Zika virus (ZIKV) remain a substantial global health burden, and no broadly effective direct-acting antivirals are available. NS4B has emerged as one of the most promising targets in this space, but the inhibitors described to date, including the clinical-stage compounds JNJ-A07/JNJ-1802 and NITD-688, are largely DENV-specific. An antiviral with genuine pan-orthoflavivirus activity has been lacking. In this study we characterize SRI-38841, a xanthine analog arising from hit-to-lead optimization of a WNV inhibitor we previously described. SRI-38841 has broad spectrum activity, showing efficacy against DENV2, ZIKV, and JEV as well as WNV. It targets the NS4B protein, and and the resistance landscape converges with that of the aforementioned DENV inhibitors. Finally, docking of SRI-38841 against an AlphaFold2 model predicted a binding pocket within a cytosolic-facing funnel. These results favor the NMR/computational topology model over one placing these residues in the ER lumen — a distinction with direct consequences for how this inhibitor class is understood and optimized.

Question and background
Dengue viruses 1–4 and dengue disease remain a substantial global health burden, and no broadly effective direct-acting antivirals are available. The viral nonstructural protein NS4B has emerged as one of the most promising targets in this space, but the inhibitors described to date, including the clinical-stage compounds JNJ-A07/JNJ-1802 and NITD-688, are largely DENV-specific.

苏丹持续冲突中的多州 登革热 暴发:描述性流行病学分析

Multistate Dengue Fever Outbreak in Sudan Amid Ongoing Conflict: A Descriptive Epidemiological Analysis
作者： Ayman Azhary, Nooh Mohamed Hajhamed, Mohammed Elfatih Hamida, Abdualmoniem O. Musa, Claude Mambo Muvunyi, Mawahib A. Ahmed, Emmanuel Edwar Siddig
摘要中文翻译本研究是对苏丹多州 登革热 暴发 在激烈武装冲突和大规模流离失所期间的描述性流行病学分析。本研究使用 2024 年 8 月至 2025 年 7 月期间收集的报告对国家 监测 数据进行分析。使用 R 软件 4.2.2(版本 2025.05.1 + 513)计算和分析变量,包括每个州的病例数、死亡人数和国内流离失所者 (IDP)。10个州总共报告了13,371例病例和21人死亡。值得注意的是,受冲突影响的地区喀土穆州记录的病例数最多(n = 8040),而卡萨拉报告的死亡人数最多(n = 9)。卡萨拉州记录的国内流离失所者登革热病例数最多(472 例),其次是喀土穆州(69 例)。研究结果强调,需要在受冲突影响的环境中加强登革热 监测 和 媒介 的控制,在这些地区,卫生服务的中断可能会使 监测 的预防和应对变得复杂。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在分析苏丹在激烈武装冲突和大规模流离失所期间的多州 登革热 暴发,强调在这种情况下 暴发 管理面临的挑战。
设计与对象
该研究对 2024 年 8 月至 2025 年 7 月期间收集的全国 监测 数据进行了描述性流行病学分析,重点关注受 监测 影响的 10 个州。
核心方法
使用 R 软件版本 4.2.2 计算和分析每个州的病例数、死亡人数和境内流离失所者 (IDP) 等变量。
主要结果
10 个州共报告了 13,371 例病例和 21 例死亡,其中喀土穆州记录的病例数最多(n = 8040),卡萨拉报告的死亡人数最多(n = 9)。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,冲突和流离失所对登革热 监测 动态产生重大影响,强调需要在受影响地区采取有针对性的 监测 和控制措施。
科研与公卫意义
该研究提供了有关受冲突影响地区登革热流行病学模式的重要见解,为 暴发 预防和应对的公共卫生策略提供了信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于国家 监测 数据,这些数据可能不完整或存在报告偏差,并且由于分析的观察性质,证据是中等的。

Multistate Dengue Fever Outbreak in Sudan Amid Ongoing Conflict: A Descriptive Epidemiological Analysis

Authors: Ayman Azhary, Nooh Mohamed Hajhamed, Mohammed Elfatih Hamida, Abdualmoniem O. Musa, Claude Mambo Muvunyi, Mawahib A. Ahmed, Emmanuel Edwar Siddig
Original abstract (English)
This study is a descriptive epidemiological analysis of a multistate dengue fever outbreak in Sudan during a period of intense armed conflict and mass displacement. This study is an analysis of national surveillance data conducted using reports collected between August 2024 and July 2025. Variables, including case counts, fatalities, and internally displaced persons (IDPs) per state, were calculated and analyzed with R software 4.2.2 (Version 2025.05.1 + 513). A total of 13,371 cases and 21 fatalities were reported across 10 states. Notably, Khartoum State, a conflict-affected region, recorded the highest number of cases ( n = 8040), while Kassala reported the highest number of deaths ( n = 9). Kassala State documented the highest number of dengue cases among IDPs (472), followed by Khartoum (69). The findings highlight the need for strengthened dengue surveillance and vector control in conflict-affected settings, where disruption of health services may complicate outbreak prevention and response.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to analyze a multistate dengue fever outbreak in Sudan during a period of intense armed conflict and mass displacement, highlighting the challenges of outbreak management in such settings.
Design and population
The study is a descriptive epidemiological analysis of national surveillance data collected between August 2024 and July 2025, focusing on 10 states affected by the outbreak.
Core methods
Variables such as case counts, fatalities, and internally displaced persons (IDPs) per state were calculated and analyzed using R software version 4.2.2.
Main results
A total of 13,371 cases and 21 fatalities were reported across 10 states, with Khartoum State recording the highest number of cases (n = 8040) and Kassala reporting the highest number of deaths (n = 9).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate the significant impact of conflict and displacement on dengue outbreak dynamics, emphasizing the need for targeted surveillance and control measures in affected regions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides critical insights into the epidemiological patterns of dengue in conflict-affected areas, informing public health strategies for outbreak prevention and response.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on national surveillance data, which may be incomplete or subject to reporting biases, and the evidence is moderate due to the observational nature of the analysis.

患病率 患有急性未分化发热性疾病 (AUFI) 的儿童中疟疾、登革热和伤寒的情况登陆卡拉奇国家儿童健康研究所急诊室

Prevalence of malaria, dengue, and typhoid fever among children presenting with acute undifferentiated febrile illness (AUFI) landing at the emergency department of the National Institute of Child Health, Karachi
作者： Allah Rakhia, Muhammad Hayat Bozdar, Taj Muhammad Laghari, Mehboob Ali Noonari
摘要中文翻译目的:确定患有急性未分化发热性疾病的儿童中疟疾、登革热和伤寒的 患病率。方法:这项描述性横断面研究于 2022 年 9 月至 2023 年 2 月在卡拉奇国家儿童健康研究所急诊室进行,研究对象为 0.05 岁的儿童。结论:伤寒是急性未分化发热性疾病的三种病因中最常见的感染。然而,大多数病例仍未确诊,表明需要进行更广泛的诊断评估。关键词:急性未分化发热性疾病、儿童、登革热、疟疾、伤寒、抗菌素耐药性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决确定特定环境下急性未分化发热性疾病 (AUFI) 儿童最常见感染的科学和公共卫生问题,重点关注 登革病毒 和相关病原体。
设计与对象
2022 年 9 月至 2023 年 2 月在卡拉奇国家儿童健康研究所急诊室进行了一项描述性横断面研究,研究对象为 0.05 岁的儿童。
核心方法
该研究包括收集患有急性未分化发热性疾病的儿童的标本,并进行诊断测试以确定病原体,包括 登革病毒。
主要结果
伤寒是急性未分化发热性疾病的三种病因中最常见的感染,尽管大多数病例仍未确诊。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了对急性未分化发热性疾病进行更广泛的诊断评估的重要性,因为大多数病例未被识别为登革热或其他特定感染。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以为患有急性未分化发热性疾病的儿童的登革热和其他感染的改进诊断策略和 监测 提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其横断面设计和未确诊病例的可能性而受到限制,这可能会影响证据的强度。

Prevalence of malaria, dengue, and typhoid fever among children presenting with acute undifferentiated febrile illness (AUFI) landing at the emergency department of the National Institute of Child Health, Karachi

Authors: Allah Rakhia, Muhammad Hayat Bozdar, Taj Muhammad Laghari, Mehboob Ali Noonari
Original abstract (English)
Objective: To determine the prevalence of malaria, dengue and typhoid fever among children presenting with acute undifferentiated febrile illness. Method: The descriptive, cross-sectional study was conducted from September 2022 to February 2023 at the emergency department of the National Institute of Child Health, Karachi, and comprised children aged 0.05). Conclusion: Typhoid fever was the most frequently identified infection among the three causes of acute undifferentiated febrile illness. However, most cases remained undiagnosed, indicating the need for broader diagnostic evaluation. Key Words: Acute undifferentiated febrile illness, Children, Dengue, Malaria, Typhoid, Antimicrobial resistance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the scientific and public-health problem of identifying the most common infections among children with acute undifferentiated febrile illness (AUFI) in a specific setting, with a focus on dengue virus and related pathogens.
Design and population
A descriptive, cross-sectional study was conducted from September 2022 to February 2023 at the emergency department of the National Institute of Child Health, Karachi, involving children aged 0.05 years.
Core methods
The study involved collecting specimens from children presenting with acute undifferentiated febrile illness and performing diagnostic tests to identify the causative agents, including dengue virus.
Main results
Typhoid fever was the most frequently identified infection among the three causes of acute undifferentiated febrile illness, although most cases remained undiagnosed.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the importance of broader diagnostic evaluation for acute undifferentiated febrile illness, as most cases were not identified as dengue or other specific infections.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results can inform improved diagnostic strategies and surveillance for dengue and other infections in children with acute undifferentiated febrile illness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and the potential for undiagnosed cases, which may affect the strength of the evidence.

登革热流行病和医学实验室实验室人员的补充检查。

Prevalencia del dengue y exámenes complementarios en pacientes atendidos en el laboratorio Medic-lab.
作者： Yelisa Estefanía Durán-Pincay, Karen Michelle Loor-Moreno, Uinter Josue Cedeño-Arteaga, Yelisa Estefania Duran-Pincay, Yelisa Estefanía Durán Pincay, Uinter Josue Cedeño Arteaga
摘要中文翻译登革热是热带地区的一个公共卫生问题,其特点是高烧、全身症状,并有可能发展为严重形式。本研究通过 2022 年至 2024 年进行的观察性、描述性和回顾性研究,分析了在厄瓜多尔曼塔 MEDIC-LAB 临床实验室就诊的 320 名患者的登革热和相关实验室检测的 患病率。结果显示总体 患病率 为 27.5%,持续年率为 28%、27.1% 和 27.3%。分别。NS1和IgM阳性率为27.5%,IgG阳性率为26%,以女性为主,占阳性病例的16%。受影响最大的血液学参数是血小板(39.7%)、白细胞(21.5%)和平均红细胞体积(MCV)升高(28.4%),而肝酶 TGP 和 GGT 在 58% 的病例中升高,其中女性发生率更高,尽管这没有统计学意义(p > 0.05)。比例检验证实,观察到的 患病率 显着超过预期的 20%(Z=3.35;p<0.05)。结论是,研究人群中登革热 监测 的比例很高,血液系统受累的特点是血小板减少和肝功能障碍,这凸显了通过实验室检测进行早期 诊断 以进行适当的临床护理和流行病学 监测 的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估特定人群中登革热的 患病率 和相关实验室结果,强调登革热由于其高烧、全身症状和发展为严重形式的可能性而对热带地区的公共卫生意义。
设计与对象
该研究对 2022 年至 2024 年在厄瓜多尔曼塔 MEDIC-LAB 临床实验室就诊的 320 名患者进行了观察性、描述性和回顾性分析,重点关注登革热 患病率 和相关实验室检测。
核心方法
该研究使用包括 NS1 和 IgM 检测在内的实验室测试来检测登革热,并分析血液学和生化参数以确定患者的相关变化。
主要结果
登革热总体 患病率 为 27.5%,持续年增长率为 28%、27.1% 和 27.3%。NS1和IgM阳性率为27.5%,IgG阳性率为26%,阳性病例以女性为主。
解释与创新
观察到的 患病率 显着超过预期的 20%,表明研究人群中登革热负担显着,血液学受累的特点是血小板减少和肝功能障碍。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了通过实验室测试进行早期 诊断 在登革热流行地区进行适当临床护理和流行病学 监测 的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到回顾性设计和潜在选择偏倚的限制,但结果得到统计分析的支持(Z=3.35;p<0.05),为观察到的 患病率 和相关发现提供了中等可信度。

Prevalencia del dengue y exámenes complementarios en pacientes atendidos en el laboratorio Medic-lab.

Authors: Yelisa Estefanía Durán-Pincay, Karen Michelle Loor-Moreno, Uinter Josue Cedeño-Arteaga, Yelisa Estefania Duran-Pincay, Yelisa Estefanía Durán Pincay, Uinter Josue Cedeño Arteaga
Original abstract (English)
Dengue is a public health problem in tropical areas, characterized by high fever, systemic symptoms, and the potential to progress to severe forms. This study analyzed the prevalence of dengue and related laboratory tests in 320 patients seen at the MEDIC-LAB clinical laboratory in Manta, Ecuador, through an observational, descriptive, and retrospective study conducted from 2022 to 2024. The results showed an overall prevalence of 27.5%, with sustained annual rates of 28%, 27.1%, and 27.3%, respectively. The positivity rate for NS1 and IgM was 27.5%, while that for IgG was 26%, with females predominating, accounting for 16% of positive cases. The most affected hematological parameters were platelets (39.7%), white blood cells (21.5%), and elevated mean corpuscular volume (MCV) (28.4%), while the liver enzymes TGP and GGT were elevated in 58% of cases, with a higher frequency in women, although this was not statistically significant (p > 0.05). The proportion test confirmed that the observed prevalence significantly exceeds the expected 20% (Z=3.35; p<0.05). It is concluded that there is a high prevalence of dengue in the studied population, with hematological involvement characterized by thrombocytopenia and liver dysfunction, which highlights the importance of early diagnosis through laboratory tests for appropriate clinical care and epidemiological surveillance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to assess the prevalence of dengue and related laboratory findings in a specific population, highlighting the public health significance of dengue in tropical areas due to its high fever, systemic symptoms, and potential to progress to severe forms.
Design and population
The study conducted an observational, descriptive, and retrospective analysis of 320 patients seen at the MEDIC-LAB clinical laboratory in Manta, Ecuador, from 2022 to 2024, focusing on dengue prevalence and associated laboratory tests.
Core methods
The study used laboratory tests, including NS1 and IgM assays, to detect dengue, and analyzed hematological and biochemical parameters to identify associated changes in patients.
Main results
The overall prevalence of dengue was 27.5%, with sustained annual rates of 28%, 27.1%, and 27.3%. The positivity rate for NS1 and IgM was 27.5%, while that for IgG was 26%, with females predominating in positive cases.
Interpretation and novelty
The observed prevalence significantly exceeds the expected 20%, indicating a notable burden of dengue in the studied population, with hematological involvement characterized by thrombocytopenia and liver dysfunction.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings emphasize the importance of early diagnosis through laboratory tests for appropriate clinical care and epidemiological surveillance in dengue-endemic regions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for selection bias, but the results are supported by statistical analysis (Z=3.35; p<0.05), providing moderate confidence in the observed prevalence and associated findings.

美国南部短期气候变化及其与登革热 传播 的关联

Short-Term Climate Variability and Its Association with Dengue Transmission in the Southern United States
作者： Taylor Bowles, Gargi Deshpande, Katrin Gaardbo Kuhn, Toluwanimi M. Oni
摘要中文翻译登革热 在美国南部部分地区重新出现,引发了人们对短期气候变化可能如何影响 传播 风险的担忧。这项生态研究评估了 2020 年至 2024 年间年平均气温和年总降水量的变化是否与美国南部 7 个登革热病例的登革热 发病率 变化相关,数据来自 CDC ArboNET,气候数据来自 NOAA 监测系统。使用美国人口普查人口估计对 发病率 率进行标准化,并使用斯皮尔曼等级相关和纵向混合效应模型评估关联性。五年期间,所有研究州的年平均气温均有所上升,而降水模式则有所不同。登革热 发病率 在所有州均有所上升,其中佛罗里达州的增幅最为显着。温度变化与登革热 发病率 (rs = 0.54) 以及降水量变化与登革热 发病率 (rs = 0.71) 之间观察到中等相关性,但均未达到统计显着性。纵向混合效应模型发现温度与累积 发病率 和局部获得的 发病率 之间存在统计显着的正相关关系。这些发现表明,适度的短期气候变化可能与美国南部登革热动态的变化有关,强调需要扩大 监测 和长期分析,以更好地表征这些新出现的 媒介 传播的疾病风险。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 在美国南部部分地区重新出现,引发了人们对短期气候变化可能如何影响 传播 风险的担忧。
设计与对象
这项生态研究评估了 2020 年至 2024 年间年平均气温和年总降水量的变化是否与美国南部七个州登革热 发病率 的变化相关。
核心方法
登革热病例数据来自 CDC ArboNET,气候数据来自 NOAA 监测系统。使用斯皮尔曼等级相关和纵向混合效应模型评估关联性。
主要结果
五年期间,所有研究州的年平均气温均有所上升,而降水模式则有所不同。登革热 发病率 在所有州均有所上升,其中佛罗里达州的增幅最为显着。
解释与创新
温度变化与登革热 发病率 (rs = 0.54) 以及降水量变化与登革热 发病率 (rs = 0.71) 之间观察到中等相关性,但均未达到统计显着性。纵向混合效应模型发现温度与累积 发病率 和局部获得的 发病率 之间存在统计显着的正相关关系。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,适度的短期气候变化可能与美国南部登革热动态的变化有关,强调需要扩大 监测 和长期分析,以更好地表征这些新出现的 媒介 传播的疾病风险。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据没有报告该研究是否考虑了其他潜在的混杂因素,例如蚊子种群动态或人类行为变化。证据强度中等。

Short-Term Climate Variability and Its Association with Dengue Transmission in the Southern United States

Authors: Taylor Bowles, Gargi Deshpande, Katrin Gaardbo Kuhn, Toluwanimi M. Oni
Original abstract (English)
Dengue fever has re-emerged in parts of the Southern United States (U.S.), raising concerns about how short-term climate variability may influence transmission risk. This ecological study evaluates whether changes in mean annual temperature and total annual precipitation between 2020 and 2024 were associated with shifts in dengue incidence across seven Southern U.S. Dengue case data were obtained from CDC ArboNET, and climate data from NOAA monitoring systems. Incidence rates were standardized using U.S. Census population estimates, and associations were assessed using Spearman’s rank correlation and longitudinal mixed-effects models. All study states experienced increases in mean annual temperature over the five-year period, while precipitation patterns varied. Dengue incidence rose across all states, with Florida showing the most substantial increase. Moderate correlations were observed between temperature change and dengue incidence (rs = 0.54) and between precipitation change and dengue incidence (rs = 0.71), though neither reached statistical significance. Longitudinal mixed-effects models identified a statistically significant positive association between temperature and both cumulative incidence and locally acquired incidence. These findings suggest that modest short-term climate shifts may be associated with variations in dengue dynamics in the Southern U.S., underscoring the need for expanded surveillance and longer-term analyses to better characterize these emerging vector-borne disease risks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever has re-emerged in parts of the Southern United States (U.S.), raising concerns about how short-term climate variability may influence transmission risk.
Design and population
This ecological study evaluates whether changes in mean annual temperature and total annual precipitation between 2020 and 2024 were associated with shifts in dengue incidence across seven Southern U.S.
Core methods
Dengue case data were obtained from CDC ArboNET, and climate data from NOAA monitoring systems. Associations were assessed using Spearman’s rank correlation and longitudinal mixed-effects models.
Main results
All study states experienced increases in mean annual temperature over the five-year period, while precipitation patterns varied. Dengue incidence rose across all states, with Florida showing the most substantial increase.
Interpretation and novelty
Moderate correlations were observed between temperature change and dengue incidence (rs = 0.54) and between precipitation change and dengue incidence (rs = 0.71), though neither reached statistical significance. Longitudinal mixed-effects models identified a statistically significant positive association between temperature and both cumulative incidence and locally acquired incidence.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that modest short-term climate shifts may be associated with variations in dengue dynamics in the Southern U.S., underscoring the need for expanded surveillance and longer-term analyses to better characterize these emerging vector-borne disease risks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report whether the study accounted for other potential confounding factors such as mosquito population dynamics or human behavior changes. The evidence strength is moderate.

基于 NS1 的 ZIKV/DENV IgG ELISA 方法在登革热流行环境中进行血清学区分的评估

Evaluation of an NS1-Based ZIKV/DENV IgG ELISA approach for serological differentiation in a dengue-endemic setting
作者： Micaella C. Dato, Pichamon Sittikul, Pimolpachr Sriburin, Jittraporn Rattanamahaphoom, Pannamas Maneekan, Kulachart Jangpatarapongsa, Supawat Chatchen, Pornsawan Leaungwutiwong, Pimolpachr Sribu
摘要中文翻译摘要 寨卡病毒(ZIKV)是一种蚊媒黄病毒,最近已成为全球健康威胁。ZIKV的出现增加了出生时患有小头畸形或其他神经系统综合征的新生儿的发病率。大多数 ZIKV 感染都是轻微的或无症状的;然而,临床诊断是不准确的。此外,登革病毒 与 Zika 抗体发生交叉反应,这给 ZIKV 感染的血清学 诊断 带来了问题。由于这种现象,寨卡病毒血清学检测在登革热流行地区常常表现不佳。在本研究中,我们建立了寨卡/登革热 ELISA 测试,以改善寨卡和登革热样本之间的差异 诊断。使用 ZNS1 和 DNS1 间接 ELISA 以及商业 IgG ELISA 试剂盒测试了 60 个寨卡阳性样本和 120 个对照(20 个初级登革热样本、80 个二级登革热样本和 20 个健康血清样本)。测试了不同的寨卡抗原(EDIII、MR766 NS1 和 SV0127 NS1)并比较了 ROC 曲线。在测试的抗原中,NS1 产生了最佳的诊断潜力,AUC 范围为0.84-0.88,而 EDIII 的 AUC 为 0.77-0.82。在其他测试参数中,Zika/登革热 OD 比值也表现出最佳的敏感性 (Sn) 和特异性 (Sp)(分别为 58.3% 和 79.2%)(Sn = 26.7%–28.3% 和 Sp = 59.2%–79.3%)。与检测寨卡病毒感染的商业 ELISA 检测试剂盒相比,其 Sn 和 Sp 值分别仅为 26.7% 和 74.2%,两种简单 ELISA 的组合可用于 ZIKV 血清调查并监测妊娠期间的 ZIKV 感染,以了解 流行病学、发病机制 和 ZIKV 并发症。在 DENV 流行地区。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决登革热流行地区准确区分寨卡病毒和登革热感染的血清学需求,因为两种病毒之间的交叉反应使 诊断 变得复杂。
设计与对象
该研究使用 60 名寨卡病毒阳性个体和 120 名对照者的样本(包括原发性和继发性登革热病例以及健康血清样本)评估了一种基于 NS1 的新型 ELISA 测试,用于寨卡/登革热血清学区分。
核心方法
研究人员在 ZNS1 和 DNS1 间接 ELISA 中测试了不同的寨卡抗原(EDIII、MR766 NS1 和 SV0127 NS1),并使用 ROC 曲线和敏感性/特异性分析比较了它们的诊断潜力。
主要结果
在测试的抗原中,NS1 的诊断潜力最高,AUC 范围为 0.84-0.88,优于 EDIII (AUC 0.77-0.82)。与商业试剂盒(Sn 26.7%,Sp 74.2%)相比,寨卡/登革热 OD 比值还显示出更好的灵敏度(58.3%)和特异性(79.2%)。
解释与创新
研究表明,基于 NS1 的 ELISA 检测在检测寨卡病毒感染方面具有卓越的诊断性能,为登革热流行地区的血清学鉴别提供了更准确的方法。
科研与公卫意义
改进的诊断工具可以帮助 监测、诊断 和妊娠期间 ZIKV 感染的监测,有助于更好地了解登革热流行地区的 ZIKV 流行病学 和 发病机制。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括样本量小以及结果仅针对测试的抗原和人群,从而限制了普遍性。证据强度中等。

Evaluation of an NS1-Based ZIKV/DENV IgG ELISA approach for serological differentiation in a dengue-endemic setting

Authors: Micaella C. Dato, Pichamon Sittikul, Pimolpachr Sriburin, Jittraporn Rattanamahaphoom, Pannamas Maneekan, Kulachart Jangpatarapongsa, Supawat Chatchen, Pornsawan Leaungwutiwong, Pimolpachr Sribu
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Zika virus (ZIKV) is a mosquito-borne flavivirus that has recently emerged as a global health threat. The emergence of ZIKV has increased the incidence of neonates born with microcephaly or other neurological syndromes. The majority of ZIKV infections are mild or asymptomatic; however, clinical diagnosis is inaccurate. Moreover, Dengue virus cross-reacts with Zika antibodies, which creates problems for the serological diagnosis of ZIKV infections. Zika serological assays are often performed dismally in dengue-endemic areas because of this phenomenon. In this study, we established a Zika/Dengue ELISA Test to improve the differential diagnosis between Zika and Dengue samples. Sixty Zika-positive samples and 120 controls (20 Primary Dengue samples, 80 Secondary Dengue samples, and 20 healthy serum samples were tested using a ZNS1 and DNS1 Indirect ELISA and a commercial IgG ELISA Kit. Different Zika antigens (EDIII, MR766 NS1 and SV0127 NS1) were tested and ROC curves were compared. Among the antigens tested, NS1 yielded the best diagnostic potential with an AUC range of 0.84–0.88, compared with an AUC of 0.77–0.82 for EDIII. The Zika/Dengue OD Ratio also exhibited the best sensitivity (Sn) and specificity (Sp) (58.3% and 79.2% respectively) among the other parameters tested (Sn = 26.7%–28.3% and Sp = 59.2%–79.3%). It also performed better than the commercial kit, which yielded Sn and Sp values of only 26.7% and 74.2%, respectively. The Zika/Dengue OD Ratio has diagnostic potential and better performance compared with commercial ELISA test kits for detecting Zika infections. The combination of two simple ELISAs may be applied for ZIKV serosurveys and to monitor ZIKV infection during pregnancy to understand the epidemiology, pathogenesis, and complications of ZIKV in DENV-endemic areas.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the need for accurate serological differentiation between Zika and Dengue infections in dengue-endemic areas, where cross-reactivity between the two viruses complicates diagnosis.
Design and population
The study evaluated a novel NS1-based ELISA test for Zika/Dengue serological differentiation using samples from 60 Zika-positive individuals and 120 controls, including primary and secondary Dengue cases and healthy serum samples.
Core methods
The researchers tested different Zika antigens (EDIII, MR766 NS1, and SV0127 NS1) in a ZNS1 and DNS1 Indirect ELISA and compared their diagnostic potential using ROC curves and sensitivity/specificity analyses.
Main results
Among the tested antigens, NS1 yielded the highest diagnostic potential with an AUC range of 0.84–0.88, outperforming EDIII (AUC 0.77–0.82). The Zika/Dengue OD Ratio also showed better sensitivity (58.3%) and specificity (79.2%) compared to a commercial kit (Sn 26.7%, Sp 74.2%).
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrated that the NS1-based ELISA test has superior diagnostic performance for detecting Zika infections, providing a more accurate method for serological differentiation in dengue-endemic areas.
Scientific and public-health significance
The improved diagnostic tool can aid in surveillance, diagnosis, and monitoring of ZIKV infection during pregnancy, contributing to a better understanding of ZIKV epidemiology and pathogenesis in dengue-endemic regions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the small sample size and the fact that the results are specific to the tested antigens and populations, limiting generalizability. The evidence strength is moderate.

实施喷雾作为登革出血热(DFH)预防策略以支持家庭经济稳定

Implementation of Fogging as a Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever (DFH) Prevention Strategy to Support Family Economic Stability

Implementation of Fogging as a Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever (DFH) Prevention Strategy to Support Family Economic Stability

Authors: Dwi Aprilita, Elly Ingkiriwang, Ryani Dhyan Parashakti, Diana Frederica, Ibnu Haris Nasution, Daniel Widjaja, Elsye Yusnengsih, Theofilus Faot
Original abstract (English)
Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever (DHF) remains one of the major infectious diseases posing a serious public health threat in Indonesia. The disease is transmitted through the bites of infected Aedes aegypti mosquitoes carrying the dengue virus. Early preventive measures are essential to reduce DHF transmission, one of which is fogging as a vector control intervention implemented in 55 households in RT 005 RW 010, Warakas Urban Village. Such preventive efforts play an important role in maintaining family economic stability by reducing the risk of illness, healthcare expenditures, and productivity losses. The collaborative commitment of three higher education institutions reflects their social responsibility toward local communities through community service initiatives. This collaboration enhanced public awareness, promote the adoption of healthy lifestyle practices, strengthened community participation in dengue prevention. The program achieved full participation, with all 55 households actively supporting dengue prevention effort, thereby contributing to improved community health and the long-term economic resilience of families.

登革热 疫苗 暂停:澄清安全信号,同时增强黄病毒 疫苗 信心

Dengue vaccine suspension: clarifying safety signals while strengthening flavivirus vaccine confidence
作者： Maurício Lacerda Nogueira, André Machado Siqueira
摘要中文翻译巴西在疑似严重不良事件后暂时中止布坦坦登革热 疫苗 的上市,既不应被解释为登革热 疫苗接种 的失败,也不应被解释为对该产品安全性的明确判断。1 及时提醒,药物警戒是 疫苗 实施的积极组成部分,特别是对于在具有异质基线免疫、改变血清型的人群中使用的减毒活黄病毒 疫苗循环,以及之前接触过相关病毒。安全信号不是因果关系。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
巴西在疑似严重不良事件后暂时暂停布坦坦登革热疫苗的上市,既不应被解释为登革热疫苗接种的失败,也不应被解释为对该产品安全性的明确判断。这是一个及时的提醒,药物警戒是 疫苗 实施的一个积极组成部分,特别是对于在具有异质基线免疫、血清型循环变化和既往接触过相关病毒的人群中使用的减毒黄病毒 疫苗 而言。
设计与对象
该研究重点关注布坦坦登革热 疫苗 的安全信号及其对具有异质基线免疫、血清型循环变化和既往接触过相关病毒的人群中实施 疫苗 的影响。
核心方法
该研究没有提供分析安全信号的详细方法或程序,但强调了药物警戒在 疫苗 实施中的重要性。
主要结果
研究报告称,巴西暂时停止使用布坦坦登革热疫苗是由于可疑的安全信号,但没有提供具体的数值结果或统计数据。
解释与创新
作者将暂停解释为提醒人们药物警戒在 疫苗 实施中的重要性,特别是对于复杂免疫环境中的减毒活黄病毒 疫苗。这一解释强调了对 疫苗 安全性进行持续监控和背景理解的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明药物警戒在管理 疫苗 安全性方面的重要性,特别是对于具有不同免疫状态和血清型动态的人群中的黄病毒 疫苗。
局限与证据强度
该研究没有提供详细的方法或统计分析,证据是基于安全信号而不是明确的因果关系,这限制了结论的强度。

Dengue vaccine suspension: clarifying safety signals while strengthening flavivirus vaccine confidence

Authors: Maurício Lacerda Nogueira, André Machado Siqueira
Original abstract (English)
Brazil's temporary suspension of the Butantan dengue vaccine after suspected serious adverse events should be interpreted neither as a failure of dengue vaccination nor as a definitive judgement on the product's safety.1 It is a timely reminder that pharmacovigilance is an active component of vaccine implementation, especially for live-attenuated flavivirus vaccines used in populations with heterogeneous baseline immunity, shifting serotype circulation, and previous exposure to related viruses. A safety signal is not causality.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Brazil's temporary suspension of the Butantan dengue vaccine after suspected serious adverse events should be interpreted neither as a failure of dengue vaccination nor as a definitive judgement on the product's safety. It is a timely reminder that pharmacovigilance is an active component of vaccine implementation, especially for live-attenuated flavivirus vaccines used in populations with heterogeneous baseline immunity, shifting serotype circulation, and previous exposure to related viruses.
Design and population
The study focuses on the safety signal of the Butantan dengue vaccine and its implications for vaccine implementation in populations with heterogeneous baseline immunity, shifting serotype circulation, and previous exposure to related viruses.
Core methods
The study does not provide detailed methods or procedures for analyzing the safety signal, but it emphasizes the importance of pharmacovigilance in vaccine implementation.
Main results
The study reports that Brazil's temporary suspension of the Butantan dengue vaccine was due to a suspected safety signal, but it does not provide specific numerical results or statistical data.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the suspension as a reminder of the importance of pharmacovigilance in vaccine implementation, particularly for live-attenuated flavivirus vaccines in complex immune environments. This interpretation highlights the need for ongoing monitoring and contextual understanding of vaccine safety.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the importance of pharmacovigilance in managing vaccine safety, especially for flavivirus vaccines in populations with varying immune statuses and serotype dynamics.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study does not provide detailed methods or statistical analyses, and the evidence is based on a safety signal rather than definitive causality, which limits the strength of the conclusions.

登革热 在接受化疗的儿科肿瘤患者中的应用:来自三级护理中心的回顾性研究

Dengue fever in paediatric oncology patients undergoing chemotherapy: a retrospective study from a tertiary care center
作者： Wajiha Aijaz, Ammara Hameed, Ayza Farooq, Syed Habib Ahmed, Uzma Imam
摘要中文翻译目的:评估确诊为 登革热 的接受化疗的儿科肿瘤患者的临床表现、并发症和结局。方法:这项回顾性研究是在卡拉奇国家儿童健康研究所进行的,包含 2022 年 10 月 1 日至 2024 年 2 月 28 日期间的数据,涉及确诊为 登革热 的 1-12 岁儿科肿瘤患者以及正在接受活动性 登革热 治疗的血液或实体瘤恶性肿瘤患者。医疗记录中的数据被分类为人口统计、临床表现和并发症。采用SPSS 26对数据进行分析。结果:11例中位年龄5.5岁(范围:3-11岁)、四分位距(IQR)4岁(IQR:4-8)的患者中,男孩6例(54.5%),女孩5例(46.5%)。急性淋巴细胞白血病是最常见的恶性肿瘤7(63.7%)。登革热 是 10 例(90.9%)患者中的 诊断,而 1 例(9.1%)患有登革出血热。常见症状为发热11例(100%)、呕吐7例(72.7%)、头痛6例(54.5%)。7例(72.7%)出现血小板减少,2例(18.2%)出现胸腔积液,3例(27.3%)出现肺炎。所有11名患者(100%)均得到成功救治,无死亡病例。结论:尽管由于特征重叠,区分登革热和发热性中性粒细胞减少症在临床上仍然具有挑战性,但早期的 诊断 和支持治疗带来了良好的结果。关键词:登革热、小儿肿瘤学、化疗、回顾性研究、发热性中性粒细胞减少症、血液肿瘤、巴基斯坦。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估接受确诊为 登革热 的化疗的儿科肿瘤患者的临床表现、并发症和结果。登革热 带来了重大的公共卫生挑战,特别是在 登革热 疾病高发和同时存在医疗保健需求的地区。
设计与对象
卡拉奇国家儿童健康研究所于 2022 年 10 月 1 日至 2024 年 2 月 28 日期间进行了一项回顾性研究,研究对象为 1-12 岁、确诊为 登革热 的儿童肿瘤患者以及正在接受活动性 登革热 治疗的血液系统或实体瘤恶性肿瘤患者。
核心方法
医疗记录的数据分为人口统计、临床表现和并发症。数据采用SPSS 26进行统计处理。
主要结果
11例患者中位年龄为5.5岁(范围:3-11岁),四分位距(IQR)为4岁(IQR:4-8),其中男孩6例(54.5%),女孩5例(46.5%)。急性淋巴细胞白血病是最常见的恶性肿瘤,占7例(63.7%)。登革热 是 10 例(90.9%)患者中的 诊断,而 1 例(9.1%)患有登革出血热。
解释与创新
作者解释说,尽管由于特征重叠,区分登革热和发热性中性粒细胞减少症在临床上仍然具有挑战性,但早期的 诊断 和支持治疗带来了良好的结果。这项研究为特定弱势群体中 登革热 的临床表现提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为正在接受化疗的儿科肿瘤患者开发靶向 监测 和 登革热 的诊断策略提供信息。他们强调了在此背景下精确微分 诊断 的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的回忆偏差而受到限制。然而,证据是强有力的,因为它是基于确认的 登革热 诊断和来自三级护理中心的详细临床数据。

Dengue fever in paediatric oncology patients undergoing chemotherapy: a retrospective study from a tertiary care center

Authors: Wajiha Aijaz, Ammara Hameed, Ayza Farooq, Syed Habib Ahmed, Uzma Imam
Original abstract (English)
Objective: To evaluate the clinical presentation, complications and outcomes of paediatric oncology patients undergoing chemotherapy with confirmed dengue fever. Method: The retrospective study was conducted at the National Institute of Child Health, Karachi, and comprised data from October 1, 2022, to February 28, 2024, related to paediatric oncology patients aged 1-12 years with confirmed dengue fever and haematological or solid tumour malignancies who were on active treatment. Data from medical records was categorised as demographics, clinical presentation and complications. Data was analyzed using SPSS 26. Results: Of the 11 patients with a median age of 5.5 years (range: 3-11 years) and an interquartile range (IQR) 4 years (IQR: 4-8), 6(54.5%) were boys and 5(46.5%) were girls. Acute lymphoblastic leukaemia was the most common malignancy 7(63.7%). Dengue fever was the diagnosis in 10(90.9%) patients, while 1(9.1%) had dengue haemorrhagic fever. Common symptoms included fever 11(100%) patients, vomiting 7(72.7%) and headache 6(54.5%). Thrombocytopenia was noted in 7(72.7%) patients, pleural effusion in 2(18.2%) and pneumonia in 3(27.3%). All 11(100%) patients were treated successfully with no fatalities. Conclusion: Early diagnosis and supportive care led to favourable outcomes even though distinguishing dengue from febrile neutropenia remains clinically challenging due to overlapping features. Key Words: Dengue, Paediatric oncology, Chemotherapy, Retrospective studies, Febrile neutropenia, Haematological neoplasms, Pakistan.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the clinical presentation, complications and outcomes of paediatric oncology patients undergoing chemotherapy with confirmed dengue fever. Dengue fever poses a significant public health challenge, particularly in regions with high prevalence of the disease and concurrent healthcare needs.
Design and population
A retrospective study was conducted at the National Institute of Child Health, Karachi, from October 1, 2022, to February 28, 2024, involving paediatric oncology patients aged 1-12 years with confirmed dengue fever and haematological or solid tumour malignancies who were on active treatment.
Core methods
Data from medical records was categorised into demographics, clinical presentation and complications. The data was analyzed using SPSS 26 for statistical processing.
Main results
Of the 11 patients with a median age of 5.5 years (range: 3-11 years) and an interquartile range (IQR) of 4 years (IQR: 4-8), 6(54.5%) were boys and 5(46.5%) were girls. Acute lymphoblastic leukaemia was the most common malignancy, accounting for 7(63.7%) of the cases. Dengue fever was the diagnosis in 10(90.9%) patients, while 1(9.1%) had dengue haemorrhagic fever.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that early diagnosis and supportive care led to favourable outcomes, even though distinguishing dengue from febrile neutropenia remains clinically challenging due to overlapping features. This study provides novel insights into the clinical manifestations of dengue fever in a specific vulnerable population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of targeted surveillance and diagnostic strategies for dengue fever in paediatric oncology patients undergoing chemotherapy. They highlight the importance of accurate differential diagnosis in this context.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for recall bias. However, the evidence is strong as it is based on confirmed dengue fever diagnoses and detailed clinical data from a tertiary care center.

2023 年 暴发 期间台湾中部与登革热相关的临床和实验室特征。

Clinical and laboratory features associated with dengue in central Taiwan during the 2023 outbreak.
作者： Yu-Wen Huang, Yen-Pin Chen, Chien-Ching Hung, Wen-Chu Chiang, Chien-Hua Huang, Chien-Yu Chi, Matthew Huei-Ming Ma
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决在紧急情况下区分登革热与流感和 COVID-19 等其他病毒感染的挑战,特别是在以前未受影响的地区,这些地区的老龄化人口免疫学水平较低。
设计与对象
台大医院云林分院急诊科进行了一项回顾性 队列研究 研究,涉及台湾中部 2023 年 队列研究 期间患有急性疾病和疑似感染的 872 名成年人。
核心方法
在多重插补(m = 20)后,选择单变量分析中 p < 0.01 的变量用于多变量逻辑回归模型。亚组模型将登革热与实验室确诊的流感和 COVID-19 进行了比较,并对连续血象进行了描述性总结。
主要结果
Of 872 adults, 154 had confirmed dengue. Epidemic link (adjusted odds ratio [aOR], 9.59), age (aOR, 1.03 per year), myalgia or bone pain (aOR, 2.51), and hemoglobin (aOR, 1.35 per g/dL) were positively associated with dengue. Malignancy (aOR, 0.20), URI symptoms (aOR, 0.47), WBC count (aOR, 0.79 per 1 × 103/μL), and platelet count (aOR, 0.99 per 1 × 103/μL) were inversely associated. Epidemic link, the absence of URI symptoms, and lower platelet count remained associated with dengue in comparisons with influenza and COVID-19. WBC and platelet counts were lowest around Days 4-7.
解释与创新
作者解释说,流行病联系、没有 URI 症状和较低的血小板计数与登革热最一致,包括与流感和 COVID-19 进行比较,强调在临床应用之前需要进一步的外部验证。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为制定更准确的诊断标准提供信息,并改进急诊科在登革热 暴发 期间的分诊方案,特别是在将其与其他病毒感染区分开来方面。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的混杂因素而受到限制,但由于使用了多变量逻辑回归和多重插补,证据充足,支持适度的置信度评估。

Clinical and laboratory features associated with dengue in central Taiwan during the 2023 outbreak.

Authors: Yu-Wen Huang, Yen-Pin Chen, Chien-Ching Hung, Wen-Chu Chiang, Chien-Hua Huang, Chien-Yu Chi, Matthew Huei-Ming Ma
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Purpose: Dengue outbreaks in previously unaffected areas expose immunologically naive aging populations. Distinguishing dengue from influenza and COVID-19 in the emergency department (ED) is challenging. We identified factors associated with dengue in the 2023 outbreak in central Taiwan. METHODS: This retrospective cohort study included adults with acute illness and suspected infection who underwent dengue testing at the ED of National Taiwan University Hospital Yunlin Branch from January 1 to December 31, 2023. Patients with confirmed dengue (positive NS1 antigen test) were compared with non-dengue patients. Variables with p < 0.01 in univariate analysis were selected for a multivariable logistic regression model after multiple imputation (m = 20). Subgroup models compared dengue with laboratory-confirmed influenza and COVID-19. Serial hemograms were summarized descriptively. RESULTS: Of 872 adults, 154 had confirmed dengue. Epidemic link (adjusted odds ratio [aOR], 9.59), age (aOR, 1.03 per year), myalgia or bone pain (aOR, 2.51), and hemoglobin (aOR, 1.35 per g/dL) were positively associated with dengue. Malignancy (aOR, 0.20), URI symptoms (aOR, 0.47), WBC count (aOR, 0.79 per 1 × 103/μL), and platelet count (aOR, 0.99 per 1 × 103/μL) were inversely associated. Epidemic link, the absence of URI symptoms, and lower platelet count remained associated with dengue in comparisons with influenza and COVID-19. WBC and platelet counts were lowest around Days 4-7. CONCLUSION: Among adults tested for dengue during an outbreak, epidemic link, the absence of URI symptoms, and lower platelet count were most consistently associated with dengue, including in comparisons with influenza and COVID-19. External validation is required before clinical application.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of distinguishing dengue from other viral infections like influenza and COVID-19 in emergency settings, particularly in previously unaffected areas where aging populations are immunologically naive.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study was conducted at the ED of National Taiwan University Hospital Yunlin Branch, involving 872 adults with acute illness and suspected infection during the 2023 outbreak in central Taiwan.
Core methods
Variables with p < 0.01 in univariate analysis were selected for a multivariable logistic regression model after multiple imputation (m = 20). Subgroup models compared dengue with laboratory-confirmed influenza and COVID-19, and serial hemograms were summarized descriptively.
Main results
Of 872 adults, 154 had confirmed dengue. Epidemic link (adjusted odds ratio [aOR], 9.59), age (aOR, 1.03 per year), myalgia or bone pain (aOR, 2.51), and hemoglobin (aOR, 1.35 per g/dL) were positively associated with dengue. Malignancy (aOR, 0.20), URI symptoms (aOR, 0.47), WBC count (aOR, 0.79 per 1 × 103/μL), and platelet count (aOR, 0.99 per 1 × 103/μL) were inversely associated. Epidemic link, the absence of URI symptoms, and lower platelet count remained associated with dengue in comparisons with influenza and COVID-19. WBC and platelet counts were lowest around Days 4-7.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that epidemic link, absence of URI symptoms, and lower platelet count were most consistently associated with dengue, including in comparisons with influenza and COVID-19, highlighting the need for further external validation before clinical application.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings can inform the development of more accurate diagnostic criteria and improve triage protocols in emergency departments during dengue outbreaks, particularly in differentiating it from other viral infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for confounding factors, but the evidence is strong due to the use of multivariable logistic regression and multiple imputation, supporting a moderate confidence assessment.

探索登革热气候与社会经济驱动因素之间的关系 传播:协变量增强 SEIR-SEI 机制模型

Exploring the nexus of climate-socioeconomic drivers of dengue transmission: a covariate-augmented SEIR-SEI mechanistic model
作者： Adejimi Adesola Adeniji, Joel Miller, Abdun Naser Mahmood, Zhen He
摘要中文翻译开发了确定性协变量增强 SEIR-SEI 模型,用于深入了解气候和社会经济参数以及登革热 传播 动态之间的关系。该模型开发了一个包含两种外部影响的框架:气候和社会经济因素。该模型经过严格分析,研究了无病和地方性平衡,并使用哥伦比亚麦德林的相关数据进行了模拟。然后,我们采用机器学习算法作为经验特征选择步骤来识别登革热 发病率 的社会经济预测因素并对其进行排名,揭示失业人口是主要的社会经济驱动因素。这一发现促使选择失业率作为机械模型中的社会经济协变量。根据我们的数值模拟,我们仅使用温度并结合[温度+社会经济(失业)]参数进行计算,以确定它们的影响如何推动流行病的传播。我们的模型表明,社会经济影响可以与温度一起发挥驱动作用,增加 传播 率并导致更早的峰值。该框架适用于社会经济和气候因素是 传播 动态的主要驱动因素的流行环境。该方法展示了经验机器学习见解如何为机械流行病学模型中的协变量选择提供信息,从而加强对社会经济和气候条件如何相互作用以塑造流行病潜力的解释。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨气候和社会经济因素在驱动登革热 传播 动态方面的相互作用,满足更好地了解这些变量如何影响登革热传播的需要。
设计与对象
使用哥伦比亚麦德林相关数据开发并模拟了确定性协变量增强 SEIR-SEI 机制模型,以研究登革热的 传播 动力学。
核心方法
采用机器学习算法作为经验特征选择步骤来识别登革热 发病率 的社会经济预测因素并对其进行排名,其中失业成为主要的社会经济驱动因素。
主要结果
该模型显示,社会经济因素,特别是失业,可以与气温一起发挥驱动作用,增加 传播 率,并导致登革热传播高峰提前到来。
解释与创新
作者解释说,社会经济影响可以显着影响登革热 传播 动力学,为将经验见解整合到机制模型中提供了一个新颖的框架。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以通过强调社会经济因素在登革热 监测 中的重要性来为公共卫生策略提供信息,帮助有针对性的 监测 和干预工作。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于麦德林这一单一地点,并依赖于模拟数据,这可能会影响结果的普遍性。证据的强度中等。

Exploring the nexus of climate-socioeconomic drivers of dengue transmission: a covariate-augmented SEIR-SEI mechanistic model

Authors: Adejimi Adesola Adeniji, Joel Miller, Abdun Naser Mahmood, Zhen He
Original abstract (English)
A deterministic covariate-augmented SEIR-SEI model is developed and used to gain insight into the nexus between climate and socioeconomic parameters and the transmission dynamics of dengue. The model developed a framework that incorporates two external influences: climate and socioeconomic factors. The model is rigorously analyzed, with disease-free and endemic equilibria investigated, and is simulated using data relevant to Medellín, Colombia. We then employ machine learning algorithms as an empirical feature-selection step to identify and rank socioeconomic predictors of dengue incidence, revealing that the unemployed population is the dominant socioeconomic driver. This finding motivates the selection of unemployment as the socioeconomic covariate in the mechanistic model. From our numerical simulation, we computed using temperature only and combined [temperature + socioeconomic (unemployment)] parameters to determine how their influences drive the epidemic spread. Our model suggests that socioeconomic influence can act as a driver alongside temperature, increasing the transmission rate and leading to an earlier peak. The framework is appropriate for endemic settings where socioeconomic and climatic factors are the primary drivers of transmission dynamics. The approach demonstrates how empirical machine-learning insights can inform covariate selection in mechanistic epidemiological models, thereby strengthening interpretation of how socioeconomic and climatic conditions interact to shape epidemic potential.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to explore the interplay between climate and socioeconomic factors in driving dengue transmission dynamics, addressing the need to better understand how these variables influence the spread of dengue.
Design and population
A deterministic covariate-augmented SEIR-SEI mechanistic model was developed and simulated using data relevant to Medellín, Colombia, to investigate the transmission dynamics of dengue.
Core methods
Machine learning algorithms were employed as an empirical feature-selection step to identify and rank socioeconomic predictors of dengue incidence, with unemployment emerging as the dominant socioeconomic driver.
Main results
The model revealed that socioeconomic factors, particularly unemployment, can act as a driver alongside temperature, increasing the transmission rate and leading to an earlier peak in dengue spread.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that socioeconomic influence can significantly impact dengue transmission dynamics, providing a novel framework for integrating empirical insights into mechanistic models.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform public health strategies by highlighting the importance of socioeconomic factors in dengue transmission, aiding in targeted surveillance and intervention efforts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single location, Medellín, and relies on simulated data, which may affect the generalizability of the results. The evidence is moderate in strength.

登革热窦性心动过缓:患病率 和临床意义 - 一项观察性研究。

Sinus bradycardia in dengue: prevalence and clinical implications-an observational study.
作者： Marianna Zanette Molha, Maria Julia Barbosa Dos Santos, Ana Beatriz Sintoni, Beatriz Fernandes Lima, Beatriz Yukie Filosi Hasegawa, Cael Ostrovski Sauberlich, Erika Yinqi Wu, Fernanda Petrini Rodrigues das Neves, Isabela Ussifati Negrine, Omontayo Stephanie Honkpehedji
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在估计住院登革热成人患者窦性心动过缓的频率,并评估其与临床结果的关联。
设计与对象
巴西一家三级医院进行了一项前瞻性观察研究,连续招募了因登革热住院的成年人。
核心方法
该研究比较了患有和不患有窦性心动过缓(心率<60 bpm)的患者,并进行了探索性事后分析,以评估心动过缓发作与血小板恢复之间的相关性。
主要结果
Among 33 patients, 12 developed sinus bradycardia (36.4%; 95% CI, 20.4%-54.9%). Baseline characteristics were similar except for lethargy, which was less frequent with bradycardia (8.3% vs 52.4%; P=.022). ICU admission did not differ significantly (16.7% vs 28.6%; risk ratio, 0.58; 95% CI, 0.14-2.45; P=.678).
解释与创新
心动过缓发作与血小板恢复之间的相关性表明,窦性心动过缓可能发生在登革热的后期。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调需要进一步研究,以了解登革热窦性心动过缓的临床意义及其在疾病进展中的潜在作用。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到 队列研究 规模较小的限制,并且与血小板回收的相关性基于事后分析,可能无法建立因果关系。

Sinus bradycardia in dengue: prevalence and clinical implications-an observational study.

Authors: Marianna Zanette Molha, Maria Julia Barbosa Dos Santos, Ana Beatriz Sintoni, Beatriz Fernandes Lima, Beatriz Yukie Filosi Hasegawa, Cael Ostrovski Sauberlich, Erika Yinqi Wu, Fernanda Petrini Rodrigues das Neves, Isabela Ussifati Negrine, Omontayo Stephanie Honkpehedji
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: To estimate the frequency of sinus bradycardia in hospitalized adults with dengue, and assess its association with clinical outcomes. METHODS: This prospective observational study enrolled consecutive adults hospitalized with dengue at a tertiary hospital in Brazil. Patients with and without sinus bradycardia (heart rate <60 bpm) were compared. An exploratory post hoc analysis assessed the correlation between bradycardia onset and platelet recovery. RESULTS: Among 33 patients, 12 developed sinus bradycardia (36.4%; 95% CI, 20.4%-54.9%). Baseline characteristics were similar except for lethargy, which was less frequent with bradycardia (8.3% vs 52.4%; P=.022). ICU admission did not differ significantly (16.7% vs 28.6%; risk ratio, 0.58; 95% CI, 0.14-2.45; P=.678). In 11 patients with complete temporal data, bradycardia onset correlated positively with platelet recovery (Spearman's ρ=0.61; 95% CI, 0.00-0.92; P=.048). CONCLUSIONS: Sinus bradycardia was common in this small cohort, without a detected association with ICU admission. Its correlation with platelet recovery suggests possible occurrence during a later phase of dengue. Larger studies are needed.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to estimate the frequency of sinus bradycardia in hospitalized adults with dengue and assess its association with clinical outcomes.
Design and population
A prospective observational study was conducted at a tertiary hospital in Brazil, enrolling consecutive adults hospitalized with dengue.
Core methods
The study compared patients with and without sinus bradycardia (heart rate <60 bpm) and performed an exploratory post hoc analysis to assess the correlation between bradycardia onset and platelet recovery.
Main results
Among 33 patients, 12 developed sinus bradycardia (36.4%; 95% CI, 20.4%-54.9%). Baseline characteristics were similar except for lethargy, which was less frequent with bradycardia (8.3% vs 52.4%; P=.022). ICU admission did not differ significantly (16.7% vs 28.6%; risk ratio, 0.58; 95% CI, 0.14-2.45; P=.678).
Interpretation and novelty
The correlation between bradycardia onset and platelet recovery suggests that sinus bradycardia may occur during a later phase of dengue.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for further research to understand the clinical implications of sinus bradycardia in dengue and its potential role in disease progression.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited by its small cohort size, and the correlation with platelet recovery was based on post hoc analysis, which may not establish causality.

克服登革热漏报:基于电子病历的自动登革热通知的机器学习框架

Overcoming Dengue Underreporting: A Machine Learning Framework for Automated Dengue Notification Based on Electronic Medical Records
作者： Warodom Sornsurin, Worasait Suwannik, Panithee Thammawijaya, Till D. Frank, Lily Ingsrisawang
摘要中文翻译医院对登革热病例的通知经常被延迟,导致漏报。在泰国省级医院,瓶颈在于管理,而不是诊断:延迟出院编码和文书步骤意味着通知延迟提交或遗漏。现有的机器学习 (ML) 研究需要临床医生注释的特征,而被动电子病历 (EMR) 中缺乏这些特征。我们提出了一种双通道 EMR 框架,用于基于 ML 的 监测 报告预填充。我们分析了泰国素林医院(2019-2022 年)的 1031 例登革热疑似病例(508 例需通报)。途径 A 将 EMR 数据与手动记录的临床特征相结合;路径 B 仅使用被动 EMR 数据。我们使用 80/20 分层分割,通过 SHAP 引导的特征消除和 10 倍交叉验证,测试了 11 个特征集(77 种组合)的 7 种 ML 方法。在初次分割中,途径 A (CatBoost) 的 F1 得分为 96.62%、敏感性为 95.10%、特异性为 98.10%(10 粒种子平均 F1 为 95.7% ± 1.4%)。通路 B (LightGBM) 的 F1 评分为 84.42%,敏感性为 94.12%,特异性为 75.24%,在 10% 患病率 时预计阴性预测值为 99.1%;10 粒种子的表现较低(F1 78.5% ± 4.3%;灵敏度 89.1%)。探索性 LSTM-16 堆叠模型(F1 83.99%;精确度 79.49%)在途径 B 中的精确度和特异性方面表现最佳。在 77 个组合中,有 75 个组合中的 75 个组合中 10 个种子的测试 F1 SD ≤ 5.0%。在此 队列研究 中,被动 EMR 建模在初级分割上实现了 91-94% 的灵敏度(种子间为 85-89%),无需额外的数据收集,支持在资源有限的医院中基于 ML 的通知的可行性。这是否会减少登革热漏报,必须通过前瞻性、多中心研究来确定。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决由于医院通知延迟而导致登革热病例报告不足的问题,这是泰国省级医院的一个常见问题。
设计与对象
该研究分析了泰国素林医院(2019-2022 年)的 1031 例疑似登革热病例,重点关注被动电子病历。
核心方法
研究人员使用了具有两种途径的机器学习框架:途径 A 将 EMR 数据与手动记录的临床特征相结合,而途径 B 仅使用被动 EMR 数据。他们使用 SHAP 引导的特征消除和 10 倍交叉验证测试了 11 个特征集的 7 种 ML 方法。
主要结果
途径 A (CatBoost) 的 F1 评分为 96.62%、敏感性为 95.10%、特异性为 98.10%。通路 B (LightGBM) 的 F1 评分为 84.42%、敏感性为 94.12%、特异性为 75.24%。探索性 LSTM-16 堆叠模型在 Pathway B 中的精度和特异性方面表现最佳。
解释与创新
该研究提出了一种双路径 EMR 框架,用于基于 ML 的 监测 报告预填充,证明了被动 EMR 建模在资源有限的医院中的可行性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,基于机器学习的通知系统可以减少登革热漏报,支持此类系统在资源有限的环境中的可行性。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括使用单一医院以及需要进行前瞻性、多中心研究来确定对漏报的影响。

Overcoming Dengue Underreporting: A Machine Learning Framework for Automated Dengue Notification Based on Electronic Medical Records

Authors: Warodom Sornsurin, Worasait Suwannik, Panithee Thammawijaya, Till D. Frank, Lily Ingsrisawang
Original abstract (English)
Hospital notification of dengue cases is frequently delayed, contributing to underreporting. At Thai provincial hospitals the bottleneck is administrative, not diagnostic: delayed discharge coding and clerical steps mean notifications are submitted late or omitted. Existing machine learning (ML) studies require clinician-annotated features absent in passive electronic medical records (EMRs). We propose a dual-pathway EMR framework for ML-based pre-population of surveillance reports. We analyzed 1031 dengue-suspected encounters (508 notifiable) from Surin Hospital, Thailand (2019–2022). Pathway A combines EMR data with manually documented clinical features; Pathway B uses only passive EMR data. Using an 80/20 stratified split, we tested 7 ML methods across 11 feature sets (77 combinations) with SHAP-guided feature elimination and 10-fold cross-validation. On the primary split, Pathway A (CatBoost) achieved an F1 score of 96.62%, 95.10% sensitivity, and 98.10% specificity (10-seed mean F1 95.7% ± 1.4%). Pathway B (LightGBM) achieved an F1 score of 84.42%, 94.12% sensitivity, 75.24% specificity, and a projected negative predictive value of 99.1% at 10% prevalence; 10-seed performance was lower (F1 78.5% ± 4.3%; sensitivity 89.1%). An exploratory LSTM-16 stacking model (F1 83.99%; precision 79.49%) performed best in precision and specificity among Pathway B. Test F1 SD across 10 seeds was ≤5.0% in 75 of 77 combinations. Within this cohort, passive EMR modeling achieved 91–94% sensitivity on the primary split (85–89% across seeds) without additional data collection, supporting the feasibility of ML-based notification in resource-constrained hospitals. Whether this reduces dengue underreporting must be established in prospective, multi-center studies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the underreporting of dengue cases due to delayed hospital notifications, a common issue in Thai provincial hospitals.
Design and population
The study analyzed 1031 dengue-suspected encounters from Surin Hospital, Thailand (2019–2022), focusing on passive electronic medical records.
Core methods
The researchers used a machine learning framework with two pathways: Pathway A combined EMR data with manually documented clinical features, while Pathway B used only passive EMR data. They tested 7 ML methods across 11 feature sets using SHAP-guided feature elimination and 10-fold cross-validation.
Main results
Pathway A (CatBoost) achieved an F1 score of 96.62%, 95.10% sensitivity, and 98.10% specificity. Pathway B (LightGBM) achieved an F1 score of 84.42%, 94.12% sensitivity, and 75.24% specificity. An exploratory LSTM-16 stacking model performed best in precision and specificity among Pathway B.
Interpretation and novelty
The study proposed a dual-pathway EMR framework for ML-based pre-population of surveillance reports, demonstrating the feasibility of passive EMR modeling in resource-constrained hospitals.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that ML-based notification systems could reduce dengue underreporting, supporting the feasibility of such systems in resource-constrained settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the use of a single hospital and the need for prospective, multi-center studies to establish the impact on underreporting.

气候和免疫对巴西2024年史无前例的登革热暴发的作用

The role of climate and immunity in Brazil unprecedented outbreak of dengue in 2024
作者： Bachir Assao, Bernardo García‐Carreras, Mondal Hasan Zahid, Angkana T. Huang, Youngsuk Ko, Paloma M. Cárcamo, Luciano Andrade Moreira, Maurício Lacerda Nogueira, Mauro Martins Teixeira, Albert Icksang Ko
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study aimed to investigate the drivers of an unprecedented dengue outbreak in Brazil during the 2024 season, which saw almost 5.7 million total cases compared to under 1.3 million in 2023, highlighting the need to understand the role of climate and immunity in this surge.
设计与对象
研究人员将 2024 年的州级登革热 发病率 数据与 2000 年至 2023 年的数据进行了比较,以量化异常情况。27 个州中的 9 个州(主要位于东南部)记录了 25 年来最高的 发病率,占病例数的 97%,但仅占人口的 67%。
核心方法
该研究使用广义加性模型来分析有效繁殖数,并使用机械 SEIR-SEI 模型估计州级易感性,该模型描述了支持高 传播 率的高温与降低易感性之间的权衡。
主要结果
Brazil experienced a large dengue epidemic during the 2024 season, with almost 5.7 million total cases (compared to under 1.3 million in 2023). In contrast, nine states (mostly in the North and Northeast) reported below-average incidence.
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为表明磁化率损耗(而不是温度)是 2024 年 暴发 的关键因素,这表明免疫在调节温度对 发病率 的影响中发挥着重要作用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,未来 监测 整合免疫措施可以增强预测和预警系统,特别是当气候变化将登革热扩大到免疫学上的幼稚人群时。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据基于州级数据和相关性,可能无法捕捉当地的变化。由于依赖观察数据和潜在的混杂因素,研究结果的可信度为中等。

The role of climate and immunity in Brazil unprecedented outbreak of dengue in 2024

Authors: Bachir Assao, Bernardo García‐Carreras, Mondal Hasan Zahid, Angkana T. Huang, Youngsuk Ko, Paloma M. Cárcamo, Luciano Andrade Moreira, Maurício Lacerda Nogueira, Mauro Martins Teixeira, Albert Icksang Ko
Original abstract (English)
Brazil experienced a large dengue epidemic during the 2024 season, with almost 5.7 million total cases (compared to under 1.3 million in 2023), raising questions about underlying drivers, including rising temperatures, expansion into susceptible populations, changes in reporting, and novel strains. We compared state-level 2024 incidence with 2000 - 2023 data to quantify anomalies. Nine of 27 states, primarily in the Southeast, recorded their highest incidence in 25 years, accounting for 97% of cases while comprising only 67% of the population. In contrast, nine states (mostly in the North and Northeast) reported below-average incidence. Counterintuitively, the correlation between state-level temperature and incidence declined significantly over the study period, turning negative by 2024 (r = -0.72), a reversal from the positive correlations observed at the start. To determine whether this pattern was driven by northern temperatures being too high for transmission, or by spatial variation in immunity, we fit generalized additive models to effective reproductive numbers. Transmission remained substantial at the highest temperatures, while reproductive numbers declined with lagged cumulative incidences, indicating susceptibility depletion was important. We then estimated state-level susceptibility using a mechanistic SEIR-SEI model, describing a trade-off: high temperatures supported high transmission rates, but were associated with reduced susceptibility. Linear regression models confirmed both temperature and susceptibility were necessary to predict the 2024 outbreaks, suggesting that immunity plays an important role in modulating temperature's effect on incidence. Future surveillance integrating measures of immunity could enhance forecasts and early-warning systems, particularly as climate change expands dengue into immunologically naive populations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the drivers of an unprecedented dengue outbreak in Brazil during the 2024 season, which saw almost 5.7 million total cases compared to under 1.3 million in 2023, highlighting the need to understand the role of climate and immunity in this surge.
Design and population
The researchers compared state-level dengue incidence data from 2024 with data from 2000 to 2023 to quantify anomalies. Nine of 27 states, primarily in the Southeast, recorded their highest incidence in 25 years, accounting for 97% of cases while comprising only 67% of the population.
Core methods
The study used generalized additive models to analyze effective reproductive numbers and estimated state-level susceptibility using a mechanistic SEIR-SEI model, which described a trade-off between high temperatures supporting high transmission rates and reduced susceptibility.
Main results
Brazil experienced a large dengue epidemic during the 2024 season, with almost 5.7 million total cases (compared to under 1.3 million in 2023). In contrast, nine states (mostly in the North and Northeast) reported below-average incidence.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating that susceptibility depletion, rather than temperature, was a key factor in the 2024 outbreak, suggesting that immunity plays an important role in modulating temperature's effect on incidence.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that future surveillance integrating measures of immunity could enhance forecasts and early-warning systems, particularly as climate change expands dengue into immunologically naive populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is based on state-level data and correlations, which may not capture local variations. The confidence in the findings is moderate due to the reliance on observational data and the potential for confounding factors.

登革病毒在南方地区雌性埃及伊蚊种群中的种群组成、性别和传播类型分析(2022–2025)

Thành phần loài, giới tính và sự lưu hành các týp vi rút dengue trong quần thể muỗi aedes cái ở khu vực phía Nam, 2022–2025
作者： Huy La Hoàng, Thảo Lê Trọng Ly, Ngọc Phạm Thị Thúy, Quyên Đỗ Lệ, Khánh Lý Huỳnh Kim, Ly Lê Trọng Thảo
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在描述2022–2025年南方地区雌性埃及伊蚊种群中登革病毒的种群组成、性别和传播类型。对埃及伊蚊种群中登革病毒的监测有助于评估疾病风险。
设计与对象
一项横断面研究在南部20个省/市进行(2022–2025年)。共4.709个雌性埃及伊蚊样本被送去检测病毒,其中509个样本来自疫情热点地区,4.200个样本来自常规监测点。
核心方法
通过手持吸蚊器在疫情点和常规监测点采集埃及伊蚊,进行种类鉴定和实时逆转录聚合酶链反应(Real-time RT-PCR)检测。
主要结果
结果是 1,38% (7/509),而 0,14% (6/4.200)。Tỷ số chênh OR = 9,75(khoảng Tin cậy 95%:3,18–29,91;p < 0,001)。
解释与创新
本研究在检测样本中观察到DENV-1、DENV-2和DENV-4的传播。
科研与公卫意义
Kết quả nghiên cứu cho thấy xét nghiệm vi rút trên muỗi cái Ae。埃及伊蚊 có thể góp phần bổ sung thông Tin hữu ích cho công tác giám sát véc tơ và đánh giá nguy cơ sốt xuất huyết 登革热 tại khu vực菲亚南。
局限与证据强度
本研究为横断面研究,因此可能无法充分反映随时间变化的情况。然而,数据来自多个省/市,提供了对该地区情况的总体视角。

Thành phần loài, giới tính và sự lưu hành các týp vi rút dengue trong quần thể muỗi aedes cái ở khu vực phía Nam, 2022–2025

Authors: Huy La Hoàng, Thảo Lê Trọng Ly, Ngọc Phạm Thị Thúy, Quyên Đỗ Lệ, Khánh Lý Huỳnh Kim, Ly Lê Trọng Thảo
Original abstract (English)
Hoạt động giám sát vi rút Dengue trên quần thể muỗi Aedes góp phần đánh giá nguy cơ dịch bệnh. Tại Việt Nam, dữ liệu so sánh tỉ lệ muỗi nhiễm vi rút Dengue giữa điểm ổ dịch và điểm giám sát thường xuyên còn hạn chế. Nghiên cứu nhằm mô tả thành phần loài, giới tính và sự lưu hành các týp vi rút dengue trong quần thể muỗi Aedes aegypti cái ở khu vực phía Nam, 2022–2025. Nghiên cứu mô tả cắt ngang thực hiện tại 20 tỉnh/thành phía Nam (2022–2025). Muỗi Aedes được thu thập bằng máy hút cầm tay tại các ổ dịch và điểm giám sát thường xuyên, định loại và xét nghiệm bằng Real-time RT-PCR. Nghiên cứu đã thu thập 7.252 muỗi Aedes, loài Ae. aegypti chiếm ưu thế (>98%). Tổng cộng 4.709 mẫu muỗi cái Ae. aegypti được đưa vào xét nghiệm vi rút, gồm 509 mẫu tại điểm ổ dịch và 4.200 mẫu tại điểm giám sát thường xuyên. aegypti nhiễm vi rút tại điểm ổ dịch là 1,38% (7/509), với điểm giám sát thường xuyên (0,14%, 6/4.200) với tỷ số chênh OR = 9,75 (khoảng tin cậy 95%: 3,18–29,91; p < 0,001). Ghi nhận sự lưu hành của DENV-1, DENV-2 và DENV-4 trên các mẫu muỗi xét nghiệm. Kết quả nghiên cứu cho thấy xét nghiệm vi rút trên muỗi cái Ae. aegypti có thể góp phần bổ sung thông tin hữu ích cho công tác giám sát véc tơ và đánh giá nguy cơ sốt xuất huyết Dengue tại khu vực phía Nam.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Nghiên cứu được thực hiện để mô tả thành phần loài, giới tính và sự lưu hành các týp vi rút dengue trong quần thể muỗi Aedes aegypti cái ở khu vực phía Nam, 2022–2025. Hoạt động giám sát vi rút Dengue trên quần thể muỗi Aedes góp phần đánh giá nguy cơ dịch bệnh.
Design and population
Nghiên cứu mô tả cắt ngang thực hiện tại 20 tỉnh/thành phía Nam (2022–2025). Tổng cộng 4.709 mẫu muỗi cái Ae. aegypti được đưa vào xét nghiệm vi rút, gồm 509 mẫu tại điểm ổ dịch và 4.200 mẫu tại điểm giám sát thường xuyên.
Core methods
Muỗi Aedes được thu thập bằng máy hút cầm tay tại các ổ dịch và điểm giám sát thường xuyên, định loại và xét nghiệm bằng Real-time RT-PCR.
Main results
Tỷ lệ nhiễm vi rút tại điểm ổ dịch là 1,38% (7/509), với điểm giám sát thường xuyên là 0,14% (6/4.200). Tỷ số chênh OR = 9,75 (khoảng tin cậy 95%: 3,18–29,91; p < 0,001).
Interpretation and novelty
Nghiên cứu ghi nhận sự lưu hành của DENV-1, DENV-2 và DENV-4 trên các mẫu muỗi xét nghiệm.
Scientific and public-health significance
Kết quả nghiên cứu cho thấy xét nghiệm vi rút trên muỗi cái Ae. aegypti có thể góp phần bổ sung thông tin hữu ích cho công tác giám sát véc tơ và đánh giá nguy cơ sốt xuất huyết Dengue tại khu vực phía Nam.
Limitations and evidence strength
Nghiên cứu mô tả cắt ngang, do đó có thể không phản ánh đầy đủ sự biến đổi theo thời gian. Tuy nhiên, dữ liệu được thu thập từ nhiều tỉnh/thành phố, cung cấp một cái nhìn tổng quát về tình hình.

将紫外线和太阳辐射监测数据与 MMS-GRU 框架集成以进行多尺度登革热预报

Integrating Ultraviolet and Solar Radiation Monitoring Data with an MMS-GRU Framework for Multi-Scale Dengue Forecasting
作者： Mei-Ling Huang, Netnapha Chamnisampan, Yu-Fang Huang
摘要中文翻译登革热 继续在热带和亚热带地区扩展,给公共卫生 监测 系统带来压力,部分原因是登革热时间序列表现出非平稳性、多周期结构以及因地理位置而异的气候驱动因素。本研究提出了 MMS-GRU,这是一种混合多尺度框架,它使用最大重叠离散小波变换 (MODWT) 分解登革热和气候序列,将季节性趋势与短期动态分开,应用两阶段 Lasso-SHAP 程序来选择信息丰富的气候预测变量,并将每个频率分量输入堆叠双向门控循环单元 (SBi-GRU) 以捕获时间依赖性。该框架在三个流行地点(伊基托斯、圣胡安和菲律宾)进行了为期 13、26 和 52 周的评估,使用传统气候记录,对于菲律宾,则使用卫星衍生的 NASA POWER 变量。MODWT 在所有三个地点产生的误差均低于 EEMD 和 CEEMDAN,而特征选择的好处因地点、地平线和建筑而异,紫外线指数和太阳辐射变量位列对菲律宾最有影响力的输入之一。这些发现表明,将多尺度分解与可解释的特征选择相结合是登革热预测的一个有前途的基础,尽管在框架支持 媒介 控制规划之前需要在通用协议下重复运行并与简单基准进行比较。

问题与背景
提供的证据没有明确说明研究问题或其基本原理。

本研究提出了 MMS-GRU,这是一种混合多尺度框架,它使用最大重叠离散小波变换 (MODWT) 分解登革热和气候序列,将季节性趋势与短期动态分开,应用两阶段 Lasso-SHAP 程序来选择信息丰富的气候预测变量,并将每个频率分量输入堆叠双向门控循环单元 (SBi-GRU) 以捕获时间依赖性。
核心方法
该框架在三个流行地点(伊基托斯、圣胡安和菲律宾)进行了为期 13、26 和 52 周的评估,使用传统气候记录,对于菲律宾,则使用卫星衍生的 NASA POWER 变量。
主要结果
提供的证据没有明确说明直接观察到的主要结果。

这些发现表明,将多尺度分解与可解释的特征选择相结合是登革热预测的一个有前途的基础,尽管在框架支持 媒介 控制规划之前需要在通用协议下重复运行并与简单基准进行比较。
科研与公卫意义
登革热 继续在热带和亚热带地区扩展,给公共卫生 监测 系统带来压力,部分原因是登革热时间序列表现出非平稳性、多周期结构以及因地理位置而异的气候驱动因素。
局限与证据强度
MODWT 在所有三个地点产生的误差均低于 EEMD 和 CEEMDAN,而特征选择的好处因地点、地平线和建筑而异,紫外线指数和太阳辐射变量位列对菲律宾最有影响力的输入之一。

Integrating Ultraviolet and Solar Radiation Monitoring Data with an MMS-GRU Framework for Multi-Scale Dengue Forecasting

Authors: Mei-Ling Huang, Netnapha Chamnisampan, Yu-Fang Huang
Original abstract (English)
Dengue fever continues to expand across tropical and subtropical regions, straining public health surveillance systems, partly because dengue time series exhibit non-stationarity, multi-periodic structure, and climatic drivers that vary geographically. This study proposes MMS-GRU, a hybrid multi-scale framework that decomposes dengue and climate series using the Maximal Overlap Discrete Wavelet Transform (MODWT) to separate seasonal trends from short-term dynamics, applies a two-stage Lasso–SHAP procedure to select informative climate predictors, and feeds each frequency component into a Stacked Bidirectional Gated Recurrent Unit (SBi-GRU) to capture temporal dependencies. The framework was evaluated at three endemic sites (Iquitos, San Juan, and the Philippines) over 13-, 26-, and 52-week horizons, using conventional climate records and, for the Philippines, satellite-derived NASA POWER variables. MODWT produced lower errors than EEMD and CEEMDAN at all three sites, whereas the benefit of feature selection varied with site, horizon, and architecture, and UV index and solar radiation variables ranked among the most influential inputs for the Philippines. These findings indicate that combining multi-scale decomposition with interpretable feature selection is a promising basis for dengue forecasting, although repeated runs and comparison with simple benchmarks under a common protocol are needed before the framework can support vector control planning.
