气候-社会经济影响下登革热的混合流行病学-神经模型

Hybrid Epidemiological–Neural Modelling of Dengue Under Climate-Socioeconomic Influence
作者： Adejimi Adeniji, Abdun Mahmood, Joel Miller, Zhen He
摘要中文翻译气候敏感和社会经济异质环境中的登革热 传播 源于环境适宜性、蚊子生态和人类行为之间的非线性相互作用。经典的 SEIR 型模型提供了机械解释性,但常常受到观测噪声和不完整数据的限制,而纯粹的数据驱动方法通常无法尊重潜在的 传播 动态。在这项研究中,我们引入了一种混合流行病学框架,该框架将温度-失业驱动的 SEIR-SEI 模型嵌入到物理信息神经网络 (SEIR-PINN) 中。使用哥伦比亚麦德林的每周登革热 发病率 数据,我们将失业率作为社会经济指标,与气候适宜性一起调节 传播。模型性能通过两种方式进行评估;根据观测到的 发病率 的预测,SEIR-PINN 的平均绝对误差 (MAE) 为 15.05,R2 为 0.975,而在相同的气候和社会经济假设下拟合的机械 SEIR-SEI 基线的 MAE 为 43.57,R2 为 0.870。改组五倍交叉验证提供了 2009-2011 年期间观察到的 发病率 的预测,平均 MAE 为 14.87,平均 R2 为 0.977(折叠外 MAE 14.89,R2 0.978)。这些保留的结果插值在观察到的流行病中,并不是对前瞻性预测性能的评估。重要的是,SEIR-PINN 重建了潜在的人类和蚊子区室,这些区室在整个流行病阶段保持生物学一致。通过将机械结构与数据自适应灵活性相融合,所提出的 SEIR-PINN 提供了一个可解释的框架,用于分析城市流行环境中登革热 传播 的联合气候和社会经济调节。尽管使用麦德林作为单城市方法学案例研究,但将此框架应用于其他登革热流行环境需要使用其他地点、更长的时间段和独立数据集进行外部验证。因此,结果支持物理信息混合模型作为登革热 监测 和 传播 动力学研究的可解释工具的潜在用途,同时认识到需要在更长的时间段和更多地理区域进行进一步的外部验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
气候敏感和社会经济异质环境中的登革热 传播 源于环境适宜性、蚊子生态和人类行为之间的非线性相互作用。
设计与对象
经典的 SEIR 型模型提供了机械解释性,但常常受到观测噪声和不完整数据的限制,而纯粹的数据驱动方法通常无法尊重潜在的 传播 动态。
核心方法
在这项研究中,我们引入了一种混合流行病学框架,该框架将温度-失业驱动的 SEIR-SEI 模型嵌入到物理信息神经网络 (SEIR-PINN) 中。
主要结果
模型性能通过两种方式进行评估;根据观测到的 发病率 的预测,SEIR-PINN 的平均绝对误差 (MAE) 为 15.05,R2 为 0.975,而在相同的气候和社会经济假设下拟合的机械 SEIR-SEI 基线的 MAE 为 43.57,R2 为 0.870。
解释与创新
通过将机械结构与数据自适应灵活性相融合,所提出的 SEIR-PINN 提供了一个可解释的框架,用于分析城市流行环境中登革热 传播 的联合气候和社会经济调节。
科研与公卫意义
因此,结果支持物理信息混合模型作为登革热 监测 和 传播 动力学研究的可解释工具的潜在用途,同时认识到需要在更长的时间段和更多地理区域进行进一步的外部验证。
局限与证据强度
这些保留的结果插值在观察到的流行病中,并不是对预期预测表现的评估。重要的是,SEIR-PINN 重建了潜在的人类和蚊子区室,这些区室在整个流行阶段保持生物学一致性。

Hybrid Epidemiological–Neural Modelling of Dengue Under Climate-Socioeconomic Influence

Authors: Adejimi Adeniji, Abdun Mahmood, Joel Miller, Zhen He
Original abstract (English)
Dengue transmission in climate-sensitive and socioeconomically heterogeneous settings arises from nonlinear interactions between environmental suitability, mosquito ecology, and human behaviour. Classical SEIR-type models offer mechanistic interpretability but are often constrained by observational noise and incomplete data, while purely data-driven approaches typically fail to respect underlying transmission dynamics. In this study, we introduce a hybrid epidemiological framework that embeds a temperature–unemployment driven SEIR–SEI model within a Physics-Informed Neural Network (SEIR–PINN). Using weekly dengue incidence data from Medellín, Colombia, we incorporate unemployment as a socioeconomic proxy that modulates transmission alongside climatic suitability. The Model performance was evaluated in two ways; the prediction of observed incidence, the SEIR–PINN achieved a mean absolute error (MAE) of 15.05 and R2 of 0.975, compared with MAE of 43.57 and R2 of 0.870 for a mechanistic SEIR–SEI baseline fitted under identical climatic and socioeconomic assumptions. Shuffled five-fold cross-validation provided held-out prediction of observed incidence within the 2009–2011 period, with mean MAE of 14.87 and mean R2 of 0.977 (out-of-fold MAE 14.89, R2 0.978). These held-out results interpolate within the observed epidemic and are not an assessment of prospective forecasting performance Importantly, the SEIR–PINN reconstructs latent human and mosquito compartments that remain biologically consistent across epidemic phases. By fusing mechanistic structure with data-adaptive flexibility, the proposed SEIR–PINN provides an interpretable framework for analysing the joint climatic and socioeconomic modulation of dengue transmission in an urban endemic setting. Although using Medellín as a single-city methodological case study, application of this framework to other dengue endemic settings requires external validation using additional locations, longer time periods, and independent datasets. The results therefore support the potential use of physics-informed hybrid models as interpretable tools for dengue surveillance and transmission-dynamics research, while recognising the need for further external validation across longer time periods and additional geographical regions.

Interpretation and novelty

基于不完整的医院 监测 的贝叶斯疾病图谱:解释印度尼西亚望加锡登革热 监测 的流域和缺失病例起源

Bayesian disease mapping from incomplete hospital-based surveillance: accounting for catchment and missing case origins in dengue surveillance in Makassar, Indonesia
作者： Bobby Poerwanto, Ian Hall, Emma Louise Fairbanks, Thomas House, Olatunji Olugoke Johnson
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革热 监测 数据通常是在医疗机构而不是患者的住所收集的,因此报告的病例位置反映了就医行为以及潜在的疾病过程。在实践中,案例具有不同程度的位置详细信息:有些是地理定位的,有些仅是该分区已知的,有些根本不包含任何居住信息。这种不完整性使空间风险估计变得复杂。我们开发了一个分层贝叶斯点过程框架,该框架可以绘制登革热风险图,同时对医院流域行为和未观察到的病例起源进行联合建模。分区级强度采用具有人口偏移量的 Besag--York--Molli\'e (BYM) 结构以每人为基础进行建模;基于距离的重力(幂律)内核代表医院吸引力;位置不完整的情况在单一可能性内处理,该可能性容纳三个级别的空间分辨率并边缘化未知来源。该框架应用于印度尼西亚望加锡八家医院的 1{,}842 例登革热病例(2021--2025 年),它比传统的指数流域内核和确定性分配基线更好地恢复了保留的病例起源。完整模型将 Manggala 确定为估计相对风险最高的分区 (2.7),Tamalate、Rappocini 和 Tallo 也符合指定的高风险标准。按模型估计的相对风险进行的排名与按记录病例数进行的排名不同。该框架提供了一种根据不完整的、基于设施的 监测 数据绘制疾病图谱的原则性方法。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决登革热 监测 位置数据不完整的挑战,其中报告的病例位置反映了就医行为,而不是真实的疾病分布。
设计与对象
该研究利用贝叶斯点过程框架,利用 2021 年至 2025 年期间八家医院的数据绘制了印度尼西亚望加锡的登革热风险图。
核心方法
该框架包含 Besag--York--Mollié (BYM) 结构、基于距离的重力内核以及适应位置数据不完整的情况的可能性。
主要结果
该模型比传统的指数流域内核和确定性分配基线更好地恢复了保留案例的起源。
解释与创新
该框架提供了一种根据不完整的、基于设施的 监测 数据绘制疾病图谱的原则性方法,从而提高了风险估计的准确性。
科研与公卫意义
该模型确定了高风险分区,可以为有针对性的公共卫生干预措施和资源分配提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的结果受到望加锡具体情况和对医院数据的依赖的限制,这些数据可能无法完全代表更广泛的人群。

Bayesian disease mapping from incomplete hospital-based surveillance: accounting for catchment and missing case origins in dengue surveillance in Makassar, Indonesia

Authors: Bobby Poerwanto, Ian Hall, Emma Louise Fairbanks, Thomas House, Olatunji Olugoke Johnson
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Dengue surveillance data are typically collected at healthcare facilities rather than at patients' residences, so reported case locations reflect healthcare-seeking behaviour as much as the underlying disease process. In practice, cases carry differing levels of location detail: some are geolocated, some are known only to the subdistrict, and some carry no residential information at all. This incompleteness complicates spatial risk estimation. We develop a hierarchical Bayesian point-process framework that maps dengue risk while jointly modelling hospital catchment behaviour and unobserved case origins. Subdistrict-level intensity is modelled on a per-person basis using a Besag--York--Molli\'e (BYM) structure with a population offset; a distance-based gravity (power-law) kernel represents hospital attraction; and cases with incomplete location are handled within a single likelihood that accommodates three levels of spatial resolution and marginalises over unknown origins. Applied to 1{,}842 dengue cases from eight hospitals in Makassar, Indonesia (2021--2025), the framework recovers held-out case origins substantially better than a conventional exponential catchment kernel and than deterministic allocation baselines. The full model identifies Manggala as the subdistrict with the highest estimated relative risk (2.7), with Tamalate, Rappocini and Tallo also meeting the specified high-risk criterion. Rankings by model-estimated relative risk differ from rankings by recorded case count. The framework offers a principled approach to disease mapping from incomplete, facility-based surveillance data.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of incomplete location data in dengue surveillance, where reported case locations reflect healthcare-seeking behavior rather than the true disease distribution.
Design and population
The study utilized a Bayesian point-process framework to map dengue risk in Makassar, Indonesia, using data from eight hospitals over the period 2021-2025.
Core methods
The framework incorporated a Besag--York--Mollié (BYM) structure, a distance-based gravity kernel, and a likelihood that accommodates cases with incomplete location data.
Main results
The model recovered held-out case origins better than a conventional exponential catchment kernel and deterministic allocation baselines.
Interpretation and novelty
The framework offers a principled approach to disease mapping from incomplete, facility-based surveillance data, improving the accuracy of risk estimation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The model identifies high-risk subdistricts, which can inform targeted public health interventions and resource allocation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's findings are limited by the specific context of Makassar and the reliance on hospital-based data, which may not fully represent the broader population.

城市热岛对登革热热 传播 适宜性的影响对比美国城市

Impact of urban heat islands on dengue thermal transmission suitability across contrasting U.S. cities
作者： Ava C Johnson, Di Yang, Sadie J. Jane Ryan
摘要中文翻译蚊子等变温动物对环境条件(包括温度)非常敏感。登革热 是由 登革病毒 引起并由伊蚊传播的疾病,通常被认为是一种热带疾病,但最近在美国的小型局部 暴发 中出现。城市热岛效应是一种众所周知的现象,但由于气象观测的局限性,在城市环境范围内测量其对气温的影响变得复杂。这些观测结果仅在气象站所在的离散坐标处收集,因此很难解释整个景观的空间变化。尽管ERA5等全球网格化再分析数据可以每小时一次获得,但最精细的空间分辨率为0.25∘,因此丢失了变化的细节。在这个项目中,我们开发了一个本地化机器学习模型框架,能够以 30 米的精细分辨率预测给定研究区域的气温。我们提供的模型是根据特定位置的卫星图像和当地气象站数据进行训练的,但我们提供了与免费可用数据一起使用的自动化代码,使它们可以针对几乎任何位置进行重新训练。在本研究中,我们使用这些预测来评估登革热 传播 对埃及伊蚊和伊蚊的适宜性的季节性。白纹伊蚊分布在美国两个城市。城市化地区与 媒介 物种的预计登革热季节延长有关。这凸显了需要关注城市规划和 媒介 控制,以减轻密集城市地区虫媒病毒的负担。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查城市热岛如何影响 登革病毒 的热 传播 适宜性,因为由 登革病毒 引起并由伊蚊传播的 登革热 最近在美国局部 暴发 中出现。
设计与对象
该研究使用本地化机器学习模型以 30 米分辨率精细预测美国两个城市的气温,评估登革热 传播 对埃及伊蚊和伊蚊的适宜性。
核心方法
这些模型根据特定位置的卫星图像和当地气象站数据进行训练,并提供自动化代码以与免费可用的数据一起使用,从而使它们能够针对几乎任何位置进行重新训练。
主要结果
城市化地区与 媒介 物种的预计登革热季节延长有关。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,城市热岛可能会导致登革热季节延长,这凸显了城市规划和 媒介 控制的必要性,以减轻密集城市地区虫媒病毒的负担。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为城市规划和 媒介 控制策略提供参考,以降低人口稠密地区登革热 暴发 的风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究对再分析数据和离散气象站观测的依赖可能会限制空间温度变化的精度,但该框架允许使用本地数据进行重新训练,提供中等水平的证据。

Impact of urban heat islands on dengue thermal transmission suitability across contrasting U.S. cities

Authors: Ava C Johnson, Di Yang, Sadie J. Jane Ryan
Original abstract (English)
Ectotherms such as mosquitoes are exquisitely sensitive to environmental conditions, including temperature. Dengue fever, caused by dengue virus and transmitted by Aedes mosquitos, is commonly thought of as a tropical disease but has recently emerged in small local outbreaks in the USA. Urban heat island effects are a well-known phenomenon, but measuring their impact on air temperature at the scale of urban environments is complicated by the limitations of meteorological observations. These observations are collected only at discrete coordinates in which weather stations are located, making it difficult to interpret spatial variation across a landscape. Although global gridded reanalysis data such as ERA5 are available at hourly frequency, the finest spatial resolution is 0.25 ∘ , and thus the details in variation are lost. In this project, we developed a framework for localized machine learning models to predict the air temperature at a fine-scale 30 meter resolution for a given study area. The models we present are trained on location-specific satellite imagery and local weather station data, but we provide automated code for use with freely available data, allowing them to be retrained for nearly any location. In this study, we used these predictions to assess the seasonality of dengue thermal transmission suitability for Aedes aegypti and Ae. albopictus in two US cities. Urbanized areas were associated with extended projected dengue seasons for both vector species. This highlights the need for attention to urban planning and vector control in order to reduce the burden of arboviruses in dense urban areas.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate how urban heat islands affect the thermal transmission suitability of dengue virus, as dengue fever, caused by dengue virus and transmitted by Aedes mosquitoes, has recently emerged in small local outbreaks in the USA.
Design and population
The study used localized machine learning models to predict air temperature at a fine-scale 30 meter resolution for two US cities, assessing the seasonality of dengue thermal transmission suitability for Aedes aegypti and Ae.
Core methods
The models were trained on location-specific satellite imagery and local weather station data, and automated code was provided for use with freely available data, allowing them to be retrained for nearly any location.
Main results
Urbanized areas were associated with extended projected dengue seasons for both vector species.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that urban heat islands may contribute to prolonged dengue seasons, highlighting the need for urban planning and vector control to mitigate the burden of arboviruses in dense urban areas.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results can inform urban planning and vector control strategies to reduce the risk of dengue outbreaks in densely populated areas.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's reliance on reanalysis data and discrete weather station observations may limit the precision of spatial temperature variation, but the framework allows for retraining with local data, providing a moderate level of evidence.

基于 COMSOL 的用于登革热 NS1 检测的叉指电极生物传感器的阻抗和灵敏度分析

COMSOL-Based Impedance and Sensitivity Analysis of an Interdigitated Electrode Biosensor for Dengue NS1 Detection
作者： Saleh I. Alzahrani
摘要中文翻译登革热仍然是全球主要的公共卫生问题,这突出表明需要快速、灵敏的方法来早期检测 登革病毒 生物标志物。在本研究中,我们在 COMSOL Multiphysics 中开发了一种有限元模型,用于使用基于叉指电极 (IDE) 的阻抗生物传感器检测登革热非结构蛋白 1 (NS1)。所提出的传感器由涂有基于抗体的生物识别层并浸入导电水性样品介质中的金 IDE 组成。使用 Langmuir 吸附等温线对抗体-NS1 结合进行建模。使用频域模拟来检查 NS1 浓度从 0 到 1000 ng/cm3 范围内的阻抗响应。结果表明,模拟阻抗幅度随着 NS1 浓度和激励频率的增加而减小。在 10 MHz 时,归一化阻抗响应在低 NS1 浓度下迅速增加,并在较高浓度下逐渐接近饱和。对三个模拟浓度点 (1–3 ng/cm3) 的线性拟合产生了 0.9223%/(ng/cm3) (R2 = 0.9963) 的归一化响应斜率,提供了模型预测的低浓度响应的初步表征。这些发现证明了计算模型在表征基于 IDE 的阻抗 NS1 生物传感器的预测电响应方面的潜力,为传感器优化和未来的实验验证提供了基础。报告的灵敏度和浓度依赖性响应是基于模拟的预测,尚未经过实验验证。

问题与背景
登革热仍然是全球主要的公共卫生问题,这突出表明需要快速、灵敏的方法来早期检测 登革病毒 生物标志物。

COMSOL-Based Impedance and Sensitivity Analysis of an Interdigitated Electrode Biosensor for Dengue NS1 Detection

Authors: Saleh I. Alzahrani
Original abstract (English)
Dengue remains a major global public health concern, highlighting the need for rapid and sensitive methods for the early detection of dengue virus biomarkers. In the present study, a finite element model for the detection of dengue nonstructural protein 1 (NS1) using an interdigitated electrode (IDE)-based impedimetric biosensor was developed in COMSOL Multiphysics. The proposed sensor consists of gold IDEs coated with an antibody-based biorecognition layer and immersed in a conductive aqueous sample medium. Antibody–NS1 binding was modeled using the Langmuir adsorption isotherm. Frequency-domain simulations were used to examine the impedance response over a range of NS1 concentrations from 0 to 1000 ng/cm3. The results showed that the simulated impedance magnitude decreased with increasing NS1 concentration and excitation frequency. At 10 MHz, the normalized impedance response increased rapidly at low NS1 concentrations and gradually approached saturation at higher concentrations. A linear fit to three simulated concentration points (1–3 ng/cm3) yielded a normalized response slope of 0.9223%/(ng/cm3) (R2 = 0.9963), providing a preliminary characterization of the model-predicted low-concentration response. These findings demonstrate the potential of computational modeling for characterizing the predicted electrical response of an IDE-based impedimetric NS1 biosensor, providing a basis for sensor optimization and future experimental validation. The reported sensitivity and concentration-dependent responses are simulation-based predictions and have not been experimentally validated.
View research seven-element analysis

用于登革热 NS1 检测的人工免疫系统集成氧化还原电容生物传感架构:概念的计算证明

An Artificial Immune System-Integrated Redox-Capacitive Biosensing Architecture for Dengue NS1 Detection: A Computational Proof of Concept
作者： Fabio Roberto Chavarette, Lucas Bueno Pereira, Adriano dos Santos
摘要中文翻译登革病毒 感染仍然是全球主要的公共卫生问题,需要快速可靠的诊断工具。这项工作介绍了智能生物分析架构的计算概念验证,该架构将氧化还原电容生物传感平台与人工免疫系统 (AIS) 机器学习框架相结合,以检测登革热 NS1 蛋白。使用跨 49 个频率点的 32 个独立样本配置文件的模拟数据集,在不同的操作场景下对隔离的实数 (C') 和虚数 (C'') 电容组件系统地评估了 AIS 算法。利用真实电容 (C'),AIS 算法在区分阳性 NS1 轮廓与对照和阴性基质方面实现了 100.0% 的准确度,同时动态调整其形状亲和力 Limen 参数以在模糊的空白与统计重叠分析中保持噪声弹性(83.3% 准确度)。使用 Bhattacharyya 指数的统计重叠分析显示,虚部电容 (C'') 充当高度特异性的互补标记。虽然 C'' 显示空白对照和阴性样本之间几乎完全结构重叠 (0.995),证明了对背景基质噪声的物理盲性,但它实现了 NS1 相互作用的完美目标识别(100.0% 准确度)。这种双通道电容计算框架以亚毫秒算法执行时间为后盾,为未来的实时护理点诊断工具提供了坚实的基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革病毒 感染仍然是全球主要的公共卫生问题,需要快速可靠的诊断工具。
设计与对象
这项工作介绍了智能生物分析架构的计算概念验证,该架构将氧化还原电容生物传感平台与人工免疫系统 (AIS) 机器学习框架相结合,以检测登革热 NS1 蛋白。
核心方法
使用跨 49 个频率点的 32 个独立样本配置文件的模拟数据集,在不同的操作场景下对隔离的实数 (C') 和虚数 (C'') 电容组件系统地评估了 AIS 算法。
主要结果
利用真实电容 (C'),AIS 算法在区分正 NS1 轮廓与对照和负矩阵方面实现了 100.0% 的准确度,同时动态调整其形状亲和力 Limen 参数以在模糊的空白与空白对照中保持噪声弹性(83.3% 准确度)。
解释与创新
使用 Bhattacharyya 指数的统计重叠分析表明,虚电容 (C'') 充当高度特异性的互补标记。
科研与公卫意义
虽然 C'' 显示空白对照和阴性样本之间几乎完全结构重叠 (0.995),证明了对背景基质噪声的物理盲性,但它实现了 NS1 相互作用的完美目标识别(100.0% 准确度)。
局限与证据强度
这种双通道电容计算框架以亚毫秒算法执行时间为后盾,为未来的实时护理点诊断工具提供了坚实的基础。

An Artificial Immune System-Integrated Redox-Capacitive Biosensing Architecture for Dengue NS1 Detection: A Computational Proof of Concept

Authors: Fabio Roberto Chavarette, Lucas Bueno Pereira, Adriano dos Santos
Original abstract (English)
Dengue virus infection remains a major global public health concern, demanding rapid and reliable diagnostic tools. This work introduces a computational proof of concept for an intelligent bioanalytical architecture combining a redox-capacitive biosensing platform with an Artificial Immune System (AIS) machine learning framework to detect the Dengue NS1 protein. Using a simulated dataset of 32 independent sample profiles across 49 frequency points, the AIS algorithm was systematically evaluated on isolated real (C′) and imaginary (C′′) capacitance components under distinct operational scenarios. Utilizing real capacitance (C′), the AIS algorithm achieved 100.0% accuracy in discriminating positive NS1 profiles from controls and negative matrices, while dynamically adapting its Shape Affinity Limen parameter to maintain noise-resilience (83.3% accuracy) in the ambiguous Blank vs. Statistical overlap analysis using the Bhattacharyya Index revealed that imaginary capacitance (C′′) acts as a highly specific complementary marker. While C′′ showed near-total structural overlap (0.995) between blank controls and negative samples—proving physical blindness to background matrix noise—it achieved flawless target identification (100.0% accuracy) for NS1 interaction. Backed by sub-millisecond algorithmic execution times, this dual-channel capacitive–computational framework provides a robust foundation for prospective real-time, point-of-care diagnostic tools.

Limitations and evidence strength

Original abstract (English)
Nghiên cứu nhằm mô tả đặc điểm lâm sàng, cận lâm sàng và kết quả điều trị sốc sốt xuất huyết Dengue(SXHD) ở trẻ vị thành niên (từ 13 đến dưới 16 tuổi) tại Bệnh viện Đa khoa Tiền Giang. Nghiên cứu cắtngang có phân tích, hồi cứu hồ sơ bệnh án của 35 trẻ được chẩn đoán sốc SXHD trong giai đoạn 7/2023–7/2025. Tuổi trung bình là 14,5 ± 0,9 tuổi; nam chiếm 48,6%. 51,4% trẻ thừa cân hoặc béo phì và 22,9% cósốc SXHD nặng. Phần lớn trẻ vào sốc ở ngày bệnh thứ 5–6 (62,9%). Khi vào sốc, hematocrit trung bình là49,4 ± 4,8%, tiểu cầu 32.549 ± 16.167 tế bào/mm3; 77,1% có lactate ≥ 2 mmol/L và 28,6% có AST và/hoặcALT ≥ 400 U/L. Dung dịch cao phân tử được sử dụng ở 77,1% trường hợp; tổng lượng dịch truyền trungbình là 148,2 ± 41,6 mL/kg. 伊朗海员中 登革病毒 暴露的证据:来自伊朗南部的简短 血清流行率 报告

Evidence of dengue virus exposure among Iranian seafarers: a brief seroprevalence report from Southern Iran
作者： Amir Hossein Darabi, Mahboobeh Hayati, Golnaz Hayatdavoudi, Omid Gharibi, Sahand Khajeheian, Mohsen Keshavarz
摘要中文翻译简介:登革病毒 (DENV) 是伊朗新出现的公共卫生问题。伊朗南部港口与报告登革热 传播 的邻国之间频繁的海上旅行可能会促进登革热病毒传入伊朗。因此,本研究旨在确定伊朗海员中 DENV 的 血清流行率。方法:我们对布什尔省的 270 名海员进行了横断面血清调查。使用 ELISA 检测血清样本中的 DENV IgM/IgG。通过问卷调查收集旅行历史和人口统计数据。结果:血清学检测发现 7 名 DENV 血清阳性个体(2.6%),其中 2 名 IgM 阳性和 5 名 IgG 阳性参与者。最近旅行者中的血清阳性率高于过去旅行者中的血清阳性率(5.6%,而在布什尔(5.0%)、阿萨卢耶(16.7%)、杰纳韦(5.0%)、坎甘(1.7%)和代耶尔(1.3%)检测到阳性病例,而在迪拉姆雇用的参与者中没有发现血清阳性病例。结论:尽管伊朗人中的 DENV 血清流行率 仍然较低对于海员来说,最近和过去感染的证据表明,职业性海上旅行可能会导致登革热病毒暴露,鉴于海员在跨境流动中的战略作用,定期对该职业群体进行血清学检测可能有助于及早发现登革热病毒的传入,并加强伊朗南部的公共卫生准备。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
因此,本研究旨在确定伊朗海员中 DENV 的 血清流行率。
证据基础与方法
我们对布什尔省的 270 名海员进行了横断面血清调查。
共识与结论
尽管伊朗海员中 DENV 血清流行率 仍然较低,但最近和过去感染的证据表明,职业海上旅行可能会导致 DENV 暴露。
争议与缺口
所提供的证据没有明确指出争议或证据差距。
科研与实践启示
鉴于海员在跨境流动中的战略作用,该职业群体的定期血清学 监测 可能有助于及早发现登革热病毒的传入,并加强伊朗南部的公共卫生准备。

Evidence of dengue virus exposure among Iranian seafarers: a brief seroprevalence report from Southern Iran

Authors: Amir Hossein Darabi, Mahboobeh Hayati, Golnaz Hayatdavoudi, Omid Gharibi, Sahand Khajeheian, Mohsen Keshavarz
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Dengue virus (DENV) is an emerging public health concern in Iran. Frequent maritime travel between southern Iranian ports and neighboring countries with reported dengue transmission may facilitate the introduction of DENV into Iran. Therefore, this study aimed to determine the seroprevalence of DENV among Iranian seafarers. METHODS: We conducted a cross-sectional serosurvey among 270 seafarers in Bushehr Province. Serum samples were tested for DENV IgM/IgG using ELISA. Travel history and demographics were collected via questionnaires. RESULTS: Serological testing identified 7 DENV-seropositive individuals (2.6%), including 2 IgM-positive and 5 IgG-positive participants. Seropositivity was higher among recent travelers than among past travelers (5.6% vs. Positive cases were detected in Bushehr (5.0%), Asaluyeh (16.7%), Genaveh (5.0%), Kangan (1.7%), and Dayyer (1.3%), whereas no seropositive cases were identified among participants employed in Deylam. CONCLUSIONS: Although DENV seroprevalence remained low among Iranian seafarers, evidence of both recent and past infections indicates that occupational maritime travel may contribute to DENV exposure. Given the strategic role of seafarers in cross-border movement, periodic serological surveillance of this occupational group may facilitate early detection of DENV introduction and strengthen public health preparedness in southern Iran.

Therefore, this study aimed to determine the seroprevalence of DENV among Iranian seafarers.
Evidence base and method
We conducted a cross-sectional serosurvey among 270 seafarers in Bushehr Province.
Consensus and conclusions
Although DENV seroprevalence remained low among Iranian seafarers, evidence of both recent and past infections indicates that occupational maritime travel may contribute to DENV exposure.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not clearly state controversies or evidence gaps.
Research and practice implications
Given the strategic role of seafarers in cross-border movement, periodic serological surveillance of this occupational group may facilitate early detection of DENV introduction and strengthen public health preparedness in southern Iran.

墨西哥2020-2024年登革热的区域与时间动态:一项全面的流行病学分析。

Regional and Temporal Dynamics of Dengue in Mexico (2020-2024): A Comprehensive Epidemiological Analysis.
作者： Blanca Taboada, Selene Zárate, Lorena Díaz-González, Juan Manuel Hurtado
摘要中文翻译登革热仍然是墨西哥的主要公共卫生问题,近年来其流行程度和临床负担显著增加。本研究利用2020年至2024年的全国登革热数据,全面概述了登革热流行病学景观的变化,重点分析了地理分布、时间趋势、血清型动态以及疾病严重程度。在这一时期,墨西哥的登革热病例数几乎增加了五倍,2024年达到超过123,000例确诊病例和426例死亡,这是有记录以来最高的年度病例数。季节性模式发生了变化,持续的登革热传播从2023年底延伸到了不寻常的冬季月份。血清型数据揭示了主要病毒血清型的变化,DENV2在2022年之前占主导地位,DENV1在2021年达到高峰,而DENV3则从2022年开始急剧上升,成为2023年的主导血清型,并在2024年几乎成为唯一流行的血清型。这种变化与登革热病例数的持续上升以及警告信号的增加(从2020年的16.9%上升到2023-2024年的>42%)相吻合,同时医院入院率也显著提高,而重症登革热病例仍低于5%。尽管DENV3在全国范围内占主导地位,但DENV1和DENV2在某些州仍持续存在,而DENV4则在南部和北部地区偶有流行。一些传统上登革热流行率较低的州出现了非典型的流行模式,突显了登革热暴发可能突然扩展的潜在风险。气候分析显示,温度与DENV3的流行率之间存在一致的正相关关系,而降水的影响则因年份而异。比值比分析表明,不同血清型在不同年份的严重程度存在差异。这些发现突显了墨西哥登革热传播模式的异质性和动态性,并强调了制定区域性监测、预防和临床准备策略的必要性。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述聚焦于2020年至2024年墨西哥登革热的区域与时间动态,分析了地理分布、时间趋势、血清型动态以及疾病严重程度。核心问题是了解这一时期登革热传播模式的变化及其对公共卫生的影响。
证据基础与方法
该评价利用了2020年至2024年的国家监测数据。它包括对血清型特异性数据、气候因素和住院率的综合分析。研究设计基于观察流行病学数据,没有明确提及荟萃分析或共识过程。
共识与结论
墨西哥的登革热 重症登革热 和临床负担大幅增加,到 2024 年 DENV3 将成为主导。审查还指出,重症登革热 病例的比例仍低于 5%,而警告信号显着增加。研究结果强调了登革热 传播 在不同地区和年份的动态和异质性。
争议与缺口
审查发现,随着时间的推移,主要血清型发生了变化,从 2022 年起,DENV3 急剧上升。然而,驱动这种转变的确切机制尚未得到充分解释。此外,研究指出,一些传统上低 患病率 的状态经历了非典型的 暴发,但这些变化的原因尚未明确说明。
科研与实践启示
该综述强调,由于流行病学形势的变化,需要制定区域 监测、预防和临床准备策略。它还表明需要进一步的研究来了解导致血清型优势转变的因素以及某些州非典型 暴发 背后的原因。

Regional and Temporal Dynamics of Dengue in Mexico (2020-2024): A Comprehensive Epidemiological Analysis.

Authors: Blanca Taboada, Selene Zárate, Lorena Díaz-González, Juan Manuel Hurtado
Original abstract (English)
Dengue remains a major public health concern in Mexico, with incidence and clinical burden increasing substantially in recent years. Using national surveillance data from 2020 to 2024, this study provides a comprehensive overview of the changing epidemiological landscape, with an emphasis on geographic distribution, temporal trends, serotype dynamics, and disease severity. Over this period, Mexico saw an almost fivefold increase in dengue cases, reaching over 123,000 confirmed infections and 426 deaths in 2024, the highest annual number recorded. Seasonal patterns shifted, with sustained transmission extending into unusual winter months from late 2023. Serotyping data revealed a shift in predominant virus serotypes, with DENV2 predominating until 2022, DENV1 peaking in 2021, and DENV3 rising sharply from 2022 onward, becoming dominant in 2023 and almost exclusive by 2024. This shift coincided with a sustained rise in dengue with warning signs (from 16.9% in 2020 to > 42% in 2023-2024) and higher hospitalisation rates, while severe dengue remained below 5%. Although DENV3 dominated nationally, DENV1 and DENV2 persisted in specific states, and DENV4 circulated sporadically, particularly in southern and northern regions. Several traditionally low-prevalence states experienced atypical outbreaks, underscoring the potential for sudden epidemic expansion. Climatic analysis showed a consistent positive association between temperature and DENV3 prevalence, with precipitation effects varying by year. Odds ratio analysis suggested serotype-specific differences in severity across years. These findings underscore the heterogeneous and dynamic nature of dengue transmission in Mexico and highlight the need for regional surveillance, prevention, and clinical preparedness strategies.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on the regional and temporal dynamics of dengue in Mexico from 2020 to 2024, examining geographic distribution, temporal trends, serotype dynamics, and disease severity. The central question is to understand how dengue transmission patterns have evolved over this period and their implications for public health.
Evidence base and method
The review utilizes national surveillance data from 2020 to 2024. It includes a comprehensive analysis of serotype-specific data, climatic factors, and hospitalization rates. The study design is based on observational epidemiological data, with no explicit mention of meta-analysis or consensus processes.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that dengue incidence and clinical burden have increased substantially in Mexico, with DENV3 becoming dominant by 2024. It also notes that severe dengue remained below 5% of cases, while warning signs increased significantly. The findings highlight the dynamic and heterogeneous nature of dengue transmission across different regions and years.
Controversies and gaps
The review identifies a shift in predominant serotypes over time, with DENV3 rising sharply from 2022 onward. However, the exact mechanisms driving this shift are not fully explained. Additionally, the study notes that several traditionally low-prevalence states experienced atypical outbreaks, but the reasons for these variations are not clearly addressed.
Research and practice implications
The review emphasizes the need for regional surveillance, prevention, and clinical preparedness strategies due to the changing epidemiological landscape. It also suggests that further research is required to understand the factors contributing to the shift in serotype dominance and the reasons behind the atypical outbreaks in certain states.

超越儿科登革热支持性护理:新兴疗法和 WHO PADO 2026 优先事项——叙述性回顾

Beyond supportive care in pediatric dengue: Emerging therapeutics and the WHO PADO 2026 priorities–A narrative review
作者： Meet Hiteshkumar Patel, Sarika Bhimrao Gaikwad
摘要中文翻译儿童登革热仍然是流行病环境下住院和休克的重要原因,而 治疗 继续依赖支持性护理。这篇叙述性综述批判性地评估了研究中的直接作用 抗病毒药物、中和单克隆抗体和 宿主 定向药物以及世界卫生组织 (WHO) 儿科药物优化 (PADO) 2026 年优先事项。截至 2026 年 10 月,PubMed 索引的临床和机制文献以及 WHO 指南提供了选择性综合信息。NS4B 和 NS5 抑制剂提供了令人信服的机制信号,但早期病毒抑制尚未被证明可以预防儿童 重症登革热。在一项针对健康成人的对照人类感染研究中,Mosnodenvir 可减少病毒 RNA;这并不能确定对有症状的儿科感染的疗效,并且耐药性是一个问题。在一项涉及 250 名成年人的随机试验中,登革热单克隆抗体(以前称为 VIS513)加速了感染病毒和发烧的清除,但尚未建立对休克、器官功能障碍和住院治疗的预防措施。儿科转化还取决于免疫史、突然退热相关的血管渗漏、年龄依赖性生理学、早期 治疗 的可行性以及基于抗体的干预措施的安全性,而不仅仅是剂量调整。PADO 优先考虑 SII 登革热单克隆抗体,并将 AV-1、EYU-688、芬维A胺和莫诺登韦列入观察名单。病毒学终点对于早期开发仍然有用;儿科试验必须最终证明具有可接受的安全性和可部署的治疗方案的持续临床恢复或预防恶化。目前尚未确定用于常规儿科登革热 治疗 的研究药物。

范围与问题
儿童登革热仍然是流行病环境下住院和休克的重要原因,而 治疗 继续依赖支持性护理。
证据基础与方法
这篇叙述性综述批判性地评估了研究中的直接作用 抗病毒药物、中和单克隆抗体和 宿主 定向药物以及世界卫生组织 (WHO) 儿科药物优化 (PADO) 2026 年优先事项。截至 2026 年 10 月,PubMed 索引的临床和机制文献以及 WHO 指南提供了选择性综合信息。

NS4B 和 NS5 抑制剂提供了令人信服的机制信号,但早期病毒抑制尚未被证明可以预防儿童 重症登革热。病毒学终点对于早期开发仍然有用;儿科试验必须最终证明具有可接受的安全性和可部署的治疗方案的持续临床恢复或预防恶化。
争议与缺口
在一项针对健康成人的对照人类感染研究中,Mosnodenvir 可减少病毒 RNA;这并不能确定对有症状的儿科感染的疗效,并且耐药性是一个问题。目前尚未确定用于常规儿科登革热 治疗 的研究药物。
科研与实践启示
儿科转化还取决于免疫史、突然退热相关的血管渗漏、年龄依赖性生理学、早期 治疗 的可行性以及基于抗体的干预措施的安全性,而不仅仅是剂量调整。

Beyond supportive care in pediatric dengue: Emerging therapeutics and the WHO PADO 2026 priorities–A narrative review

Authors: Meet Hiteshkumar Patel, Sarika Bhimrao Gaikwad
Original abstract (English)
Pediatric dengue remains an important cause of hospitalization and shock in endemic settings, while treatment continues to rely on supportive care. This narrative review critically evaluates investigational direct-acting antivirals, neutralizing monoclonal antibodies, and host-directed agents alongside the World Health Organization (WHO) Paediatric Drug Optimization (PADO) 2026 priorities. PubMed-indexed clinical and mechanistic literature and WHO guidance available through October 2026 informed a selective synthesis. NS4B and NS5 inhibitors provide convincing mechanistic signals, but early viral suppression has not been shown to prevent severe dengue in children. Mosnodenvir reduced viral RNA in a controlled human infection study conducted as prophylaxis in healthy adults; this does not establish efficacy in symptomatic pediatric infection, and resistance is a concern. In a randomized trial involving 250 adults, dengue-mAb (formerly VIS513) accelerated infectious-virus and fever clearance, but prevention of shock, organ dysfunction, and hospitalization was not established. Pediatric translation also depends on immune history, abrupt defervescence-associated vascular leakage, age-dependent physiology, the feasibility of very early treatment, and the safety of antibody-based interventions, not merely dose adjustment. PADO prioritizes SII dengue-mAb and places AV-1, EYU-688, fenretinide, and mosnodenvir on its watch list. Virological endpoints remain useful for early development; pediatric trials must ultimately demonstrate sustained clinical recovery or prevention of deterioration with acceptable safety and deployable regimens. No investigational agent reviewed is currently established for routine pediatric dengue treatment.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Pediatric dengue remains an important cause of hospitalization and shock in endemic settings, while treatment continues to rely on supportive care.
Evidence base and method
This narrative review critically evaluates investigational direct-acting antivirals, neutralizing monoclonal antibodies, and host-directed agents alongside the World Health Organization (WHO) Paediatric Drug Optimization (PADO) 2026 priorities. PubMed-indexed clinical and mechanistic literature and WHO guidance available through October 2026 informed a selective synthesis.
Consensus and conclusions
NS4B and NS5 inhibitors provide convincing mechanistic signals, but early viral suppression has not been shown to prevent severe dengue in children. Virological endpoints remain useful for early development; pediatric trials must ultimately demonstrate sustained clinical recovery or prevention of deterioration with acceptable safety and deployable regimens.
Controversies and gaps
Mosnodenvir reduced viral RNA in a controlled human infection study conducted as prophylaxis in healthy adults; this does not establish efficacy in symptomatic pediatric infection, and resistance is a concern. No investigational agent reviewed is currently established for routine pediatric dengue treatment.
Research and practice implications
Pediatric translation also depends on immune history, abrupt defervescence-associated vascular leakage, age-dependent physiology, the feasibility of very early treatment, and the safety of antibody-based interventions, not merely dose adjustment.

拉丁美洲流行病学和登革热预防和 媒介 社区社区控制。

Epidemiología y prevención del dengue mediante el control de criaderos del vector en comunidades endémicas en América Latina.
作者： Eva Patricia Montalvo-Flores, Steffi Gabriela Haro-Quijije, Renny Elian Pujupat-Choez, Jairo Geovanny Cañarte Químis, Jairo Geovanny Cañarte-Quimis
摘要中文翻译登革热是一种由埃及伊蚊传播的病毒性疾病,由于其高流行病学负担以及有利于其扩散的因素,已成为拉丁美洲的一个主要公共卫生问题。本系统评价的目的是分析 流行病学 和登革热预防以及流行社区繁殖地的控制。结果显示,DENV-1、DENV-2、DENV-3和DENV-4血清型广泛传播,其中以无预警信号的登革热为主,患病率的比例大于90%。与媒介扩散相关的主要因素包括气候条件、无计划的城市化、水容器的堆积、储水不足、卫生设施缺陷以及健康教育水平低。在预防策略方面,综合干预措施被证明对减少病例最为有效,特别是有针对性的室内滞留喷洒、流行病学监测和社区参与,并辅以通过控制繁殖地进行的社区环境管理。结论是,登革热预防需要可持续的 媒介 控制计划,将 监测、生物技术创新和社区参与结合起来,以减少流行地区的疾病 监测。

范围与问题
登革病毒 1-4 和登革热病;其他黄病毒属于近邻排除项。生物相关的动物、分类学、模型、差异-诊断 和比较实体仅是补充,除非还存在材料目标特定证据;只有显式的词汇或不相关的硬排除才是最终的。
证据基础与方法
本系统评价的目的是分析 流行病学 和登革热预防以及流行社区繁殖地的控制。
共识与结论
结果显示,DENV-1、DENV-2、DENV-3和DENV-4血清型广泛传播,其中以无预警信号的登革热为主,患病率的比例大于90%。
争议与缺口
与媒介扩散相关的主要因素包括气候条件、无计划的城市化、水容器的堆积、储水不足、卫生设施缺陷以及健康教育水平低。
科研与实践启示
在预防策略方面,综合干预措施被证明对减少病例最为有效,特别是有针对性的室内滞留喷洒、流行病学监测和社区参与,并辅以通过控制繁殖地进行的社区环境管理。

Epidemiología y prevención del dengue mediante el control de criaderos del vector en comunidades endémicas en América Latina.

Authors: Eva Patricia Montalvo-Flores, Steffi Gabriela Haro-Quijije, Renny Elian Pujupat-Choez, Jairo Geovanny Cañarte Químis, Jairo Geovanny Cañarte-Quimis
Original abstract (English)
Dengue is a viral disease transmitted by Aedes aegypti, constituting a major public health problem in Latin America due to its high epidemiological burden accompanied by factors that favor its proliferation. The objective of this systematic review was to analyze the epidemiology and prevention of dengue and the control of breeding sites in endemic communities. The results showed a wide circulation of the serotypes DENV-1, DENV-2, DENV-3 and DENV-4, with a predominance of dengue without warning signs with prevalence’s greater than 90%. Among the main factors associated with the proliferation of the vector were climatic conditions, unplanned urbanization, accumulation of water containers, inadequate water storage, deficiencies in sanitation, and low levels of health education. Regarding preventive strategies, integrated interventions proved most effective in reducing cases, particularly targeted indoor residual spraying, epidemiological surveillance, and community participation, complemented by environmental management in communities through the control of breeding sites. It is concluded that dengue prevention requires sustainable vector control programs that integrate surveillance, biotechnological innovation, and community participation to reduce disease transmission in endemic areas.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Dengue viruses 1–4 and dengue disease; other flaviviruses are near-neighbour exclusions. Biologically related animal, taxonomic, model, differential-diagnosis and comparative entities are supplementary-only unless material target-specific evidence is also present; only explicit lexical or unrelated hard exclusions are terminal.
Evidence base and method
The objective of this systematic review was to analyze the epidemiology and prevention of dengue and the control of breeding sites in endemic communities.
Consensus and conclusions
The results showed a wide circulation of the serotypes DENV-1, DENV-2, DENV-3 and DENV-4, with a predominance of dengue without warning signs with prevalence’s greater than 90%.
Controversies and gaps
Among the main factors associated with the proliferation of the vector were climatic conditions, unplanned urbanization, accumulation of water containers, inadequate water storage, deficiencies in sanitation, and low levels of health education.
Research and practice implications
Regarding preventive strategies, integrated interventions proved most effective in reducing cases, particularly targeted indoor residual spraying, epidemiological surveillance, and community participation, complemented by environmental management in communities through the control of breeding sites.

宿主 遗传学、种群多样性和登革热感染:一个未解决的范式

Host Genetics, Population Diversity, and Dengue Infection: An Unsolved Paradigm
作者： Juan Bautista De Sanctis, Alexis Hipólito García
摘要中文翻译这篇叙述性综述考察了有关人类遗传变异和登革热临床谱的最新文献。截至 2026 年 9 月的全面检索综合了遗传关联研究、HLA 分型和 基因组 范围研究的结果。证据根据世界卫生组织临床分类进行系统分类。大多数可用数据来自在东南亚进行的调查,非洲和拉丁美洲的贡献有限。值得注意的是,特定的 HLA 等位基因(尤其是 HLA-A*24)以及 MICB 和 PLCE1 基因变体证明与 重症登革热 的关联性最强;然而,这些关联因人群和病毒血清型而异。其他 免疫应答 基因,包括 CD209、FCGR2A 和 TNF,也受到影响,尽管它们的作用在不同的 队列研究 和流行病学环境中表现出不一致。相反,某些变异(例如 FCGR2A R/R131)的影响似乎受到人群背景、感染血清型和感染史的调节。现有研究的一个主要限制是样本量小、不同指南中病例定义的异质性以及对遗传多样性的调整不足。迄今为止,还没有任何遗传标记被验证可在全球范围内常规临床使用;尽管如此,大规模的遗传分析已经产生了新颖的见解,值得在整合 宿主 和病毒基因组数据的大型多种族研究中进行进一步调查。遗传分析对于提高 疫苗 疗效以及确定预防登革热感染和降低严重疾病风险的治疗靶点至关重要。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
这篇叙述性综述考察了有关人类遗传变异和登革热临床谱的最新文献。
证据基础与方法
截至 2026 年 9 月的全面检索综合了遗传关联研究、HLA 分型和 基因组 范围研究的结果。证据根据世界卫生组织临床分类进行系统分类。
共识与结论
特定的 HLA 等位基因(尤其是 HLA-A*24)以及 MICB 和 PLCE1 基因变异表明与 重症登革热 的关联性最强;然而,这些关联因人群和病毒血清型而异。其他 免疫应答 基因,包括 CD209、FCGR2A 和 TNF,也受到影响,尽管它们的作用在不同的 队列研究 和流行病学环境中表现出不一致。某些变异(例如 FCGR2A R/R131)的影响似乎受到人群背景、感染血清型和感染史的调节。
争议与缺口
现有研究的一个主要限制是样本量小、不同指南中病例定义的异质性以及对遗传多样性的调整不足。
科研与实践启示
遗传分析对于提高 疫苗 疗效以及确定预防登革热感染和降低严重疾病风险的治疗靶点至关重要。

Host Genetics, Population Diversity, and Dengue Infection: An Unsolved Paradigm

Authors: Juan Bautista De Sanctis, Alexis Hipólito García
Original abstract (English)
This narrative review examines recent literature on human genetic variation and the clinical spectrum of dengue. A comprehensive search through September 2026 synthesized findings from genetic association studies, HLA typing, and genome-wide studies. The evidence is systematically categorized according to the World Health Organization clinical classification. The majority of available data derive from investigations conducted in Southeast Asia, with limited contributions from Africa and Latin America. Notably, specific HLA alleles—particularly HLA-A*24—as well as MICB and PLCE1 gene variants demonstrate the strongest associations with severe dengue; however, these associations vary by population and viral serotype. Other immune response genes, including CD209, FCGR2A, and TNF, have also been implicated, although their effects exhibit inconsistency across diverse cohorts and epidemiological settings. Conversely, the impact of certain variants, such as FCGR2A R/R131, appears to be modulated by population context, infecting serotype, and infection history. A major limitation of the existing body of research is the prevalence of small sample sizes, heterogeneity in case definitions across guidelines, and insufficient adjustment for genetic diversity. To date, no genetic marker has been validated for routine clinical use on a global scale; nevertheless, large-scale genetic analyses have yielded novel insights that warrant further investigation in large, multi-ethnic studies integrating both host and viral genomic data. Genetic analyses are crucial for improving vaccine efficacy and for identifying therapeutic targets to prevent dengue infection and reduce the risk of severe disease.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This narrative review examines recent literature on human genetic variation and the clinical spectrum of dengue.
Evidence base and method
A comprehensive search through September 2026 synthesized findings from genetic association studies, HLA typing, and genome-wide studies. The evidence is systematically categorized according to the World Health Organization clinical classification.
Consensus and conclusions
Specific HLA alleles—particularly HLA-A*24—as well as MICB and PLCE1 gene variants demonstrate the strongest associations with severe dengue; however, these associations vary by population and viral serotype. Other immune response genes, including CD209, FCGR2A, and TNF, have also been implicated, although their effects exhibit inconsistency across diverse cohorts and epidemiological settings. The impact of certain variants, such as FCGR2A R/R131, appears to be modulated by population context, infecting serotype, and infection history.
Controversies and gaps
A major limitation of the existing body of research is the prevalence of small sample sizes, heterogeneity in case definitions across guidelines, and insufficient adjustment for genetic diversity.
Research and practice implications
Genetic analyses are crucial for improving vaccine efficacy and for identifying therapeutic targets to prevent dengue infection and reduce the risk of severe disease.

预防登革热的创新策略和 媒介 农村人口传播的其他措施。预防农村人口登革热和其他 媒介 传播疾病的创新策略。

Estrategias innovadoras para la prevención del dengue y otras enfermedades transmitidas por vectores en poblaciones rurales. Innovative strategies for the prevention of dengue and other vector-borne diseases in rural populations.
作者： Anita María Murillo-Zavala, Joseline Yomaira Burgos-Cuaces, Emily Xiomara Rodríguez, Alan Mishael Mendoza-Guanoluisa, Joseline Yomaira Burgos Cuaces
摘要中文翻译媒介 传播的疾病主要被归类为公共卫生问题,因为由于环境、气候和社会因素使个体易患这种疾病,它们主要影响热带和亚热带地区。因此,目标是描述在农村人口中预防登革热和其他 媒介 传播疾病的创新策略。本综述涉及采用描述性方法和文献设计的文献检索。结果表明,研究中得到的患病率范围为2.5%~63.3%,表现出较高的异质性。与高 患病率 相关的因素包括:储水不足、卫生设施不足、垃圾或固体废物、气候变化和社区参与度低。最常应用的创新策略是:使用智能移动应用程序、数字流行病学监测、使用地理信息系统、互动社区教育、使用生物媒介控制方法以及释放感染沃尔巴克氏体的蚊子。总之,媒介 传播的疾病仍然是一个潜在的公共卫生问题。上述策略通过实施新技术、生物防治和社区教育相结合的工具来改善预防工作,从而在高风险地区实现更好的控制。

Estrategias innovadoras para la prevención del dengue y otras enfermedades transmitidas por vectores en poblaciones rurales. Innovative strategies for the prevention of dengue and other vector-borne diseases in rural populations.

Authors: Anita María Murillo-Zavala, Joseline Yomaira Burgos-Cuaces, Emily Xiomara Rodríguez, Alan Mishael Mendoza-Guanoluisa, Joseline Yomaira Burgos Cuaces
Original abstract (English)
Vector-borne diseases are primarily classified as public health problems, as they mainly affect tropical and subtropical regions due to environmental, climatic, and social factors that predispose individuals to this condition. Therefore, the objective was to describe innovative strategies for the prevention of dengue and other vector-borne diseases in rural populations. This review involved a literature search with a descriptive approach and a documentary design. The results show that the prevalence obtained in the studies ranged from 2.5% to 63.3%, exhibiting high heterogeneity. Factors associated with high prevalence were: inadequate water storage, inadequate sanitation, garbage or solid waste, climate change, and low community participation. The most frequently applied innovative strategies were: the use of smart mobile applications, digital epidemiological surveillance, the use of geographic information systems, interactive community education, the use of biological vector control methods, and the release of mosquitoes infected with Wolbachia. In conclusion, vector-borne diseases remain a latent public health problem. The aforementioned strategies improve prevention efforts by implementing tools that combine new technologies, biological control, and community education, thus achieving better control in high-risk areas.

Evidence base and method

Consensus and conclusions

Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not clearly state controversies or evidence gaps.
Research and practice implications
The aforementioned strategies improve prevention efforts by implementing tools that combine new technologies, biological control, and community education, thus achieving better control in high-risk areas.

有关登革热的相关行动和实践以及农村人口中的矢量图。关于农村人口登革热和 媒介 传播疾病的知识、态度和实践。

Conocimiento, actitudes y prácticas sobre dengue y enfermedades vectoriales en poblaciones rurales. Knowledge, Attitudes, and Practices Regarding Dengue and Vector-Borne Diseases in Rural Populations.
作者： William Antonio Lino-Villacreses, Liliana Isabel Alvarado-Lino, Adriana Lorenis Quintero-Bedoya
摘要中文翻译登革热和其他 媒介 传播的疾病至今仍然是一个公共卫生问题。在热带和亚热带地区的农村地区,环境、社会和学校教育因素共同为蚊子 媒介(埃及伊蚊)提供了最佳繁殖场所。目的是回顾农村社区对登革热和 媒介 传播疾病的知识、态度和做法。采用描述性方法和文献设计进行了综合书目审查。搜索是在 Google Scholar、PubMed、SciELO、Elsevier 和 Springer 数据库上进行的,考虑了 20 月 22 日至 20 月 26 日之间发表的研究,有西班牙语、英语和葡萄牙语版本。分析这一人群的研究显示了知识水平之间的差异。在新加坡和秘鲁进行的研究中观察到了良好的水平,而在尼泊尔、巴基斯坦和也门进行的研究则观察到了不满意或中等的水平。此外,贫困、未接受正规教育、储水条件差、无计划的城市化和难以获得基本服务等决定因素也得到了证实。在预防方法方面,观察到的主要干预措施是健康教育、消除繁殖场所、固体废物管理、疾病监测和集体社区工作。为了控制登革热,应鼓励对社区培训、改善环境以及基于农村地区特点的媒介控制行动进行投资。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注农村人口中有关登革热和 媒介 传播疾病的知识、态度和实践。核心问题是评估这些社区对登革热和其他 媒介 传播疾病的理解和行为,特别强调 登革病毒 及其相关感染。
证据基础与方法
采用描述性方法和文献设计进行了综合书目审查。该搜索是在 Google Scholar、PubMed、SciELO、Elsevier 和 Springer 数据库上进行的,考虑了 2022 年至 2026 年间发表的西班牙语、英语和葡萄牙语版本的研究。
共识与结论
分析农村人口的研究显示了知识水平的差异。新加坡和秘鲁的水平良好,而尼泊尔、巴基斯坦和也门的水平不令人满意或中等。贫穷、缺乏正规教育、储水不良、无计划的城市化以及获得基本服务的机会有限等决定性因素被认为影响知识和实践。
争议与缺口
该评论强调了不同地区知识水平的异质性。缺乏关于农村地区具体干预措施有效性的全面数据,并且该研究没有提供有关这些差异背后机制的详细信息。
科研与实践启示
为了控制登革热,应鼓励对社区培训、改善环境以及针对农村地区特点的媒介控制行动进行投资。健康教育、消灭繁殖地、固体废物管理、疾病监测和集体社区工作被强调为关键干预措施。

Conocimiento, actitudes y prácticas sobre dengue y enfermedades vectoriales en poblaciones rurales. Knowledge, Attitudes, and Practices Regarding Dengue and Vector-Borne Diseases in Rural Populations.

Authors: William Antonio Lino-Villacreses, Liliana Isabel Alvarado-Lino, Adriana Lorenis Quintero-Bedoya
Original abstract (English)
Dengue and other vector-borne diseases continue to be a public health problem today. In rural regions of tropical and subtropical areas, environmental, social, and schooling factors converge to provide optimal breeding sites for the mosquito vector, Aedes aegypti. The aim was to review knowledge, attitudes and practices towards dengue and vector-borne diseases in rural communities. An integrative bibliographic review, with descriptive approach and documentary design, was conducted. The search was performed on Google Scholar, PubMed, SciELO, Elsevier and Springer databases, considering studies published between 20 22 and 20 26, available in Spanish, English and Portuguese languages. Studies analyzing this population showed disparities between levels of knowledge. Favorable levels were observed in studies performed in Singapore and Peru and unsatisfactory or moderate levels in Nepal, Pakistan and Yemen. Additionally, determinant factors as poverty, not receiving formal education, poor water storage conditions, unplanned urbanization and scarce access to essential services were evidenced. For prevention methods, health education, elimination of breeding sites, solid waste management, disease surveillance and collective community work were the main interventions observed. To control Dengue, investment in community training, better environments and vector control actions based on rural area characteristics should be encouraged.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on knowledge, attitudes, and practices regarding dengue and vector-borne diseases in rural populations. The central question is to assess the understanding and behaviors related to dengue and other vector-borne diseases in these communities, with a specific emphasis on the dengue virus and its associated infections.
Evidence base and method
An integrative bibliographic review with a descriptive approach and documentary design was conducted. The search was performed on Google Scholar, PubMed, SciELO, Elsevier, and Springer databases, considering studies published between 2022 and 2026, available in Spanish, English, and Portuguese languages.
Consensus and conclusions
Studies analyzing rural populations showed disparities in knowledge levels. Favorable levels were observed in Singapore and Peru, while unsatisfactory or moderate levels were found in Nepal, Pakistan, and Yemen. Determinant factors such as poverty, lack of formal education, poor water storage, unplanned urbanization, and limited access to essential services were identified as influencing knowledge and practices.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights heterogeneity in knowledge levels across different regions. There is a lack of comprehensive data on the effectiveness of specific interventions in rural settings, and the study does not provide detailed information on the mechanisms underlying these disparities.
Research and practice implications
To control dengue, investment in community training, improved environments, and vector control actions tailored to rural area characteristics should be encouraged. Health education, elimination of breeding sites, solid waste management, disease surveillance, and collective community work are emphasized as key interventions.

Impacto epidemiológico del dengue y del chikungunya en zonas Urbanas: revisión sistemática 城市地区登革热和基孔肯雅热的流行病学影响:系统评价

Impacto epidemiológico del dengue y del chikungunya en zonas urbanas: revisión sistemática Epidemiological impact of dengue and chikungunya in urban areas: a systematic review
作者： Nayeli Mishell Zamora-Villaprado, Thais Mité Silva-Saavedra, Mariam Mustafá Ibrahim-Santiago, Bolívar Fernando Mena-Hidalgo
摘要中文翻译目的:证明城市流行病学对登革热和基孔肯雅热的影响。方法:PubMed 和 SciELO 中的 síntesis narrativa 的修订(2012-2025);包括 15 个工作室。结果:1990 年世界登革热事件,拉丁美洲地区市场和货运方面存在差异。基孔肯雅热在 2013 年美洲地区得到扩展,并产生了城市爆炸性的产品,并具有持久的关节表现。城市化、埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊的扩张以及对传播有利于杀虫剂的抵抗力。Destacan como estrategias las nuevas vacunas y el control vectorial integrado。结论:虫媒病毒在热带和亚热带城市中发挥着重要作用,并在预防和控制方面发挥着重要作用。

强调了新疫苗和综合媒介控制策略的重要性。

Impacto epidemiológico del dengue y del chikungunya en zonas urbanas: revisión sistemática Epidemiological impact of dengue and chikungunya in urban areas: a systematic review

Authors: Nayeli Mishell Zamora-Villaprado, Thais Mité Silva-Saavedra, Mariam Mustafá Ibrahim-Santiago, Bolívar Fernando Mena-Hidalgo
Original abstract (English)
Objetivo: sintetizar la evidencia sobre el impacto epidemiológico urbano del dengue y del chikungunya. Método: revisión sistemática con síntesis narrativa en PubMed y SciELO (2012-2025); se incluyeron 15 estudios. Resultados: la incidencia mundial del dengue aumentó desde 1990, con diferencias regionales marcadas y alta carga en América Latina. El chikungunya se expandió por las Américas desde 2013 y produjo brotes urbanos explosivos, con manifestaciones articulares persistentes. La urbanización, la expansión de Aedes aegypti y Aedes albopictus y la resistencia a insecticidas favorecieron la transmisión. Destacan como estrategias las nuevas vacunas y el control vectorial integrado. Conclusión: ambas arbovirosis son una carga creciente para las ciudades tropicales y subtropicales y exigen vigilancia y control sostenidos.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Objetivo: sintetizar la evidencia sobre el impacto epidemiológico urbano del dengue y del chikungunya.
Evidence base and method
Método: revisión sistemática con síntesis narrativa en PubMed y SciELO (2012-2025); se incluyeron 15 estudios.
Consensus and conclusions
Resultados: la incidencia mundial del dengue aumentó desde 1990, con diferencias regionales marcadas y alta carga en América Latina. El chikungunya se expandió por las Américas desde 2013 y produjo brotes urbanos explosivos, con manifestaciones articulares persistentes.
Controversies and gaps
La urbanización, la expansión de Aedes aegypti y Aedes albopictus y la resistencia a insecticidas favorecieron la transmisión.
Research and practice implications
Destacan como estrategias las nuevas vacunas y el control vectorial integrado.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

Estrategias de prevención del dengue en comunidades Urbanas:revisión sistemática 城市社区登革热预防策略:系统回顾

Estrategias de prevención del dengue en comunidades urbanas: revisión sistemática Dengue prevention strategies in urban communities: a systematic review
作者： Angie Nicole Acosta-Obando, Gabriela Monica Echeverría-Chavez, Carlos Alejandro Troya-Altamirano
摘要中文翻译目的:证明在城市社区和厄瓜多尔环境中预防登革热的有效策略。方法:PubMed 和 SciELO 中的 síntesis narrativa 的修订(2012-2025);其中包括 15 个研究室:对系统的修订和元分析、厄瓜多尔的研究室、社区干预和经济评估。结果:控制力降低了昆虫学指标,从而影响了传播。El manejo 环境与社区参与、生活和受惠者的立方体最有利的结果。厄瓜多尔的水、水的感知和基本预防服务的故障。结论:城市登革热的预防需要综合、社区和支持、流行病学和基本服务的良好策略。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该审查旨在综合城市社区登革热预防策略有效性的证据,并评估其在厄瓜多尔国情中的适用性。
证据基础与方法
使用 PubMed 和 SciELO 数据库从 2012 年至 2025 年进行了系统评价。证据包括 15 项研究:有或没有荟萃分析的系统评价、在厄瓜多尔进行的研究、社区干预措施和经济评估。
共识与结论
审查发现,综合、基于社区和持续的策略,包括环境管理、纱窗和有盖水容器,在预防登革热方面显示出最有利的结果。然而,单独的化学控制降低了昆虫学指数,但没有显示出对 传播 的影响。
争议与缺口
审查强调,储水做法、风险认知和缺乏基本服务等因素影响了厄瓜多尔的预防有效性。没有明确提及结果中相互矛盾的发现或异质性。
科研与实践启示
城市地区的登革热预防需要综合、基于社区和持续的战略,以及流行病学 监测 和改善基本服务。

Estrategias de prevención del dengue en comunidades urbanas: revisión sistemática Dengue prevention strategies in urban communities: a systematic review

Authors: Angie Nicole Acosta-Obando, Gabriela Monica Echeverría-Chavez, Carlos Alejandro Troya-Altamirano
Original abstract (English)
Objetivo: sintetizar la evidencia sobre la efectividad de las estrategias de prevención del dengue en comunidades urbanas y su aplicabilidad al contexto ecuatoriano. Método: revisión sistemática con síntesis narrativa en PubMed y SciELO (2012-2025); se incluyeron 15 estudios: revisiones sistemáticas con y sin metaanálisis, estudios en Ecuador, intervenciones comunitarias y una evaluación económica. Resultados: el control químico aislado redujo los índices entomológicos sin efecto demostrado sobre la transmisión. El manejo ambiental con participación comunitaria, las mallas en las viviendas y las cubiertas de recipientes mostraron los resultados más favorables. El almacenamiento de agua, la percepción del riesgo y la falta de servicios básicos condicionaron la prevención en Ecuador. Conclusión: la prevención del dengue urbano requiere estrategias integradas, comunitarias y sostenidas, con vigilancia epidemiológica y mejoras de los servicios básicos.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review aims to synthesize evidence on the effectiveness of dengue prevention strategies in urban communities and assess their applicability to the Ecuadorian context.
Evidence base and method
A systematic review was conducted using PubMed and SciELO databases from 2012 to 2025. The evidence includes 15 studies: systematic reviews with and without meta-analyses, studies conducted in Ecuador, community interventions, and an economic evaluation.
Consensus and conclusions
The review found that integrated, community-based, and sustained strategies, including environmental management, window screens, and covered water containers, showed the most favorable results in dengue prevention. However, chemical control alone reduced entomological indices without demonstrated impact on transmission.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights that factors such as water storage practices, risk perception, and lack of basic services influenced prevention effectiveness in Ecuador. There is no explicit mention of conflicting findings or heterogeneity in the results.
Research and practice implications
The review concludes that dengue prevention in urban areas requires integrated, community-based, and sustained strategies, along with epidemiological surveillance and improvements in basic services.

Published: 2026-11-02  |  Publisher: World Health Organization (WHO)
A 高优先级公共卫生新闻

世界卫生组织(WHO)健康紧急事件EPI-WIN网络研讨会:发布登革热、基孔肯雅热、寨卡病毒和黄热病临床管理指南 - 世界卫生组织(WHO)

WHO Health Emergencies EPI-WIN webinar: Launch of WHO guidelines for clinical management of arboviral diseases: dengue, chikungunya, Zika and yellow fever - World Health Organization (WHO)
新闻简报世界卫生组织 (WHO) 在 2026 年 11 月 2 日的网络研讨会上发布了登革热、基孔肯雅热、寨卡病毒和黄热病等虫媒病毒性疾病的临床管理指南。这些指南是世卫组织应对突发卫生事件和改善疾病应对的更广泛努力的一部分。该活动还伴随着其他活动,例如在世卫组织、联合国儿童基金会和其他合作伙伴的支持下于 2026 年 10 月至 11 月在苏丹开展的脊髓灰质炎 疫苗接种 运动,涉及约 1100 万儿童。此外,世卫组织还强调了与突发卫生事件有关的持续举措,包括推出全球大型地图以及有关疾病 暴发 和健康相关挑战的其他报告。该指南旨在加强虫媒病毒性疾病的临床管理和防备,虫媒病毒性疾病在全球范围内构成了重大的公共卫生风险。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该网络研讨会于2026年11月2日启动,相关活动于2026年11月4日至2026年11月13日举行。
地点与对象
The event was hosted by the World Health Organization (WHO) globally, with a specific mention of Sudan's polio vaccination campaign involving around 11 million children.
事件
世卫组织通过网络研讨会推出了登革热、基孔肯雅热、寨卡病毒和黄热病等虫媒病毒疾病的临床管理指南。这是与突发卫生事件和疾病 暴发 相关的一系列更广泛活动的一部分。
规模、影响与风险
该指南旨在改善虫媒病毒疾病的临床管理和应对措施,虫媒病毒疾病是重大的公共卫生威胁。世界卫生组织还强调了在脊髓灰质炎 疫苗接种 运动和其他卫生举措方面正在进行的努力。
应对、状态与不确定性
该指南的发布是世卫组织应对突发卫生事件持续努力的一部分。据报道,苏丹的脊髓灰质炎 疫苗接种 运动是一项全国性举措,得到了多个合作伙伴的支持。
A high priorityPublic-health news

WHO Health Emergencies EPI-WIN webinar: Launch of WHO guidelines for clinical management of arboviral diseases: dengue, chikungunya, Zika and yellow fever - World Health Organization (WHO)

News Brief
The World Health Organization (WHO) launched guidelines for the clinical management of arboviral diseases, including dengue, chikungunya, Zika, and yellow fever, through a webinar on 2 November 2026. These guidelines are part of WHO's broader efforts to address health emergencies and improve disease response. The event was accompanied by other activities, such as a polio vaccination campaign in Sudan involving around 11 million children, which was conducted from October to November 2026 with support from WHO, UNICEF, and other partners. Additionally, WHO highlighted ongoing initiatives related to health emergencies, including the launch of a global mega-map and other reports on disease outbreaks and health-related challenges. The guidelines aim to enhance clinical management and preparedness for arboviral diseases, which pose significant public health risks worldwide.
Original source text (English)

Epidemic and emerging disease alerts in the Pacific as of 03 November 2026 - ReliefWeb

Epidemic and emerging disease alerts in the Pacific as of 03 November 2026 - ReliefWeb
新闻简报截至 2026 年 11 月 3 日,美属萨摩亚登革热病例仍然较低,为 1,064 例,马绍尔群岛有 165 例阳性病例。媒介 控制和 监测 工作正在进行中。麻疹和流感活动正在增加,澳大利亚报告了 122 例,法属波利尼西亚报告了 52 例甲型流感病例。登革热、麻疹和流感的警报仍然有效。

时间
跳过主要内容 帮助 登录 ReliefWeb 内容搜索 搜索内容 你在找什么?
地点与对象
美属萨摩亚、马绍尔群岛、萨摩亚、密克罗尼西亚联邦(雅浦州)、澳大利亚、巴布亚新几内亚、法属波利尼西亚、新西兰。
事件
登革热病例仍然很低,美属萨摩亚、马绍尔群岛和萨摩亚报告了新病例数。媒介 控制和 监测 工作正在进行中。麻疹和流感警报在多个地点均已启动。
规模、影响与风险
登革热病例数较低,美属萨摩亚有 1,064 例,马绍尔群岛有 165 例阳性病例。麻疹和流感活动正在增加,澳大利亚报告了 122 例,法属波利尼西亚报告了 52 例甲型流感病例。
应对、状态与不确定性
针对登革热、麻疹和流感的蓝色和红色警报仍然有效。媒介 控制、监测 和 疫苗接种 活动正在实施。
A high priorityPublic-health news

Epidemic and emerging disease alerts in the Pacific as of 03 November 2026 - ReliefWeb

News Brief
As of November 3, 2026, dengue cases remain low in American Samoa with 1,064 cases, and 165 positive cases in the Marshall Islands. Vector control and surveillance efforts are ongoing. Measles and influenza activity is increasing, with 122 notifications in Australia and 52 influenza A cases in French Polynesia. Alerts for dengue, measles, and influenza remain active.
Original source text (English)
Skip to main content Help Log in ReliefWeb Search Content Search What are you looking for? Search|t Updates Countries Disasters Organizations Topics Jobs Training Informing humanitarians worldwide 24/7 — a service provided by UN OCHA World + 15 more Epidemic and emerging disease alerts in the Pacific as of 03 November 2026 Format Map Source SPC Posted 3 Nov 2026 Originally published 3 Nov 2026 Attachments Download Map (PDF | 569.56 KB) Highlights/updates since the last map was sent on PacNet on 27 October 2026: Dengue • American Samoa: The Department of Health Situational Report for 19 to 25 October 2026 records 1,064 laboratory-confirmed dengue cases as of 27 October 2026 since the outbreak began in 2025. New case numbers remain low, with four cases reported over the last three EpiWeeks: two cases in EpiWeek 40 and one case each in EpiWeeks 41 and 42. Dengue cases peaked in early 2026 (EpiWeek 02), with weekly case counts remaining low since May. The blue alert for dengue (DENV-1 & DENV-2) remains in effect. – Source: American Samoa Department of Health, Situational Report: Respiratory & Notifiable Diseases shared with the PPHSN Coordinating Body Focal point on 30 October 2026. • Marshall Islands: The 16th Outbreak Situation Report covering 15 August to 22 October 2026, records 165 positive cases (confirmed and probable) among 540 specimens tested: 127 in Majuro and 38 in Ebeye, compared with 124 and 32 cases, respectively, in the previous report covering 15 August to 15 October 2026. There have been 15 hospitalisations (13 in Majuro and two in Ebeye) and no deaths since the beginning of the outbreak. Attack rates are 5.5 per 1,000 population in Majuro and 3.9 per 1,000 in Ebeye, while positivity rates are 29% and 37%, respectively. Only DENV-1 has been identified. Vector control has been completed at the households of 147 positive cases and at 206 sites. About 250 spatial repellents and mosquito nets have been delivered to Ebeye, and surveillance in the neighbouring islands is being strengthened. The red alert for dengue (DENV-1) remains in effect. – Source: Republic of the Marshall Islands Ministry of Health and Human Services, 16th Outbreak Situation Report on dengue fever in Majuro and Ebeye accessed on 03 November 2026. • Samoa: Dengue Fever Outbreak Situation Report Issue No. 79, covering EpiWeek 42 (12 to 18 October 2026), reports 51 new cases, down 4% on the previous week (53, revised from 14), with no laboratory-confirmed case, no new admission and no death. Since 01 January 2025, 20,478 clinically diagnosed cases, 5,547 laboratory-confirmed cases and nine dengue-related deaths have been recorded. DENV-1 accounts for 52% and DENV-2 for 48% of serotyped cases, and children under 15 years for 73% of cases. The blue alert for dengue (DENV-1 & DENV-2) remains in effect. – Source: Ministry of Health Samoa, Dengue Fever Outbreak Situation Report Issue No. 79 accessed on 03 November 2026. Diarrhea • Federated States of Micronesia – Yap State: The Yap weekly bulletin for EpiWeek 43 (19 to 25 October 2026) shows diarrhoea on alert for a tenth consecutive week, rising from 9 to 15 cases, above the alert threshold of 7. All 5 stool specimens tested on the BioFire gastrointestinal panel were positive: three patients had adenovirus, two of them co-infected with E. coli pathotypes, and two had E. coli pathotype co-infections only. Among 100 diarrhoea cases in children aged 0 to 5 years since the start of the outbreak, 34 had completed rotavirus vaccination, 27 were partially vaccinated, 28 were unvaccinated and 11 had their status still being verified. The blue alert for rotavirus is changed to blue alert for diarrhea for the Federated States of Micronesia (Yap State). – Source: Yap State Department of Health Services, Communicable Disease Weekly Bulletin, shared with the PPHSN Coordinating Body Focal point on 29 October 2026. Measles • Australia: On 30 October 2026, the Western Australia Department of Health confirmed a new measles case in a young child treated at a Perth hospital. The case is not linked to overseas travel and has no known link to the case reported on 28 October, and the Department says that a locally acquired case with no known source raises concern for undetected community transmission. The earlier case is a returned overseas traveller who visited several public venues in Perth while infectious. People who attended the listed exposure locations are advised to monitor for symptoms and to call ahead before visiting a GP clinic or emergency department. As of 03 November 2026 at the national level, 122 measles notifications have been recorded in Australia, compared with 181 in 2025. The red alert for measles remains in effect for Australia. – Sources: Western Australia Department of Health, Measles alert for Perth in returned overseas traveller, Western Australia Department of Health, WA Health confirms new measles case in young child and Dashboard · NINDSS Portal accessed on 03 November 2026. • Papua New Guinea: The National Department of Health weekly bulletin for EpiWeek 43 (19 to 25 October 2026) reports 26 acute fever and rash cases nationally, down from 30 in EpiWeek 42. Year to date, 452 cases have been reported and 396 samples tested, with 19 IgM-positive measles results. After Expert Review Group review, two measles cases (New Ireland 1, Western Highlands 1) and two rubella cases are confirmed in 2026. The last IgM-positive measles onset was on 15 September 2026 and the last rubella onset on 29 September 2026. The measles-rubella vaccination campaign targets all children under seven in districts with a detected case. The red alert for measles remains in effect for Papua New Guinea. – Source: Papua New Guinea National Department of Health, Public Health Events Weekly Bulletin, shared with the PPHSN Coordinating Body Focal point on 30 October 2026. Respiratory diseases • French Polynesia: In French Polynesia, influenza activity increased markedly in EpiWeek 43 (19–25 October 2026), confirming the territory’s entry into an epidemic phase. A total of 52 laboratory-confirmed influenza A cases, predominantly A(H1N1), were reported during the week, with 14 hospitalisations. Increased cases and hospitalisations were observed in Tahiti, Raiatea and the Marquesas, indicating circulation across several parts of the territory. The seasonal influenza vaccination campaign began on 26 October, with vaccination available through pharmacies and health facilities. A red alert for influenza A is issued for French Polynesia. – Source: Agence de régulation de l'action sanitaire et sociale (ARASS), Bulletin de Surveillance Sanitaire de Polynésie française n°38 shared with the PPHSN Coordinating Body Focal point on 03 November 2026. • New Zealand: For the week ending 25 October 2026, the all-cause SARI hospitalisation rate in the Auckland region remained stable and in the low activity band. The influenza-positive SARI hospitalisation rate increased slightly but also remained low. Influenza remained the most commonly detected virus among SARI patients, with 17 positive samples, and SARI influenza test positivity increased from 19.0% to 28.3%. Positivity among sentinel GP influenza-like illness cases also increased, from 9.5% to 13.0%. In 2026 to date, 707 of 2,924 (24.2%) SARI samples and 229 of 1,220 (18.8%) sentinel GP samples have tested positive for influenza. The blue alert for influenza A remains in effect for New Zealand. – Source: New Zealand acute respiratory illness dashboard accessed on 03 November 2026. • Tonga: The weekly syndromic surveillance report for EpiWeek 43 (19 to 25 October 2026) shows influenza-like illness (ILI) falling for a second week, from 125 to 84 cases, after the EpiWeek 41 peak of 137. Severe acute respiratory infection (SARI) fell from 13 to 9 cases. From 03 August to 25 October 2026, 89 SARI patients were hospitalised. Of 732 ILI cases, 499 (68.1%) were tested: 235 (47%) were positive for influenza A, 6 for influenza B and 29 for RSV. Four deaths have occurred among SARI cases, unchanged since EpiWeek 42, three of them laboratory-confirmed influenza A. The red alert for influenza A remains in effect for Tonga. – Source: Tonga Ministry of Health, Weekly Communicable Diseases Syndromic Surveillance shared with the PPHSN Coordinating Body Focal point on 30 October 2026. Rotavirus • Vanuatu: Situation Update No. 1 on watery diarrhoea on Ambae Island, Penama Province covering 12 to 18 October 2026, reports seven cases in the period, below the weekly alert threshold of 8. Two laboratory-confirmed rotavirus cases were detected in EpiWeek 43, both from Navonda village in East Ambae, confirming ongoing transmission. The Ministry of Health states that the current data do not support an outbreak declaration: from week 1 to week 42, weekly counts at Lolowai Hospital exceeded the threshold only once, in week 10. Most cases are aged over 5 years (59%) and 75% come from East Ambae. One case was admitted and discharged, with no severe case and no death. Enhanced surveillance, active case finding, risk communication on hygiene and safe water, and testing of suspected cases are under way. The red alert for rotavirus is changed to blue alert for rotavirus for Vanuatu. – Source: Vanuatu Ministry of Health, Watery Diarrhoea on Ambae Island, Penama Province, Situation Update No. 1, shared with the PPHSN Coordinating Body Focal point on 28 October 2026. Other Information: One Health - High pathogenicity avian influenza • Australia: A dead common dolphin that washed ashore at Encounter Bay on South Australia's Fleurieu Peninsula tested positive for avian influenza (H5), according to the state's chief veterinary officer, who assessed the risk of an extensive outbreak among dolphins as low while flagging ongoing concern for seals and sea lions. South Australia has recorded 341 positive H5 events and Australia 732 confirmed events in wildlife as of 02 November 2026. New detections are less frequent, with no recent mass mortality. Vaccine has been released for wild little penguin colonies at Granite Island, Penneshaw and Emu Bay, with no start date set. H5 has not been reported from any Pacific Island country or territory to date. – Source: BEACON, HPAI detected in a dolphin, South Australia, Australia as federal government rolls out wildlife vaccination and Latest data - DAFF accessed on 03 November 2026. • Hawai’i: On 29 October 2026, the Hawaiʻi State Department of Health reported that genomic sequencing identified H5N1 genetic material, genotype D1.1, in a wastewater sample collected on 14 October 2026 at a site in East Kauaʻi. It is the first detection of this genotype in Hawaiʻi, where genotype A3 was previously found. The detection does not mean that live, infectious virus is or has been present in a human. No human case has been linked to this signal and no H5N1 has been detected in local dairy milk testing. – Source: Hawaiʻi Department of Health, DOH reports H5N1 avian flu detected in Kauaʻi County wastewater sample accessed on 03 November 2026. • Japan: A dead wild goose recovered in Kurihara City, Miyagi Prefecture, on 27 October 2026 tested preliminarily positive for avian influenza virus. The Environment Ministry is awaiting genetic confirmation of whether the strain is of high pathogenicity, which would be the first such detection in a wild bird in Japan this season. A 10 km priority zone for wild bird monitoring has been designated around the site. No poultry, mammal or human case has been reported. – Source: BEACON, First preliminary positive detection of avian influenza in a wild goose this migratory season in Japan accessed on 03 November 2026. Many thanks again for your continuous contributions. Please note that the alerts displayed on the map are based on information available to us as of the date of this report. Location and population

Blue and red alerts for dengue, measles, and influenza remain in effect. Vector control, surveillance, and vaccination campaigns are being implemented.

India's first dengue vaccine due in 2027: Japanese pharma. - en.etemaaddaily.com

Original source text (English)
12:44 AM Thursday, 5th November UrduMatrimonialsClassifieds مزید دریافت کریں صحافتی آرٹیکلز اٹلس نقشے دیکھیں مقامی گائیڈ دیکھیں مزید دریافت کریں آن لائن خبریں اخبار کی فراہمی تازہ ترین خبریں الیکٹرانک پیپر نیوز الرٹس World Business Sports Entertainment Health Crime Edu and Jobs Politics Photos Videos Menu Home > Health India's first dengue vaccine due in 2027: Japanese pharma Wed 04 Nov 2026, 23:52:56 India’s first dengue vaccine, Japanese pharma major Takeda’s Qdenga, is expected to reach the private market in the first half of 2027, offering a new tool against a disease that infects millions worldwide and causes recurring outbreaks across the country. Clinicians, however, urge caution. Some of them cite unanswered questions about protection against different strains, how long immunity lasts and the risk of severe disease in later infections. The Central Drugs Standard Control Organisation (CDSCO) authorised Qdenga in July for people aged four to 60. Takeda will launch it in India with Dr Reddy’s Laboratories. Until now, no widely available vaccine existed for protection against dengue – the world’s fastest-growing mosquito-borne disease. A World Health ORGnisation surveillance system recorded 14.1 million cases worldwide in 2024, the highest ever reported, and cases appeared in more than 100 countries. India is part of that picture. Here, reported cases rose from 44,585 in 2020 to 1,93,245 in 2021, and 2024 saw 233,519 cases and 297 deaths. Cases fell to 1,13,440 and 94 deaths through November 2025, the lowest since 2021, but official counts understate the real burden. One Indian study estimated actual cases at 282 times the number reported, and dengue is now a year-round problem rather than a seasonal one. Against this background, the promise of a vaccine offers hope. But some clinicians insist that the evidence on the vaccine needs closer scrutiny before wide adoption. Leading paediatrician Dr Sanjeev Bagai shared his concerns, which include the virus’s evolution, differences in circulating strains across India and antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE), in which the immune response from an earlier infection can make a later one more severe. Dr Bagai’s first concern is durability. Trial results are encouraging: Qdenga protected against virologically confirmed dengue and reduced dengue-related hospitalisations, though protection varies by serotype and by whether a person had been exposed before. A longer-term analysis of the pivotal trial, published by the manufacturer, reported efficacy of 61.2% against confirmed dengue and 84.1% against hospitalisation 4.5 years after vaccination. That is meaningful protection, but it does not stop every infection. Dr Bagai wants closer scrutiny of how protection changes in later years, especially for individual serotypes. “The problem is that people look at the مزید دریافت کریں بریکنگ نیوز ای پیپر سبسکرپشن ای بکس تلاش کریں top-line vaccine efficacy figures without examining what happens to protection over three or four years,” he says. He also questions whether trial data adequately represent India’s diverse population and the geography of dengue. Waning protection, however, is not evidence of harm. A decline in efficacy does not by itself show that later infections become more severe. The World Health Organization (WHO) recommends Qdenga for children aged six to 16 in high-transmission settings but flags uncertainty about protection against DENV-3 and DENV-4 in people never previously infected. It advises against routine introduction in low-to-moderate transmission areas until more evidence emerges, and urges countries to account for regional differences in transmission rather than adopt a uniform strategy. Dr Bagai’s most serious concern is ADE. Antibodies from an earlier infection may fail to neutralise a later one and can help the virus enter cells, raising the risk of severe disease. Dengue is among the best-known examples: a second infection with a different serotype can trigger a stronger inflammatory response and complications. Because people’s exposure histories differ widely,Dr Bagai says vaccinations must be examined in this light. “If you are giving a vaccine, you need to understand how the body’s existing immune status is going to respond to it,” he says. He also suggests that prior infections such as COVID-19 and influenza could shape responses to dengue vaccination through similarities in immune-recognition pathways. But claims that shared protein sequences could make the body respond as if facing another dengue infection are not established clinical evidence of ADE after Qdenga. They would need specific validation. Dr Sumit Jain, director of internal medicine at Paras Health, Panchkula, agrees that ADE has historically worried dengue vaccination but says the risk cannot be assumed for every vaccine. “Dengvaxia’s experience shouldn’t be assumed to be representative of the risk profile of any new dengue vaccine,” he says. Sanofi’s Dengvaxia has a different formulation and is restricted to people with evidence of prior infection, because those never infected face a higher risk of severe dengue after vaccination and later exposure. Qdenga has been through separate trials and is approved in India without proof of previous infection. That does not settle every safety question. Its risk profile must be judged on its own clinical and post-marketing evidence. مزید دریافت کریں ٹریول گائیڈ پڑھیں آن لائن میگزین دیسی ملبوسات Share Tweet No Comments For This Post, Be first to write a Comment. 50 ڈگری تک پہنچ گیا حیدرآباد: حیڈرا کی تلہ پور میں کارروائی، 7 غیر قانونی بورویلز بند، 6 واٹر سمپس منہدم حیدرآباد آؤٹر رنگ روڈ پر کار حادثہ، ڈیوائیڈر سے ٹکرا کر الٹ گئی سکندرآباد ریلوے اسٹیشن پر 60 B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

印度登革热 疫苗 预计将于 2027 年首次接种 - Times Bull

Dengue Vaccine in India, First Shot Expected in 2027 - Times Bull
新闻简报Times Bull 于 2026 年 11 月 4 日发表的文章讨论了登革热 疫苗 Qdenga 将于 2027 年在印度上市。疫苗 于 2026 年 7 月获得中央药物标准控制组织 (CDSCO) 批准,旨在针对 登革病毒 的所有四种血清型(DENV-1 至 DENV-4)。它是使用弱化的 DENV-2 主链和其他血清型的表面蛋白开发的。虽然 疫苗 在 疫苗接种 后 4.5 年显示出对确诊登革热的疗效为 61.2%,对住院治疗的疗效为 84.1%,但它并不能预防所有感染。世界卫生组织 (WHO) 建议在高 传播 地区的 6 至 16 岁儿童中使用该药物,但强调对于未感染过 DENV-3 和 DENV-4 的儿童的预防作用存在不确定性。包括 Sanjeev Bagai 博士在内的临床医生对 疫苗 的有效性和潜在风险(例如 抗体依赖性增强 (ADE))表示担忧,并呼吁进一步评估。文章还指出,印度的登革热病例多年来显着增加,报告的病例到 2025 年 11 月有所下降,但由于该疾病的全年性质以及涉及多种血清型的混合感染的可能性,可能导致报告不足。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该文章发表于 2026 年 11 月 4 日。疫苗 预计将于 2027 年在印度推出。报告了 2020 年、2021 年和 2024 年印度登革热病例和死亡数据,其中提到到 2025 年 11 月病例数有所下降。
地点与对象
印度是多个地区报告登革热病例的地点。受影响的人群包括 4 至 60 岁的个人,重点是高 传播 地区的 6 至 16 岁儿童。
事件
中央药物标准控制组织 (CDSCO) 于 2026 年 7 月批准 Qdenga,一种登革热 疫苗,用于 4 至 60 岁的个体。疫苗 旨在针对 登革病毒 的所有四种血清型(DENV-1 至 DENV-4)。
规模、影响与风险
印度的登革热病例从 2020 年的 44,585 例增加到 2021 年的 193,245 例,2024 年记录了 233,519 例病例和 297 例死亡。到 2025 年 11 月,病例已降至 113,440 例,其中 94 例死亡。然而,实际病例可能比报道的多282倍。疫苗 在 疫苗接种 后 4.5 年对确诊登革热的疗效为 61.2%,对住院治疗的疗效为 84.1%,但它并不能预防所有感染。
应对、状态与不确定性
世界卫生组织 (WHO) 建议在高 传播 地区的 6 至 16 岁儿童使用 Qdenga,但指出对未感染过 DENV-3 和 DENV-4 的个体的预防作用存在不确定性。包括 Sanjeev Bagai 博士在内的一些临床医生对 疫苗 的有效性和潜在风险(例如 抗体依赖性增强 (ADE))表示担忧,并呼吁进一步评估。
